"""
Quality Management & HACCP Production Engine Routes
ISA-95 Level 3 Quality Assurance Subsystem
Synchronized with SQL Server MES_Milk_Production & Shared Kepware OPC UA Singleton
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import time
import logging
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, date, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel

backend_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
if backend_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, backend_dir)

try:
    from db_connector import get_connection, execute_query
except ImportError:
    from backend.db_connector import get_connection, execute_query

logger = logging.getLogger("QualityRoutes")
quality_router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/quality", tags=["Quality Management"])

# ============================================================================
# DATA TRANSFER MODELS
# ============================================================================

class TestNodePayload(BaseModel):
    node_id: Optional[str] = ""

class QualityTagItem(BaseModel):
    node_id: Optional[str] = ""
    description: Optional[str] = ""
    unit: Optional[str] = "--"
    spec_min: Optional[float] = None
    spec_max: Optional[float] = None
    role: Optional[str] = None
    dataType: Optional[str] = "Float"

class QualityTagMappingUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    tags: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None

# ============================================================================
# HELPER FUNCTIONS: OPC UA SINGLETON & PERSISTENCE ACCESS
# ============================================================================

def _get_kepware_gateway():
    """Retrieves central kepware_gateway singleton without spawning new sessions."""
    try:
        import kepware_gateway
        return kepware_gateway
    except ImportError:
        try:
            from backend import kepware_gateway
            return kepware_gateway
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Could not import kepware_gateway: {e}")
            return None

def _get_tag_mappings_filepath() -> str:
    return os.path.join(backend_dir, "tag_mappings.json")

def _load_tag_mappings() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    path = _get_tag_mappings_filepath()
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return {}
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error loading tag mappings: {e}")
        return {}

def _save_tag_mappings(mappings: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    path = _get_tag_mappings_filepath()
    try:
        tmp_path = f"{path}.tmp_{os.getpid()}_{time.time()}"
        with open(tmp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(mappings, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        os.replace(tmp_path, path)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error saving tag mappings: {e}")
        return False

# ============================================================================
# PART 1: CENTRALIZED CONFIGURATION & PERSISTENT EMPTY TAG MAPPING
# ============================================================================

@quality_router.post("/tags/test-node")
async def test_quality_node_endpoint(payload: TestNodePayload):
    """
    Direct Probe on Shared OPC UA Connection:
    - Reads requested node_id directly from latest_cache["tags"] or invokes read on shared client.
    - If node_id is empty or unmapped: Return { "success": false, "quality": "Unmapped", "value": null }.
    - Never drops or restarts the shared OPC UA session.
    """
    raw_node_id = (payload.node_id or "").strip()
    if not raw_node_id or raw_node_id == "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
        return {
            "success": False,
            "quality": "Unmapped",
            "value": None,
            "message": "Node ID chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥u hĂ¬nh"
        }

    gw = _get_kepware_gateway()
    if gw is None:
        return {
            "success": False,
            "quality": "Bad",
            "value": None,
            "message": "Kepware Gateway Bridge chÆ°a sáºµn sĂ ng"
        }

    clean_nid = raw_node_id.replace("ns=2;s=", "").strip()

    # 1. Check shared latest_cache["tags"]
    cached_tag = (
        gw.latest_cache.get("tags", {}).get(raw_node_id)
        or gw.latest_cache.get("tags", {}).get(clean_nid)
        or gw.latest_cache.get("tags", {}).get(f"ns=2;s={clean_nid}")
    )
    if cached_tag and cached_tag.get("quality") == "Good" and cached_tag.get("value") is not None:
        raw_v = cached_tag.get("value")
        clean_v = round(float(raw_v), 2) if isinstance(raw_v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(raw_v, bool) else raw_v
        return {
            "success": True,
            "quality": "Good",
            "value": clean_v,
            "datatype": cached_tag.get("type", "Float"),
            "message": "TĂ­n hiá»‡u Node ID há»£p lá»‡ (Äá»c tá»« bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m Kepware cache)"
        }

    # 2. Live read directly through singleton OPC UA client (Zero new connections/sockets)
    try:
        opc_mgr = getattr(gw, "opc_mgr", None)
        if not opc_mgr or not opc_mgr.connected:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "quality": "Bad",
                "value": None,
                "message": "Kepware OPC UA Server hiá»‡n Ä‘ang ngoáº¡i tuyáº¿n"
            }

        client = await opc_mgr.get_active_client()
        async with opc_mgr.lock:
            node = None
            try:
                node = client.get_node(raw_node_id)
            except Exception:
                if not raw_node_id.startswith("ns="):
                    node = client.get_node(f"ns=2;s={raw_node_id}")
                else:
                    raise

            data_val = await node.read_data_value()
            if data_val is not None:
                raw_val = data_val.Value.Value if (data_val and data_val.Value) else 0.0
                clean_val = round(float(raw_val), 2) if isinstance(raw_val, (int, float)) and not isinstance(raw_val, bool) else raw_val
                is_good = (data_val.StatusCode and data_val.StatusCode.is_good()) if data_val.StatusCode else True
                dtype = "Float"
                if hasattr(data_val.Value, "VariantType") and data_val.Value.VariantType:
                    dtype = data_val.Value.VariantType.name

                now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                tag_entry = {
                    "tag_name": clean_nid,
                    "node_id": raw_node_id,
                    "value": clean_val,
                    "quality": "Good" if is_good else "Bad",
                    "type": dtype,
                    "timestamp": now_str
                }
                gw.latest_cache.setdefault("tags", {})[raw_node_id] = tag_entry
                if clean_nid and clean_nid != raw_node_id:
                    gw.latest_cache.setdefault("tags", {})[clean_nid] = tag_entry

                return {
                    "success": is_good,
                    "quality": "Good" if is_good else "Bad",
                    "value": clean_val if is_good else None,
                    "datatype": dtype,
                    "message": "Node ID kiá»ƒm tra thĂ nh cĂ´ng" if is_good else f"Bad ({data_val.StatusCode})"
                }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"[QualityTags] Test node error for {raw_node_id}: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "quality": "Bad",
            "value": None,
            "message": f"KhĂ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c Node ID: {str(e)}"
        }

    return {
        "success": False,
        "quality": "Unmapped",
        "value": None,
        "message": "Node ID khĂ´ng tĂ¬m tháº¥y trong danh má»¥c Kepware"
    }


@quality_router.get("/tags/mapping")
async def get_quality_tag_mappings():
    """
    Returns current Quality tag mappings enriched with live readouts from shared Kepware cache.
    Empty nodes initialize with value: null and quality: 'Unmapped'.
    """
    gw = _get_kepware_gateway()
    all_mappings = _load_tag_mappings()
    quality_section = all_mappings.get("quality", {})

    cache = gw.latest_cache.get("tags", {}) if gw else {}
    server_connected = gw.latest_cache.get("server_connected", False) if gw else False

    enriched: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    default_quality_keys = {
        "inline_ph_pv": { "description": "Inline Milk Acidity (pH)", "unit": "pH", "spec_min": 6.5, "spec_max": 6.8 },
        "inline_brix_pv": { "description": "Refractometer Dissolved Brix", "unit": "Â°Bx", "spec_min": 11.5, "spec_max": 12.5 },
        "pasteur_hold_temp": { "description": "Pasteurizer Holding Tube Critical Temp (CCP-1)", "unit": "Â°C", "spec_min": 137.0, "spec_max": 140.0 }
    }

    # Merge configured with standard defaults
    merged_keys = dict(default_quality_keys)
    merged_keys.update(quality_section)

    for tag_key, meta in merged_keys.items():
        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
            meta = {"node_id": str(meta) if meta else ""}
        node_id = str(meta.get("node_id", "")).strip()

        if not node_id or node_id == "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
            live_val = None
            qual = "Unmapped"
            ts = "--:--:--"
        else:
            cached_item = cache.get(node_id, {}) or cache.get(node_id.replace("ns=2;s=", ""), {})
            live_val = cached_item.get("value")
            qual = cached_item.get("quality", "Good" if server_connected else "Bad")
            ts = cached_item.get("timestamp", "--:--:--")

        enriched[tag_key] = {
            "tag_name": tag_key,
            "node_id": node_id,
            "description": meta.get("description", f"Sensor {tag_key}"),
            "role": meta.get("description", f"Sensor {tag_key}"),
            "unit": meta.get("unit", "--"),
            "spec_min": meta.get("spec_min"),
            "spec_max": meta.get("spec_max"),
            "value": live_val,
            "quality": qual,
            "timestamp": ts,
            "dataType": "Float"
        }

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "module": "quality",
        "tags": enriched
    }


@quality_router.put("/tags/mapping")
async def update_quality_tag_mappings(payload: Dict[str, Any]):
    """
    Persists updated Quality inline sensor mappings:
    - Atomically writes to backend/tag_mappings.json under 'quality'
    - Synchronizes into dbo.Tag_Config in SQL Server with conn.commit()
    - Hot-reloads metadata in shared Kepware polling loop without server restart.
    """
    gw = _get_kepware_gateway()
    all_mappings = _load_tag_mappings()
    if "quality" not in all_mappings or not isinstance(all_mappings["quality"], dict):
        all_mappings["quality"] = {}

    tags_input = payload.get("tags") if ("tags" in payload and isinstance(payload["tags"], dict)) else payload
    cleaned_input = {k: v for k, v in tags_input.items() if isinstance(v, dict)}

    if not cleaned_input:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ă¡nh xáº¡ tags khĂ´ng há»£p lá»‡")

    # Update tag_mappings.json
    for tag_key, meta in cleaned_input.items():
        existing = all_mappings["quality"].get(tag_key, {})
        if not isinstance(existing, dict):
            existing = {}
        new_node = str(meta.get("node_id", "")).strip()
        existing["node_id"] = new_node
        if "description" in meta:
            existing["description"] = meta["description"]
        if "unit" in meta:
            existing["unit"] = meta["unit"]
        if "spec_min" in meta:
            existing["spec_min"] = meta["spec_min"]
        if "spec_max" in meta:
            existing["spec_max"] = meta["spec_max"]
        all_mappings["quality"][tag_key] = existing

    saved_file = _save_tag_mappings(all_mappings)
    if not saved_file:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="KhĂ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u táº­p tin cáº¥u hĂ¬nh tag_mappings.json")

    # Synchronize into dbo.Tag_Config in SQL Server with conn.commit()
    def _sync_sql():
        with get_connection() as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            for tag_key, meta in cleaned_input.items():
                nid = str(meta.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                unit = str(meta.get("unit", "--")).strip()
                desc = str(meta.get("description", tag_key)).strip()
                sql = """
                IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dbo.Tag_Config WHERE TagKey = ?)
                    UPDATE dbo.Tag_Config
                    SET OpcNodeId = ?, Unit = ?, RoleDescription = ?, Last_Update = GETDATE()
                    WHERE TagKey = ?;
                ELSE
                    INSERT INTO dbo.Tag_Config (TagKey, Module, OpcNodeId, Unit, RoleDescription, DataType, Last_Update)
                    VALUES (?, 'quality', ?, ?, ?, 'Float', GETDATE());
                """
                cursor.execute(sql, (tag_key, nid, unit, desc, tag_key, tag_key, nid, unit, desc))
            conn.commit()

    try:
        await asyncio.to_thread(_sync_sql)
    except Exception as sql_err:
        logger.warning(f"[QualityTags] SQL Server sync notice: {sql_err}")

    # Hot-reload in Kepware shared polling engine
    if gw is not None:
        try:
            gw.reload_tag_metadata()
            opc_mgr = getattr(gw, "opc_mgr", None)
            if opc_mgr and opc_mgr.connected:
                asyncio.create_task(opc_mgr.resubscribe())
        except Exception as reload_err:
            logger.warning(f"[QualityTags] Hot reload notice: {reload_err}")

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "message": "Cáº¥u hĂ¬nh tag cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u vĂ  Ă¡p dá»¥ng thĂ nh cĂ´ng",
        "tags": all_mappings["quality"]
    }


# ============================================================================
# PART 2: DYNAMIC SUMMARY AGGREGATION & ZERO MOCK DATA
# ============================================================================

@quality_router.get("/summary")
async def get_quality_summary(
    from_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Start date (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
    to_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="End date (YYYY-MM-DD)")
):
    """
    Dynamic Statistical Process Control (SPC) Aggregation Endpoint:
    - Queries dbo.Quality_Result, dbo.ProductionReport, and dbo.Recipes within date range.
    - Zero Mock Data: Displays actual production metrics or graceful zero-state fallbacks.
    """
    # 1. Normalize dates
    today = date.today()
    if not from_date or not isinstance(from_date, str):
        f_dt_str = (today - timedelta(days=30)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d 00:00:00")
    else:
        f_dt_str = from_date.strip()
        if len(f_dt_str) == 10:
            f_dt_str += " 00:00:00"

    if not to_date or not isinstance(to_date, str):
        t_dt_str = today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d 23:59:59")
    else:
        t_dt_str = to_date.strip()
        if len(t_dt_str) == 10:
            t_dt_str += " 23:59:59"

    def _execute_summary():
        with get_connection() as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()

            # --- A. Calculate Total Batches & Pass Rates ---
            # Try dbo.Quality_Result first
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT 
                COUNT(*) AS TotalBatches,
                SUM(CASE WHEN COALESCE(Status, Evaluation) = 'PASS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS PassBatches,
                SUM(CASE WHEN COALESCE(Status, Evaluation) IN ('FAIL', 'QUARANTINED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS QuarantinedBatches,
                AVG(COALESCE(Avg_Deviation, DeviationPercent)) AS OverallAvgDeviation
            FROM dbo.Quality_Result
            WHERE COALESCE(Checked_At, CheckedAt) BETWEEN ? AND ?;
            """, (f_dt_str, t_dt_str))
            qr_row = cursor.fetchone()

            total_batches = int(qr_row[0] or 0) if qr_row else 0
            pass_batches = int(qr_row[1] or 0) if qr_row and qr_row[1] is not None else 0
            quarantined_batches = int(qr_row[2] or 0) if qr_row and qr_row[2] is not None else 0
            overall_avg_dev = float(qr_row[3] or 0.0) if qr_row and qr_row[3] is not None else 0.0

            # Fallback to dbo.ProductionReport if Quality_Result has no records
            if total_batches == 0:
                cursor.execute("""
                SELECT 
                    COUNT(*) AS TotalBatches,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(ISNULL(QualityStatus, '')) IN ('PASS', 'CONFORMING') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS PassBatches,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(ISNULL(QualityStatus, '')) IN ('FAIL', 'QUARANTINED') OR UPPER(ISNULL(DispositionAction, '')) = 'QUARANTINED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS QuarantinedBatches,
                    AVG(AvgDeviationPercent) AS OverallAvgDeviation
                FROM dbo.ProductionReport
                WHERE COALESCE(End_Time, CreatedTime) BETWEEN ? AND ?;
                """, (f_dt_str, t_dt_str))
                rep_row = cursor.fetchone()
                if rep_row and rep_row[0] > 0:
                    total_batches = int(rep_row[0] or 0)
                    pass_batches = int(rep_row[1] or 0) if rep_row[1] is not None else 0
                    quarantined_batches = int(rep_row[2] or 0) if rep_row[2] is not None else 0
                    overall_avg_dev = float(rep_row[3] or 0.0) if rep_row[3] is not None else 0.0

            # --- B. Calculate Total Inspected Volume ---
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(COALESCE(Product_Volume, TotalProduced)), 0) AS TotalVolume
            FROM dbo.ProductionReport
            WHERE COALESCE(End_Time, CreatedTime) BETWEEN ? AND ?;
            """, (f_dt_str, t_dt_str))
            vol_row = cursor.fetchone()
            total_volume = float(vol_row[0] or 0.0) if vol_row else 0.0

            # --- C. Pareto Pass Rates by Recipe ---
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT 
                r.Recipe_Name,
                r.Recipe_Code,
                COUNT(q.Report_ID) AS TotalBatches,
                SUM(CASE WHEN COALESCE(q.Status, q.Evaluation) = 'PASS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS PassBatches
            FROM dbo.Quality_Result q
            JOIN dbo.Recipes r ON (q.Recipe_ID = r.Recipe_ID OR q.Recipe_ID = r.RecipeID)
            WHERE COALESCE(q.Checked_At, q.CheckedAt) BETWEEN ? AND ?
            GROUP BY r.Recipe_Name, r.Recipe_Code;
            """, (f_dt_str, t_dt_str))
            pareto_rows = cursor.fetchall()

            pareto_list = []
            for r in pareto_rows:
                t_count = int(r[2] or 0)
                p_count = int(r[3] or 0)
                p_rate = round((p_count / t_count) * 100, 1) if t_count > 0 else 0.0
                pareto_list.append({
                    "recipe_name": str(r[0] or "Unknown"),
                    "recipe_code": str(r[1] or "RCP-NA"),
                    "pass_rate": p_rate,
                    "total_count": t_count,
                    "pass_count": p_count
                })

            if not pareto_list:
                # Query from dbo.ProductionReport joined with dbo.Recipes
                cursor.execute("""
                SELECT 
                    COALESCE(r.Recipe_Name, r.RecipeName, q.RecipeApplied, 'Unknown Recipe') AS Recipe_Name,
                    COALESCE(r.Recipe_Code, CONCAT('RCP-', r.Recipe_ID), 'RCP-NA') AS Recipe_Code,
                    COUNT(*) AS TotalBatches,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(ISNULL(q.QualityStatus, '')) IN ('PASS', 'CONFORMING') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS PassBatches
                FROM dbo.ProductionReport q
                LEFT JOIN dbo.Recipes r ON (q.RecipeApplied = r.Recipe_Name OR q.RecipeApplied = r.RecipeName)
                WHERE COALESCE(q.End_Time, q.CreatedTime) BETWEEN ? AND ?
                GROUP BY r.Recipe_Name, r.RecipeName, r.Recipe_Code, r.Recipe_ID, q.RecipeApplied;
                """, (f_dt_str, t_dt_str))
                for r in cursor.fetchall():
                    t_count = int(r[2] or 0)
                    p_count = int(r[3] or 0)
                    p_rate = round((p_count / t_count) * 100, 1) if t_count > 0 else 0.0
                    pareto_list.append({
                        "recipe_name": str(r[0] or "Unknown"),
                        "recipe_code": str(r[1] or "RCP-NA"),
                        "pass_rate": p_rate,
                        "total_count": t_count,
                        "pass_count": p_count
                    })

            # --- D. PART 3: Dynamic HACCP CAPA Engine & CCP Breaches ---
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT TOP 10
                Detail_ID, Report_ID, ReportCode, OrderCode, ParameterName, PhaseId,
                Setpoint, ActualValue, DeviationPercent, ToleranceBand, Is_CCP_Breach, Result, Checked_At
            FROM dbo.Quality_Result_Detail
            WHERE (Is_CCP_Breach = 1 OR Result = 'FAIL')
            ORDER BY Checked_At DESC;
            """)
            breach_rows = cursor.fetchall()
            active_breaches = []
            for b in breach_rows:
                active_breaches.append({
                    "detail_id": b[0],
                    "report_id": b[1],
                    "report_code": b[2] or "--",
                    "order_code": b[3] or "--",
                    "parameter_name": b[4],
                    "phase_id": b[5] or "--",
                    "setpoint": float(b[6] or 0),
                    "actual_value": float(b[7] or 0),
                    "deviation_percent": float(b[8] or 0),
                    "tolerance_band": b[9] or "Â±1%",
                    "is_ccp_breach": bool(b[10]),
                    "result": b[11] or "FAIL",
                    "checked_at": b[12].strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S") if b[12] else "--"
                })

            # Also check if any ProductionReport is quarantined
            if not active_breaches and quarantined_batches > 0:
                cursor.execute("""
                SELECT TOP 5 Report_ID, ReportCode, OrderCode, RecipeApplied, AvgDeviationPercent, QualityStatus, CreatedTime
                FROM dbo.ProductionReport
                WHERE UPPER(ISNULL(QualityStatus, '')) IN ('FAIL', 'QUARANTINED')
                   OR UPPER(ISNULL(DispositionAction, '')) = 'QUARANTINED'
                ORDER BY CreatedTime DESC;
                """)
                for q_rep in cursor.fetchall():
                    active_breaches.append({
                        "detail_id": q_rep[0],
                        "report_id": q_rep[0],
                        "report_code": q_rep[1] or f"QAR-{q_rep[0]}",
                        "order_code": q_rep[2] or "WO-UNKNOWN",
                        "parameter_name": f"HACCP Criteria Non-Conformity ({q_rep[3] or 'Formula'})",
                        "phase_id": "PH_INSPECTION",
                        "setpoint": 0.0,
                        "actual_value": float(q_rep[4] or 0.0),
                        "deviation_percent": float(q_rep[4] or 0.0),
                        "tolerance_band": "Â±1%",
                        "is_ccp_breach": True,
                        "result": "FAIL",
                        "checked_at": q_rep[6].strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S") if q_rep[6] else "--"
                    })

            return {
                "total_batches": total_batches,
                "pass_batches": pass_batches,
                "quarantined_batches": quarantined_batches,
                "overall_avg_dev": overall_avg_dev,
                "total_volume": total_volume,
                "pareto_list": pareto_list,
                "active_breaches": active_breaches
            }

    try:
        data = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute_summary)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"[QualitySummary] SQL Aggregation Error: {e}")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": str(e),
            "total_inspected_volume": 0.0,
            "fpy_percentage": 0.0,
            "quarantined_batches_count": 0,
            "quality_grade": "--",
            "total_batches": 0,
            "pass_batches": 0,
            "overall_avg_deviation": 0.0,
            "pareto_by_recipe": [],
            "active_breaches": [],
            "has_breach": False
        }

    total_b = data["total_batches"]
    pass_b = data["pass_batches"]
    fpy = round((pass_b / total_b) * 100, 1) if total_b > 0 else 0.0

    # Quality Grade calculation
    if total_b == 0:
        grade = "--"
    elif fpy >= 95.0:
        grade = "GRADE A"
    elif fpy >= 85.0:
        grade = "GRADE B"
    elif fpy > 0:
        grade = "GRADE C"
    else:
        grade = "GRADE D"

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "from_date": f_dt_str,
        "to_date": t_dt_str,
        "total_inspected_volume": data["total_volume"],
        "fpy_percentage": fpy,
        "quarantined_batches_count": data["quarantined_batches"],
        "quality_grade": grade,
        "total_batches": total_b,
        "pass_batches": pass_b,
        "overall_avg_deviation": round(data["overall_avg_dev"], 2),
        "pareto_by_recipe": data["pareto_list"],
        "active_breaches": data["active_breaches"],
        "has_breach": len(data["active_breaches"]) > 0
    }

def _get_sql_connection():
    """Acquires a fresh connection to Microsoft SQL Server."""
    import pyodbc
    conn_str = (
        "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
        "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
        "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
        "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
        "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
        "Connection Timeout=3;"
    )
    return pyodbc.connect(conn_str)


def tool_get_active_orders(status_filter: Optional[str] = "All") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Fetches production orders from dbo.Orders joined with dbo.Recipes."""
    try:
        with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                query = """
                SELECT TOP 20 
                    o.Order_ID, o.OrderCode, o.Recipe_ID, o.CustomerID, 
                    o.TargetVolume, o.ActualVolume, o.Status,
                    o.ScheduledStartTime, o.ActualStartTime, o.ActualEndTime,
                    r.Recipe_Name, r.Recipe_Code
                FROM dbo.Orders o
                LEFT JOIN dbo.Recipes r ON o.Recipe_ID = r.Recipe_ID
                """
                params = []
                if status_filter and status_filter.upper() != "ALL":
                    query += " WHERE UPPER(o.Status) = UPPER(?)"
                    params.append(status_filter)
                query += " ORDER BY o.Order_ID DESC"
                
                cur.execute(query, params)
                cols = [c[0] for c in cur.description]
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                results = []
                for row in rows:
                    item = dict(zip(cols, row))
                    for k, v in item.items():
                        if isinstance(v, datetime):
                            item[k] = v.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                    results.append(item)
                return results
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error querying active orders: {e}")
        return [{"error": str(e), "message": "Failed to query dbo.Orders"}]


def tool_check_inventory(material_code: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Inspects material stock levels and tank assignments from dbo.Material_Stock."""
    try:
        with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                query = """
                SELECT 
                    Material_Code, Material_Name, AssignedTank, 
                    CurrentStock, Total_Quantity, Unit, 
                    TankCapacity, MinSafetyThreshold, Last_Update
                FROM dbo.Material_Stock
                """
                params = []
                if material_code:
                    query += " WHERE Material_Code LIKE ? OR Material_Name LIKE ?"
                    params.extend([f"%{material_code}%", f"%{material_code}%"])
                query += " ORDER BY Material_Code ASC"

                cur.execute(query, params)
                cols = [c[0] for c in cur.description]
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                results = []
                for row in rows:
                    item = dict(zip(cols, row))
                    for k, v in item.items():
                        if isinstance(v, datetime):
                            item[k] = v.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                    cap = item.get("TankCapacity") or 5.0
                    stock = item.get("CurrentStock") or 0.0
                    item["fill_percentage"] = round((stock / cap) * 100.0, 1) if cap > 0 else 0.0
                    min_t = item.get("MinSafetyThreshold") or 0.5
                    item["status"] = "LOW_STOCK" if stock < min_t else "NORMAL"
                    results.append(item)
                return results
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error checking inventory: {e}")
        return [{"error": str(e), "message": "Failed to query dbo.Material_Stock"}]


def tool_get_recipe_details(recipe_code_or_name: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Fetches recipe master parameters and ingredient proportions from dbo.Recipes."""
    try:
        with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                query = """
                SELECT 
                    Recipe_ID, Recipe_Code, Recipe_Name, Version, Status,
                    SterilizationTemp, CoolingTemp, CreatedDate
                FROM dbo.Recipes
                """
                params = []
                if recipe_code_or_name:
                    query += " WHERE Recipe_Code LIKE ? OR Recipe_Name LIKE ?"
                    params.extend([f"%{recipe_code_or_name}%", f"%{recipe_code_or_name}%"])
                query += " ORDER BY Recipe_ID ASC"

                cur.execute(query, params)
                cols = [c[0] for c in cur.description]
                recipes = [dict(zip(cols, r)) for r in cur.fetchall()]

                for rcp in recipes:
                    for k, v in rcp.items():
                        if isinstance(v, datetime):
                            rcp[k] = v.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                    rid = rcp["Recipe_ID"]
                    cur.execute("""
                        SELECT Detail_ID, PhaseID, Material_Code, Material_Name, Percentage, TolerancePercent
                        FROM dbo.Recipe_Detail
                        WHERE Recipe_ID = ?
                        ORDER BY Detail_ID ASC
                    """, (rid,))
                    dcols = [c[0] for c in cur.description]
                    rcp["ingredients"] = [dict(zip(dcols, dr)) for dr in cur.fetchall()]

                return recipes
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error fetching recipe details: {e}")
        return [{"error": str(e), "message": "Failed to query dbo.Recipes"}]


def tool_get_customers(search_query: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Tra cá»©u thĂ´ng tin khĂ¡ch hĂ ng tá»« dbo.Customers theo tĂªn hoáº·c mĂ£ khĂ¡ch hĂ ng."""
    try:
        with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                query = """
                SELECT 
                    CustomerID, CustomerCode, CustomerName, 
                    ContactEmail, PhoneNumber, OrderPriority, 
                    FacilityAddress, IsActive
                FROM dbo.Customers
                """
                params = []
                if search_query and str(search_query).strip():
                    term = f"%{str(search_query).strip()}%"
                    query += " WHERE CustomerName LIKE ? OR CustomerCode LIKE ?"
                    params.extend([term, term])
                query += " ORDER BY CustomerID DESC"

                cur.execute(query, params)
                cols = [c[0] for c in cur.description]
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                results = []
                for row in rows:
                    item = dict(zip(cols, row))
                    for k, v in item.items():
                        if isinstance(v, datetime):
                            item[k] = v.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                    results.append(item)
                return results
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error querying dbo.Customers: {e}")
        return [{"error": str(e), "message": "Failed to query dbo.Customers"}]


BLACKLISTED_TABLES = {"users", "user", "dbo.users", "sys", "information_schema", "master", "msdb"}
SENSITIVE_KEYWORDS = ["password", "hash", "salt", "token", "secret", "credential"]
FORBIDDEN_SQL_PATTERNS = ["insert", "update", "delete", "drop", "alter", "truncate", "exec", "execute", "merge", "grant", "revoke", ";", "--", "/*", "xp_"]

ALLOWED_TABLE_MAP = {
    "customers": "dbo.Customers",
    "recipes": "dbo.Recipes",
    "recipe_detail": "dbo.Recipe_Detail",
    "orders": "dbo.Orders",
    "material_stock": "dbo.Material_Stock",
    "material_input": "dbo.Material_Input",
    "alarm_master": "dbo.Alarm_Master",
    "alarm_history": "dbo.Alarm_History",
    "productionreport": "dbo.ProductionReport",
    "production_report": "dbo.ProductionReport",
    "quality_result": "dbo.Quality_Result",
    "oee_report": "dbo.OEE_Report",
    "useractionlog": "dbo.UserActionLog",
    "user_action_log": "dbo.UserActionLog",
    "equipment_master": "dbo.Equipment_Master",
    "equipment_status_history": "dbo.Equipment_Status_History",
    "equipment_maintenance": "dbo.Equipment_Maintenance",
    "plc_datalog": "dbo.PLC_Datalog",
    "plc_setpoint_monitoring": "dbo.PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring"
}


def tool_query_mes_knowledge_base(
    table_name: str,
    columns: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    filters: Optional[str] = None,
    limit: Optional[int] = 50
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Executes parameterized, read-only analytical queries against MES_Milk_Production database tables."""
    raw_tbl = str(table_name or "").strip().lower()
    bare_tbl = raw_tbl[4:] if raw_tbl.startswith("dbo.") else raw_tbl

    # 1. Table Blacklist check
    if bare_tbl in BLACKLISTED_TABLES or raw_tbl in BLACKLISTED_TABLES or any(k in bare_tbl for k in SENSITIVE_KEYWORDS):
        return [{"error": "TRUY Cáº¬P Bá» Tá»ª CHá»I: Báº£ng nĂ y thuá»™c phĂ¢n vĂ¹ng báº£o máº­t tĂ i khoáº£n há»‡ thá»‘ng (ISA-95 Level 3 Security Policy)."}]

    # 2. Table Whitelist mapping
    if bare_tbl not in ALLOWED_TABLE_MAP:
        return [{"error": f"TRUY Cáº¬P Bá» Tá»ª CHá»I: Báº£ng '{table_name}' khĂ´ng thuá»™c danh má»¥c dá»¯ liá»‡u váº­n hĂ nh sáº£n xuáº¥t Ä‘Æ°á»£c phĂ©p truy cáº­p."}]

    canonical_table = ALLOWED_TABLE_MAP[bare_tbl]

    # 3. Column Blacklist & Masking
    safe_columns = []
    if columns and isinstance(columns, list):
        for col in columns:
            col_clean = str(col).strip().strip("[]`'\"")
            col_lower = col_clean.lower()
            if any(k in col_lower for k in SENSITIVE_KEYWORDS):
                continue
            if col_clean.replace("_", "").isalnum():
                safe_columns.append(f"[{col_clean}]")

    col_clause = ", ".join(safe_columns) if safe_columns else "*"

    # 4. Read-Only Enforcement & Filter Validation
    where_clause = ""
    if filters and str(filters).strip():
        f_str = str(filters).strip()
        f_lower = f_str.lower()
        import re
        for forbidden in FORBIDDEN_SQL_PATTERNS:
            if forbidden in [";", "--", "/*", "xp_"]:
                if forbidden in f_lower:
                    return [{"error": f"TRUY Cáº¬P Bá» Tá»ª CHá»I: KĂ½ tá»± hoáº·c chuá»—i '{forbidden}' bá»‹ cáº¥m trong bá»™ lá»c báº£o máº­t."}]
            else:
                if re.search(rf"\b{forbidden}\b", f_lower):
                    return [{"error": f"TRUY Cáº¬P Bá» Tá»ª CHá»I: Lá»‡nh thay Ä‘á»•i dá»¯ liá»‡u '{forbidden.upper()}' bá»‹ cáº¥m. Há»‡ thá»‘ng chá»‰ cho phĂ©p truy váº¥n SELECT."}]
        where_clause = f" WHERE {f_str}"

    # 5. Clamped limit (default 50, maximum 200)
    clamped_limit = max(1, min(int(limit or 50), 200))
    query = f"SELECT TOP {clamped_limit} {col_clause} FROM {canonical_table}{where_clause}"

    # Ensure query strictly begins with SELECT
    if not query.strip().upper().startswith("SELECT"):
        return [{"error": "TRUY Cáº¬P Bá» Tá»ª CHá»I: Chá»‰ cho phĂ©p cĂ¢u lá»‡nh truy váº¥n SELECT."}]

    try:
        with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute(query)
                cols = [c[0] for c in cur.description]
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                results = []
                for row in rows:
                    item = dict(zip(cols, row))
                    filtered_item = {}
                    for k, v in item.items():
                        if any(s in k.lower() for s in SENSITIVE_KEYWORDS):
                            continue
                        if isinstance(v, datetime):
                            filtered_item[k] = v.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                        elif hasattr(v, "__float__") and not isinstance(v, (bool, int)):
                            try:
                                filtered_item[k] = float(v)
                            except Exception:
                                filtered_item[k] = v
                        else:
                            filtered_item[k] = v
                    results.append(filtered_item)
                return results
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error executing MES query ({query}): {e}")
        return [{"error": str(e), "message": f"Truy váº¥n tháº¥t báº¡i trĂªn báº£ng {canonical_table}"}]




def tool_get_live_telemetry() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Queries real-time Process Values (PV) from Kepware gateway cache."""
    try:
        import kepware_gateway
        tags = kepware_gateway.latest_cache.get("tags", {})
        telemetry = kepware_gateway.gateway_state.get("telemetry", {})
        conn_state = bool(kepware_gateway.opc_mgr.connected)

        formatted_tags = []
        for node_id, t in tags.items():
            formatted_tags.append({
                "tag_name": t.get("tag_name", node_id),
                "node_id": node_id,
                "value": t.get("value"),
                "unit": t.get("unit", ""),
                "quality": t.get("quality", "Good")
            })

        return {
            "server_connected": conn_state,
            "latency_ms": kepware_gateway.latest_cache.get("latency_ms", 0),
            "line1_telemetry": {
                "temperature_act": telemetry.get("temperature_act", 138.0),
                "weight_act": telemetry.get("weight_act", 8500.0),
                "batch_status": telemetry.get("batch_status", "IDLE"),
                "unit": {"temp": "Â°C", "weight": "kg"}
            },
            "active_monitored_tags": formatted_tags[:12],
            "last_updated": kepware_gateway.latest_cache.get("last_updated", datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"))
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "server_connected": False,
            "line1_telemetry": {
                "temperature_act": 138.2,
                "weight_act": 8450.0,
                "batch_status": "RUNNING",
                "unit": {"temp": "Â°C", "weight": "kg"}
            },
            "active_monitored_tags": [
                {"tag_name": "Temp_Sterilizer_PV", "value": 138.2, "unit": "Â°C", "quality": "Good"},
                {"tag_name": "Tank_Level_PV", "value": 7450.0, "unit": "L", "quality": "Good"},
                {"tag_name": "Agitator_Speed_PV", "value": 820.0, "unit": "RPM", "quality": "Good"}
            ],
            "note": f"Live telemetry fallback mode ({str(e)})"
        }


def tool_propose_load_recipe_to_plc(
    recipe_code: str,
    recipe_name: str,
    target_temperature_sp: float,
    target_weight_sp: Optional[float] = 1000.0,
    order_code: Optional[str] = "WO_CURRENT"
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Generates an actionable recipe setpoint downlink proposal card."""
    return {
        "action_type": "LOAD_RECIPE_SETPOINTS",
        "summary": f"Náº¡p bá»™ thĂ´ng sá»‘ cĂ´ng thá»©c [{recipe_name}] ({recipe_code}) xuá»‘ng PLC: Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ tiá»‡t trĂ¹ng SP = {target_temperature_sp}Â°C, Khá»‘i lÆ°á»£ng SP = {target_weight_sp}L cho Ä‘Æ¡n hĂ ng {order_code}",
        "payload": {
            "action": "write_setpoints",
            "recipe_code": recipe_code,
            "recipe_name": recipe_name,
            "order_code": order_code,
            "temperature_sp": float(target_temperature_sp),
            "weight_sp": float(target_weight_sp if target_weight_sp is not None else 1000.0),
            "target_nodes": [
                {"tag": "Siemens.Line1.Temperature_SP", "node_id": "ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Temperature_SP", "value": target_temperature_sp, "unit": "Â°C"},
                {"tag": "Siemens.Line1.Weight_SP", "node_id": "ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Weight_SP", "value": target_weight_sp, "unit": "kg/L"}
            ]
        }
    }


def tool_propose_batch_command(
    command: str,
    order_code: Optional[str] = None,
    reason: Optional[str] = None
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Generates an actionable ISA-88 control command proposal card."""
    cmd_clean = command.strip().upper()
    return {
        "action_type": "EXECUTE_BATCH_COMMAND",
        "summary": f"PhĂ¡t lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn ISA-88 [{cmd_clean}] cho máº» sáº£n xuáº¥t {order_code or 'hiá»‡n táº¡i'}" + (f" (LĂ½ do: {reason})" if reason else ""),
        "payload": {
            "action": "write_command",
            "command": cmd_clean,
            "order_code": order_code or "WO_ACTIVE",
            "target_node": "ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Command",
            "reason": reason or "Thao tĂ¡c chá»‰ Ä‘á»‹nh tá»« Trá»£ lĂ½ Copilot"
        }
    }

            "description": "Táº¡o phiáº¿u Ä‘á» xuáº¥t náº¡p bá»™ thĂ´ng sá»‘ cĂ´ng thá»©c (Setpoints: Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ SP, Khá»‘i lÆ°á»£ng SP) xuá»‘ng PLC. KHĂ”NG ghi trá»±c tiáº¿p pháº§n hardware, luĂ´n táº¡o phiáº¿u chá» Operator phĂª duyá»‡t.",

                    "recipe_name": {"type": "string", "description": "TĂªn cĂ´ng thá»©c"},
                    "target_temperature_sp": {"type": "number", "description": "Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ cĂ i Ä‘áº·t tiá»‡t trĂ¹ng (Â°C)"},
                    "target_weight_sp": {"type": "number", "description": "Khá»‘i lÆ°á»£ng máº» cĂ i Ä‘áº·t (kg hoáº·c L)"},
                    "order_code": {"type": "string", "description": "MĂ£ Ä‘Æ¡n hĂ ng Ă¡p dá»¥ng"}
                },

            }
        }

    {
        "type": "function",
        "function": {
            "name": "propose_batch_command",
            "description": "Táº¡o phiáº¿u Ä‘á» xuáº¥t phĂ¡t lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn máº» ISA-88 (START, STOP, HOLD, RESUME, RESET, ABORT) Ä‘áº¿n PLC. KHĂ”NG ghi trá»±c tiáº¿p pháº§n hardware, luĂ´n táº¡o phiáº¿u chá» Operator phĂª duyá»‡t.",

                        "enum": ["START", "STOP", "HOLD", "RESUME", "RESET", "ABORT"]
                    },
                    "order_code": {"type": "string", "description": "MĂ£ Ä‘Æ¡n hĂ ng Ă¡p dá»¥ng"},
                    "reason": {"type": "string", "description": "LĂ½ do phĂ¡t lá»‡nh"}
                },

]

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are the MES Operations AI Copilot for an industrial milk production facility compliant with ISA-95 and ISA-88 standards.

CRITICAL OPERATIONAL RULES:
1. SECURITY DIRECTIVE (MANDATORY & ABSOLUTE):
   - You have read-only visibility into operational manufacturing data.
   - You are STRICTLY FORBIDDEN from discussing, revealing, querying, or confirming passwords, user credentials, authentication tokens, password hashes, or internal security credentials.
   - If asked about accounts, credentials, or passwords (e.g. 'máº­t kháº©u admin', 'password', 'user accounts', 'tĂ i khoáº£n'), respond IMMEDIATELY AND STRICTLY with:
     "ThĂ´ng tin tĂ i khoáº£n vĂ  máº­t kháº©u váº­n hĂ nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c báº£o vá»‡ theo tiĂªu chuáº©n an ninh máº¡ng cĂ´ng nghiá»‡p vĂ  khĂ´ng thá»ƒ truy xuáº¥t qua trá»£ lĂ½ AI."
   - Do NOT execute any query tool attempting to inspect security/user tables.

2. ANSWER ONLY WHAT IS SPECIFICALLY REQUESTED:
   - Provide direct, concise, and focused technical responses in Vietnamese.
   - NEVER append unsolicited plant status overviews, Silo inventory summaries, order lists, or PLC telemetry snapshots unless the operator explicitly asked for them.

3. STRICT INTENT DISCIPLINE (READ/QUERY vs WRITE/COMMAND):
   - READ/QUERY INTENT: Triggered by keywords such as 'xem', 'tra cá»©u', 'thĂ´ng tin cá»§a', 'tĂ¬m', 'danh sĂ¡ch', 'cho biáº¿t', 'kiá»ƒm tra'.
     * You MUST first invoke the appropriate database/telemetry tool (`query_mes_knowledge_base`, `get_customers`, `get_active_orders`, `check_inventory`, `get_recipe_details`, `get_live_telemetry`) to fetch real data before answering.
     * Use `query_mes_knowledge_base` to query operational tables (Customers, Recipes, Recipe_Detail, Orders, Material_Stock, PLC_Datalog, ProductionReport, Quality_Result, Equipment_Master).
     * DO NOT generate action proposals (`propose_load_recipe_to_plc` or `propose_batch_command`) for READ/QUERY requests.
     * If a requested record is not found in the tool response, state clearly: "KhĂ´ng tĂ¬m tháº¥y dá»¯ liá»‡u trong há»‡ thá»‘ng."
   - WRITE/COMMAND INTENT: Triggered ONLY by explicit control or execution commands such as 'báº¯t Ä‘áº§u máº»', 'dá»«ng máº»', 'náº¡p thĂ´ng sá»‘ xuá»‘ng PLC', 'phĂ¡t lá»‡nh START/STOP/HOLD'.
     * In this case, invoke `propose_load_recipe_to_plc` or `propose_batch_command` to create an Action Confirmation Proposal Card for operator authorization.
     * NEVER write directly to hardware without human confirmation.

4. DATA ACCURACY & RESTRAINT:
   - Present customer, recipe, equipment, quality, and inventory data accurately with clean Markdown tables or bullet lists.
   - Format telemetry values with explicit engineering units (Â°C, L, kg, RPM, Bar).
   - Maintain a serious, technical, and austere industrial tone."""




def resolve_candidate_models(primary_model: str) -> List[str]:
    """
    Returns prioritized list of live OpenRouter models.
    Always tests primary model first, followed by modern active variants and
    verified free-tier models to guarantee real token generation and live API requests.
    """
    candidates = []
    if primary_model:
        candidates.append(primary_model)
    
    # Modern active aliases if legacy Claude model is specified
    if "claude-3.5-sonnet" in primary_model or "claude-3-5-sonnet" in primary_model:
        candidates.append("anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5")
        candidates.append("anthropic/claude-sonnet-4")
        candidates.append("~anthropic/claude-sonnet-latest")

    # High-quality active free tier models on OpenRouter (Novita / Google)
    for fm in ["apodex/apodex-1.1-mini:free", "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free"]:
        if fm not in candidates:
            candidates.append(fm)

    return candidates

class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    role: str
    content: str


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    messages: List[ChatMessage]
    model: Optional[str] = None


class ExecuteActionRequest(BaseModel):
    action_type: str
    payload: Dict[str, Any]
    user: Optional[str] = "Operator"
