@ai_router.get("/health")
@ai_router.get("/status")
async def get_copilot_health():
    """Health check endpoint for AI Copilot status indicator."""
    env_file = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".env"
    load_dotenv(dotenv_path=env_file, override=True)
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "").strip() or OPENROUTER_API_KEY
    model = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_MODEL", "anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet").strip()

    has_key = bool(api_key and len(api_key) > 10)
    
    # Live probe to OpenRouter auth endpoint
    key_info = {}
    if has_key:
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=5.0) as client:
                res = await client.get(
                    "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/auth/key",
                    headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"}
                )
                if res.status_code == 200:
                    key_info = res.json().get("data", {})
        except Exception:
            pass

    return {
        "status": "online" if has_key else "offline",
        "model": model,
        "has_api_key": has_key,
        "provider": "OpenRouter",
        "is_free_tier": key_info.get("is_free_tier", True),
        "usage_limit": key_info.get("limit"),
        "free_model_requests": key_info.get("free_model_daily_requests", {}),
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
    }


@ai_router.post("/chat")
async def chat_with_copilot(req: ChatRequest):
    """
    Direct Live Multi-turn Conversational Tool Loop with OpenRouter API:
    - Strictly dispatches live API requests to https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions
    - Executes real SQL Server & Kepware tools upon model function calling
    - No hardcoded mock strings. 100% real LLM token inference.
    """
    env_file = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".env"
    load_dotenv(dotenv_path=env_file, override=True)
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "").strip() or OPENROUTER_API_KEY
    configured_model = req.model or os.getenv("OPENROUTER_MODEL", "").strip() or OPENROUTER_MODEL or "anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet"

    if not api_key:
        return {
            "message": "â ï¸ **OpenRouter API Key chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c thiáº¿t láº­p** trong `backend/.env`. Vui lĂ²ng cáº­p nháº­t `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` Ä‘á»ƒ kĂ­ch hoáº¡t AI Copilot.",
            "action_proposal": None
        }

    candidate_models = resolve_candidate_models(configured_model)
    logger.info(f"OpenRouter candidate models: {candidate_models}")

    # Build initial message stack
    conversation_messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT}
    ]

    for m in req.messages:
        conversation_messages.append({
            "role": m.role,
            "content": m.content
        })

    pending_action_proposal: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    headers = {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
        "HTTP-Referer": "http://localhost:8000",
        "X-Title": "Industrial-Batch-MES",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }

    last_error_details = ""

    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=45.0) as client:
        # Try candidate models to ensure live OpenRouter dispatch
        for model in candidate_models:
            logger.info(f"Dispatching live request to OpenRouter with model: [{model}]")
            max_tool_iterations = 4
            iteration = 0
            model_success = False

            while iteration < max_tool_iterations:
                iteration += 1
                request_body = {
                    "model": model,
                    "messages": conversation_messages,
                    "tools": TOOLS_SPEC,
                    "tool_choice": "auto",
                    "temperature": 0.2
                }

                try:
                    response = await client.post(
                        OPENROUTER_BASE_URL,
                        headers=headers,
                        json=request_body
                    )
                except httpx.TimeoutException:
                    last_error_details = f"YĂªu cáº§u tá»›i OpenRouter ({model}) bá»‹ quĂ¡ thá»i gian chá» (Timeout)."
                    break
                except Exception as e:
                    last_error_details = f"Lá»—i máº¡ng khi káº¿t ná»‘i OpenRouter ({model}): {str(e)}"
                    break

                # If model is deprecated / 404 or insufficient credits 402, try next candidate
                if response.status_code in (404, 402):
                    err_json = {}
                    try:
                        err_json = response.json()
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    msg = err_json.get("error", {}).get("message", response.text)
                    logger.warning(f"OpenRouter model [{model}] returned HTTP {response.status_code} ({msg}). Switching to fallback model...")
                    last_error_details = f"OpenRouter HTTP {response.status_code}: {msg}"
                    break  # break inner loop to try next candidate model

                if response.status_code != 200:
                    err_text = response.text
                    logger.error(f"OpenRouter error [{response.status_code}] on {model}: {err_text}")
                    last_error_details = f"OpenRouter tráº£ vá» mĂ£ lá»—i HTTP {response.status_code}: {err_text}"
                    break

                res_json = response.json()
                choices = res_json.get("choices", [])
                if not choices:
                    last_error_details = f"MĂ´ hĂ¬nh {model} khĂ´ng tráº£ vá» lá»±a chá»n nĂ o."
                    break

                model_success = True
                assistant_msg = choices[0].get("message", {})
                tool_calls = assistant_msg.get("tool_calls", [])

                # If no tool calls, this is the final synthesized answer
                if not tool_calls:
                    final_content = assistant_msg.get("content", "")
                    usage = res_json.get("usage", {})
                    logger.info(f"OpenRouter Live Completion Success ({model}) -> Tokens: {usage.get('total_tokens')}")
                    return {
                        "message": final_content,
                        "action_proposal": pending_action_proposal,
                        "model": model,
                        "usage": usage
                    }

                # If tool calls are present, append assistant message with tool calls
                conversation_messages.append(assistant_msg)

                # Execute tool calls
                for tc in tool_calls:
                    fn_name = tc.get("function", {}).get("name", "")
                    raw_args = tc.get("function", {}).get("arguments", "{}")
                    try:
                        args = json.loads(raw_args) if isinstance(raw_args, str) else raw_args
                    except Exception:
                        args = {}

                    logger.info(f"OpenRouter Model [{model}] Invoked MES Tool: {fn_name}({args})")

                    # Handle Read Tools
                    if fn_name == "query_mes_knowledge_base":
                        tbl = args.get("table_name", "")
                        cols = args.get("columns")
                        flts = args.get("filters")
                        lmt = args.get("limit", 50)
                        tool_res = await asyncio.to_thread(tool_query_mes_knowledge_base, tbl, cols, flts, lmt)
                    elif fn_name == "get_active_orders":
                        status_f = args.get("status_filter", "All")
                        tool_res = await asyncio.to_thread(tool_get_active_orders, status_f)
                    elif fn_name == "get_customers":
                        sq = args.get("search_query", "")
                        tool_res = await asyncio.to_thread(tool_get_customers, sq)
                    elif fn_name == "check_inventory":
                        mat_c = args.get("material_code")
                        tool_res = await asyncio.to_thread(tool_check_inventory, mat_c)
                    elif fn_name == "get_recipe_details":
                        rcp_n = args.get("recipe_code_or_name")
                        tool_res = await asyncio.to_thread(tool_get_recipe_details, rcp_n)
                    elif fn_name == "get_live_telemetry":
                        tool_res = await asyncio.to_thread(tool_get_live_telemetry)
                    
                    # Handle Proposal Tools (Write/Control)
                    elif fn_name == "propose_load_recipe_to_plc":
                        proposal = tool_propose_load_recipe_to_plc(
                            recipe_code=args.get("recipe_code", "RCP-01"),
                            recipe_name=args.get("recipe_name", "Fresh Pasteurized Milk"),
                            target_temperature_sp=float(args.get("target_temperature_sp", 138.0)),
                            target_weight_sp=float(args.get("target_weight_sp", 1000.0)),
                            order_code=args.get("order_code", "WO_CURRENT")
                        )
                        pending_action_proposal = proposal
                        tool_res = {
                            "status": "proposal_generated",
                            "summary": proposal["summary"],
                            "note": "Phiáº¿u Ä‘á» xuáº¥t náº¡p thĂ´ng sá»‘ Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c táº¡o trĂªn giao diá»‡n. Chá» ngÆ°á»i váº­n hĂ nh áº¥n XĂC NHáº¬N Ä‘á»ƒ thá»±c thi."
                        }
                    elif fn_name == "propose_batch_command":
                        proposal = tool_propose_batch_command(
                            command=args.get("command", "START"),
                            order_code=args.get("order_code"),
                            reason=args.get("reason")
                        )
                        pending_action_proposal = proposal
                        tool_res = {
                            "status": "proposal_generated",
                            "summary": proposal["summary"],
                            "note": "Phiáº¿u Ä‘á» xuáº¥t lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c táº¡o trĂªn giao diá»‡n. Chá» ngÆ°á»i váº­n hĂ nh áº¥n XĂC NHáº¬N Ä‘á»ƒ thá»±c thi."
                        }
                    else:
                        tool_res = {"error": f"Unknown tool: {fn_name}"}

                    # Add tool result to conversation history for next iteration
                    conversation_messages.append({
                        "role": "tool",
                        "tool_call_id": tc.get("id"),
                        "name": fn_name,
                        "content": json.dumps(tool_res, ensure_ascii=False)
                    })

            if model_success:
                return {
                    "message": assistant_msg.get("content", "ÄĂ£ xá»­ lĂ½ yĂªu cáº§u thĂ nh cĂ´ng qua OpenRouter."),
                    "action_proposal": pending_action_proposal,
                    "model": model
                }

    # If all models failed, return exact OpenRouter error transparently
    return {
        "message": f"â ï¸ **Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i OpenRouter API:** {last_error_details}\n\n*Vui lĂ²ng kiá»ƒm tra sá»‘ dÆ° credits hoáº·c thiáº¿t láº­p model trong `backend/.env`.*",
        "action_proposal": None
    }


@ai_router.post("/execute-action")
async def execute_operator_action(req: ExecuteActionRequest):
    """
    Operator-Authorized Action Execution:
    Executes setpoint downlink or ISA-88 batch command to physical/simulated Kepware OPC UA
    and writes permanent audit log to dbo.UserActionLog.
    """
    action_type = req.action_type
    payload = req.payload
    user = req.user or "Operator"

    logger.info(f"Operator [{user}] confirmed execution for action: {action_type}")

    # 1. Action: LOAD_RECIPE_SETPOINTS
    if action_type == "LOAD_RECIPE_SETPOINTS":
        temp_sp = float(payload.get("temperature_sp", 138.0))
        weight_sp = float(payload.get("weight_sp", 1000.0))
        order_code = payload.get("order_code", "WO_CURRENT")
        rcp_name = payload.get("recipe_name", "Standard Recipe")

        try:
            import kepware_gateway
            if kepware_gateway.opc_mgr.connected:
                client = await kepware_gateway.opc_mgr.get_client()
                async with kepware_gateway.opc_mgr.lock:
                    from asyncua import ua
                    var_temp_sp = client.get_node("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Temperature_SP")
                    var_weight_sp = client.get_node("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Weight_SP")
                    await var_temp_sp.write_value(ua.DataValue(ua.Variant(temp_sp, ua.VariantType.Float)))
                    await var_weight_sp.write_value(ua.DataValue(ua.Variant(weight_sp, ua.VariantType.Float)))
            
            import historian
            def _log_deviations():
                historian.log_setpoint_deviation("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Temperature_SP", temp_sp, 138.0)
                historian.log_setpoint_deviation("ns=2;s=Siemens.Line1.Weight_SP", weight_sp, 8500.0)
            asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(_log_deviations))

            with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
                with conn.cursor() as cur:
                    cur.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO dbo.UserActionLog (UserName, UserRole, ActionName, Detail, ActionTime)
                        VALUES (?, 'Operator', 'AI_SETPOINTS_DOWNLINK', ?, GETDATE())
                    """, (user, f"Authorized AI Setpoints: {rcp_name} -> Temp SP: {temp_sp}Â°C, Weight SP: {weight_sp}kg (Order: {order_code})"))
                    conn.commit()

            return {
                "status": "success",
                "message": f"ÄĂ£ náº¡p thĂ nh cĂ´ng thĂ´ng sá»‘ cĂ´ng thá»©c xuá»‘ng PLC: Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™ SP = {temp_sp}Â°C, Khá»‘i lÆ°á»£ng SP = {weight_sp}kg.",
                "details": {"temperature_sp": temp_sp, "weight_sp": weight_sp, "order_code": order_code}
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error executing setpoints downlink: {e}")
            return {
                "status": "partial_success",
                "message": f"ÄĂ£ ghi nháº­n yĂªu cáº§u náº¡p thĂ´ng sá»‘ (MĂ´ phá»ng bĂ n giao SCADA: {str(e)}).",
                "details": payload
            }

    # 2. Action: EXECUTE_BATCH_COMMAND
    elif action_type == "EXECUTE_BATCH_COMMAND":
        command = str(payload.get("command", "START")).upper()
        order_code = payload.get("order_code", "WO_CURRENT")

        try:
            import kepware_gateway
            req_cmd = kepware_gateway.BatchCommand(command=command)
            await kepware_gateway.write_command(req_cmd)

            with _get_sql_connection() as conn:
                with conn.cursor() as cur:
                    cur.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO dbo.UserActionLog (UserName, UserRole, ActionName, Detail, ActionTime)
                        VALUES (?, 'Operator', 'AI_BATCH_COMMAND', ?, GETDATE())
                    """, (user, f"Authorized AI Batch Command: [{command}] for Order [{order_code}]"))
                    conn.commit()

            return {
                "status": "success",
                "message": f"Lá»‡nh Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn ISA-88 [{command}] Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c truyá»n xuá»‘ng tráº¡m PLC thĂ nh cĂ´ng.",
                "details": {"command": command, "order_code": order_code}
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error executing batch command: {e}")
            return {
                "status": "partial_success",
                "message": f"Lá»‡nh [{command}] Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xĂ¡c nháº­n (Tráº¡ng thĂ¡i mĂ´ phá»ng: {str(e)}).",
                "details": payload
            }

    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"HĂ nh Ä‘á»™ng khĂ´ng há»£p lá»‡: {action_type}")

========================================
FILE: D:\MES\backend\batch_engine.py
========================================
"""
/* Hallmark Â· component: DualRunBatchEngine Â· register: industrial-workbench
 * standards: ISA-88 Batch Control / ISA-95 MOM / ISA-101 HMI
 * strict: Central OPC UA Gateway Session Reuse (Port 49320)
 */

Dual-Run SIMIT Digital Twin Benchmarking and Real-Time Deviation Engine.
Orchestrates parallel or isolated batch execution across:
  - Process Cell 1 (Physical Siemens S7-1500 PLC)
  - Process Cell 2 (Siemens SIMIT Virtual Digital Twin)
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List, Tuple
from datetime import datetime

logger = logging.getLogger("DualRunBatchEngine")

# Ensure backend directory in sys.path
backend_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if backend_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, backend_dir)

# ============================================================================
# STATE REPOSITORY (THREAD-SAFE SINGLETON)
# ============================================================================

class DualRunBatchState:
    def __init__(self):
        # 3-Way Execution Mode:
        # 'mode1_physical': Process Cell 1 (Physical S7-1500 Hardware Only)
        # 'mode2_simit': Process Cell 2 (SIMIT Digital Twin Only)
        # 'mode3_dual': Dual-Run Benchmark (Physical + SIMIT Twin Synchronized)
        self.execution_mode: str = "mode3_dual"
        
        # Current active batch info
        self.order_code: Optional[str] = None
        self.recipe_name: Optional[str] = "Fresh Pasteurized Milk"
        self.target_volume: float = 1000.0
        self.batch_state: str = "IDLE"  # IDLE, DOWNLOADING, READY, RUNNING, COMPLETED, HELD, STOPPED
        
        # Setpoints cache
        self.recipe_setpoints: Dict[str, float] = {
            "milk_sp": 700.0,
            "sugar_sp": 200.0,
            "additive_sp": 100.0,
            "heating_sp": 138.0,
            "cooling_sp": 4.0,
            "agitator_sp": 1200.0
        }
        
        # Timestamps for benchmarking
        self.t_start_cell1: Optional[float] = None
        self.t_start_cell2: Optional[float] = None
        self.t_end_cell1: Optional[float] = None
        self.t_end_cell2: Optional[float] = None
        
        # Handshake & Acknowledgment flags
        self.cell1_ack: bool = False
        self.cell2_ack: bool = False
        
        # Independent Completion Flags
        self.cell1_complete: bool = False
        self.cell2_complete: bool = False
        
        # State IDs (ISA-88 code 0..11)
        self.cell1_state_id: int = 0
        self.cell2_state_id: int = 0
        
        # Active procedural phase
        self.cell1_current_phase: str = "PH_IDLE"
        self.cell2_current_phase: str = "PH_IDLE"

    def reset(self):
        self.order_code = None
        self.batch_state = "IDLE"
        self.t_start_cell1 = None
        self.t_start_cell2 = None
        self.t_end_cell1 = None
        self.t_end_cell2 = None
        self.cell1_ack = False
        self.cell2_ack = False
        self.cell1_complete = False
        self.cell2_complete = False
        self.cell1_state_id = 0
        self.cell2_state_id = 0
        self.cell1_current_phase = "PH_IDLE"
        self.cell2_current_phase = "PH_IDLE"

state = DualRunBatchState()

# ============================================================================
# GATEWAY TAG UTILITIES (STRICT CENTRAL SESSION REUSE)
# ============================================================================

def get_kepware_gateway():
    """Dynamically imports kepware_gateway singleton without circular dependencies."""
    try:
        import kepware_gateway
        return kepware_gateway
    except ImportError:
        try:
            from . import kepware_gateway
            return kepware_gateway
        except Exception:
            return None

def _safe_float(val: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    """Safely converts arbitrary telemetry values to float, returning None on unparseable/bad values."""
    if val is None:
        return None
    try:
        if isinstance(val, (int, float)):
            return float(val)
        if isinstance(val, bool):
            return 1.0 if val else 0.0
        s = str(val).strip()
        if not s or s.lower() in ("nan", "none", "null", "bad", "--", "undefined"):
            return None
        return float(s)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None

def resolve_operations_tags() -> Tuple[Dict[str, str], Dict[str, str]]:
    """
    Resolves mapped NodeIds for cell1 and cell2_simit from tag_mappings.json.
    Returns: (cell1_tags, cell2_tags)
    Guarded with try...except so missing or corrupted mappings never crash the process.
    """
    try:
        kw = get_kepware_gateway()
        if not kw or not hasattr(kw, "load_modular_tag_mappings"):
            return {}, {}
        modular = kw.load_modular_tag_mappings()
        if not isinstance(modular, dict):
            return {}, {}
        ops = modular.get("operations", {})
        if not isinstance(ops, dict):
            return {}, {}
        
        def _extract_tag_dict(section_data: Any) -> Dict[str, str]:
            res: Dict[str, str] = {}
            if isinstance(section_data, dict):
                for k, v in section_data.items():
                    if isinstance(v, str):
                        nid = v.strip()
                        if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                            res[k] = nid
                    elif isinstance(v, dict):
                        nid = str(v.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                        if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                            res[k] = nid
            return res

        cell1_tags = _extract_tag_dict(ops.get("cell1", {}))
        cell2_tags = _extract_tag_dict(ops.get("cell2_simit", {}))

        # Aliases / fallbacks for Cell 1 if legacy top-level keys used
        fallback_map_cell1 = {
            "Heating_SP": ["uht_temp_sp", "pc1_uht_temp_sp"],
            "Heating_PV": ["uht_temp_pv", "pc1_uht_temp_pv"],
            "Cooling_SP": ["cooling_temp_sp"],
            "Cooling_PV": ["cooling_temp_pv"],
            "Agitator_SP": ["agitator_speed_sp"],
            "Agitator_PV": ["agitator_speed_pv"],
            "CMD_Start": ["batch_start_cmd"],
            "State_ID": ["pc1_batch_state", "batch_state"],
            "Flag_Complete": ["batch_complete_flag"],
        }
        for target_k, aliases in fallback_map_cell1.items():
            if target_k not in cell1_tags:
                for al in aliases:
                    v = ops.get(al)
                    if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() and v.strip() != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                        cell1_tags[target_k] = v.strip()
                        break
                    elif isinstance(v, dict):
                        nid = str(v.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                        if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                            cell1_tags[target_k] = nid
                            break

        # Aliases / fallbacks for Cell 2 (SIMIT)
        fallback_map_cell2 = {
            "Heating_SP": ["pc2_simit_uht_temp_sp"],
            "Heating_PV": ["pc2_simit_uht_temp_pv"],
            "State_ID": ["pc2_simit_batch_state"],
        }
        for target_k, aliases in fallback_map_cell2.items():
            if target_k not in cell2_tags:
                for al in aliases:
                    v = ops.get(al)
                    if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() and v.strip() != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                        cell2_tags[target_k] = v.strip()
                        break
                    elif isinstance(v, dict):
                        nid = str(v.get("node_id", "")).strip()
                        if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                            cell2_tags[target_k] = nid
                            break

        return cell1_tags, cell2_tags
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[resolve_operations_tags] Warning resolving tag mappings: {exc}")
        return {}, {}

def read_tag_from_cache(node_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
    """
    Sub-millisecond read strictly from central in-memory cache latest_cache["tags"].
    Zero socket port creation, zero asyncua Client instances.
    """
    try:
        if not node_id:
            return None
        kw = get_kepware_gateway()
        if not kw or not hasattr(kw, "latest_cache"):
            return None
        cache = kw.latest_cache.get("tags", {})
        clean_nid = node_id.replace("ns=2;s=", "").strip()
        
        entry = cache.get(node_id) or cache.get(clean_nid) or cache.get(f"ns=2;s={clean_nid}")
        if entry and entry.get("value") is not None:
            return entry.get("value")
        return None
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug(f"[read_tag_from_cache] Error reading {node_id}: {exc}")
        return None

async def write_tag_central(node_id: str, value: Any, data_type: str = "Float") -> bool:
    """
    Writes tag through existing central OPC UA session via kepware_gateway.write_opc_tag.
    Zero new sockets, strict reuse of opc_mgr connection.
    """
    if not node_id:
        return False
    kw = get_kepware_gateway()
    if not kw or not hasattr(kw, "write_opc_tag"):
        return False
    try:
        await kw.write_opc_tag(node_id, value, data_type)
        return True
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[write_tag_central] OPC UA write warning for {node_id}: {exc}")
        return False

# ============================================================================
# RECIPE DOWNLOAD ENGINE (ISA-88 PARAMETER DOWNLINK)
# ============================================================================

async def download_recipe(
    order_code: str,
    execution_mode: str = "mode3_dual",
    recipe_name: str = "Fresh Pasteurized Milk",
    target_volume: float = 1000.0,
    setpoints: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Executes ISA-88 Recipe Parameter Downlink:
    - Mode 1: Writes SP setpoints to operations.cell1 tags only.
    - Mode 2: Writes SP setpoints to operations.cell2_simit tags only.
    - Mode 3: Concurrently writes SP setpoints to BOTH Cell 1 and Cell 2 tags.
    - Evaluates Ack_Recipe_Loaded handshakes.
    """
    state.order_code = order_code
    state.execution_mode = execution_mode
    state.recipe_name = recipe_name
    state.target_volume = target_volume
    state.batch_state = "DOWNLOADING"
    
    if setpoints:
        state.recipe_setpoints.update(setpoints)
    else:
        # Default milk standard formula
        state.recipe_setpoints = {
            "milk_sp": round(target_volume * 0.70, 2),
            "sugar_sp": round(target_volume * 0.20, 2),
            "additive_sp": round(target_volume * 0.10, 2),
            "heating_sp": 138.0,
            "cooling_sp": 4.0,
            "agitator_sp": 1200.0
        }

    cell1_tags, cell2_tags = resolve_operations_tags()
    written_tags: List[str] = []
    
    # Mapping of setpoint keys to tag keys and types
    sp_config = [
        ("milk_sp", "Milk_SP", "Float"),
        ("sugar_sp", "Sugar_SP", "Float"),
        ("additive_sp", "Additive_SP", "Float"),
        ("heating_sp", "Heating_SP", "Float"),
        ("cooling_sp", "Cooling_SP", "Float"),
        ("agitator_sp", "Agitator_SP", "Float"),
    ]

    target_cells = []
    if execution_mode in ("mode1_physical", "mode3_dual"):
        target_cells.append(("cell1", cell1_tags))
    if execution_mode in ("mode2_simit", "mode3_dual"):
        target_cells.append(("cell2_simit", cell2_tags))

    write_tasks = []
    for cell_label, tags in target_cells:
        for sp_key, tag_key, dtype in sp_config:
            val = state.recipe_setpoints.get(sp_key, 0.0)
            node_id = tags.get(tag_key)
            if node_id:
                write_tasks.append(write_tag_central(node_id, val, dtype))
                written_tags.append(f"{cell_label}.{tag_key} -> {val}")

    if write_tasks:
        await asyncio.gather(*write_tasks, return_exceptions=True)

    # Handshake Acknowledgment evaluation
    # In live physical plant, check Ack_Recipe_Loaded; if unmapped or simulated, auto-acknowledge
    cell1_ack = False
    cell2_ack = False

    if "cell1" in [c[0] for c in target_cells]:
        ack_node_1 = cell1_tags.get("Ack_Recipe_Loaded")
        if ack_node_1:
            val = read_tag_from_cache(ack_node_1)
            cell1_ack = bool(val) if val is not None else True
        else:
            cell1_ack = True  # Simulated ack fallback when node not yet mapped
        state.cell1_ack = cell1_ack

    if "cell2_simit" in [c[0] for c in target_cells]:
        ack_node_2 = cell2_tags.get("Ack_Recipe_Loaded")
        if ack_node_2:
            val = read_tag_from_cache(ack_node_2)
            cell2_ack = bool(val) if val is not None else True
        else:
            cell2_ack = True  # SIMIT virtual twin auto-acknowledges recipe reception
        state.cell2_ack = cell2_ack

    # Determine readiness
    ready_to_start = False
    if execution_mode == "mode1_physical":
        ready_to_start = state.cell1_ack
    elif execution_mode == "mode2_simit":
        ready_to_start = state.cell2_ack
    elif execution_mode == "mode3_dual":
        ready_to_start = state.cell1_ack and state.cell2_ack

    if ready_to_start:
        state.batch_state = "READY"

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "order_code": order_code,
        "execution_mode": execution_mode,
        "recipe_name": recipe_name,
        "written_tags": written_tags,
        "cell1_ack": state.cell1_ack,
        "cell2_ack": state.cell2_ack,
        "ready_to_start": ready_to_start,
        "batch_state": state.batch_state,
        "setpoints": state.recipe_setpoints
    }

# ============================================================================
# BATCH START & 500ms CMD_START PULSE DISPATCHER
# ============================================================================

async def _send_500ms_pulse(node_id: str):
    """Sends CMD_Start = True for 500ms, then sets CMD_Start = False."""
    try:
        await write_tag_central(node_id, True, "Boolean")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
        await write_tag_central(node_id, False, "Boolean")
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[_send_500ms_pulse] Failed to send pulse to {node_id}: {exc}")

async def start_batch(order_code: Optional[str] = None, execution_mode: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Triggers batch start across active cells:
    1. Emits 500ms pulse on CMD_Start tags.
    2. Records start timestamps: t_start_cell1 and t_start_cell2.
    3. Resets completion trackers.
    """
    mode = execution_mode or state.execution_mode
    state.execution_mode = mode
    if order_code:
        state.order_code = order_code

    now_ts = time.time()
    cell1_tags, cell2_tags = resolve_operations_tags()
    pulse_tasks = []

    # Reset completion flags
    state.cell1_complete = False
    state.cell2_complete = False
    state.t_end_cell1 = None
    state.t_end_cell2 = None

    if mode in ("mode1_physical", "mode3_dual"):
        state.t_start_cell1 = now_ts
        cmd_node_1 = cell1_tags.get("CMD_Start")
        if cmd_node_1:
            pulse_tasks.append(_send_500ms_pulse(cmd_node_1))
        # Update State_ID = 1 (RUNNING)
        state_node_1 = cell1_tags.get("State_ID")
        if state_node_1:
            pulse_tasks.append(write_tag_central(state_node_1, 1, "Int16"))

    if mode in ("mode2_simit", "mode3_dual"):
        state.t_start_cell2 = now_ts
        cmd_node_2 = cell2_tags.get("CMD_Start")
        if cmd_node_2:
            pulse_tasks.append(_send_500ms_pulse(cmd_node_2))
        # Update SIMIT State_ID = 1 (RUNNING)
        state_node_2 = cell2_tags.get("State_ID")
        if state_node_2:
            pulse_tasks.append(write_tag_central(state_node_2, 1, "Int16"))

    if pulse_tasks:
        asyncio.create_task(asyncio.gather(*pulse_tasks, return_exceptions=True))

    state.batch_state = "RUNNING"

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "batch_state": state.batch_state,
        "execution_mode": mode,
        "order_code": state.order_code,
        "t_start_cell1": state.t_start_cell1,
        "t_start_cell2": state.t_start_cell2,
    }

# ============================================================================
# INDEPENDENT COMPLETION EVALUATION
# ============================================================================

def evaluate_completion(simulated_cell1: Optional[bool] = None, simulated_cell2: Optional[bool] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Checks independent completion flags:
    - Cell 1 completes when Flag_Complete == True -> records t_end_cell1.
    - Cell 2 completes when Flag_Complete == True -> records t_end_cell2.
    - In Dual-Run mode: Overall order status only finalizes when Physical Hardware reports complete.
    """
    now_ts = time.time()
    try:
        cell1_tags, cell2_tags = resolve_operations_tags()

        # 1. Evaluate Cell 1
        if state.execution_mode in ("mode1_physical", "mode3_dual") and not state.cell1_complete:
            flag_node_1 = cell1_tags.get("Flag_Complete")
            is_done = False
            if simulated_cell1 is True:
                is_done = True
            elif flag_node_1:
                val = read_tag_from_cache(flag_node_1)
                is_done = bool(val) if val is not None else False
            
            if is_done:
                state.cell1_complete = True
                state.t_end_cell1 = now_ts
                state.cell1_state_id = 11  # COMPLETE

        # 2. Evaluate Cell 2 (SIMIT Twin)
        if state.execution_mode in ("mode2_simit", "mode3_dual") and not state.cell2_complete:
            flag_node_2 = cell2_tags.get("Flag_Complete")
            is_done = False
            if simulated_cell2 is True:
                is_done = True
            elif flag_node_2:
                val = read_tag_from_cache(flag_node_2)
                is_done = bool(val) if val is not None else False
            
            if is_done:
                state.cell2_complete = True
                state.t_end_cell2 = now_ts
                state.cell2_state_id = 11  # COMPLETE

        # 3. Overall Order Finalization:
        # Mode 1: Finalizes when Cell 1 completes
        # Mode 2: Finalizes when Cell 2 completes
        # Mode 3 (Dual-Run): Finalizes strictly when Physical Hardware (Cell 1) completes
        order_finalized = False
        if state.execution_mode == "mode1_physical":
            order_finalized = state.cell1_complete
        elif state.execution_mode == "mode2_simit":
            order_finalized = state.cell2_complete
        elif state.execution_mode == "mode3_dual":
            order_finalized = state.cell1_complete

        if order_finalized and state.batch_state == "RUNNING":
            state.batch_state = "COMPLETED"

        def _format_dur(start: Optional[float], end: Optional[float]) -> str:
            if not start:
                return "--"
            e = end if end else now_ts
            diff = max(0, int(e - start))
            mins, secs = divmod(diff, 60)
            return f"{mins:02d}m{secs:02d}s"

        return {
            "batch_state": state.batch_state,
            "execution_mode": state.execution_mode,
            "cell1_complete": state.cell1_complete,
            "cell2_complete": state.cell2_complete,
            "duration_cell1": _format_dur(state.t_start_cell1, state.t_end_cell1),
            "duration_cell2": _format_dur(state.t_start_cell2, state.t_end_cell2),
            "order_finalized": order_finalized
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[evaluate_completion] Evaluation fallback warning: {exc}")
        return {
            "batch_state": state.batch_state,
            "execution_mode": state.execution_mode,
            "cell1_complete": state.cell1_complete,
            "cell2_complete": state.cell2_complete,
            "duration_cell1": "--",
            "duration_cell2": "--",
            "order_finalized": False
        }

# ============================================================================
# DIGITAL TWIN DEVIATION MATRIX & FIDELITY INDEX ENGINE
# ============================================================================

def get_deviation_matrix() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes real-time telemetry matrix comparing:
      - Master Recipe Setpoints (SP)
      - Physical Hardware Live Process Values (Cell 1 PV)
      - SIMIT Virtual Twin Live Process Values (Cell 2 PV)
      - Absolute Deviation Î” = |R - S|
      - Status Badge: MATCHED | DEVIATING | TRACKING | CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº 
      - Fidelity Index (% matched)
      - Cycle Time Real vs Twin: T_Hardware vs T_SIMIT with Î” T = |T_HW - T_SIMIT|
    """
    try:
        evaluate_completion()
        cell1_tags, cell2_tags = resolve_operations_tags()
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[get_deviation_matrix] Completion evaluation warning: {exc}")
        cell1_tags, cell2_tags = {}, {}
    
    # 6 Core ISA-88 Parameters with units and tolerance thresholds
    params_def = [
        {
            "id": "milk",
            "name": "Cáº¥p sá»¯a tÆ°Æ¡i (Milk Dosing)",
            "phase": "PH_SUPPLYMILK",
            "sp_key": "milk_sp",
            "unit": "L",
            "tolerance": 10.0,
            "cell1_pv_tag": "Milk_PV",
            "cell2_pv_tag": "Milk_PV"
        },
        {
            "id": "sugar",
            "name": "Äá»‹nh lÆ°á»£ng Ä‘Æ°á»ng (Sugar Dosing)",
            "phase": "PH_SUPPLYSUGAR",
            "sp_key": "sugar_sp",
            "unit": "kg",
            "tolerance": 2.0,
            "cell1_pv_tag": "Sugar_PV",
            "cell2_pv_tag": "Sugar_PV"
        },
        {
            "id": "additive",
            "name": "Vi cháº¥t dinh dÆ°á»¡ng (Additive)",
            "phase": "PH_SUPPLYADDITIVE",
            "sp_key": "additive_sp",
            "unit": "L",
            "tolerance": 1.0,
            "cell1_pv_tag": "Additive_PV",
            "cell2_pv_tag": "Additive_PV"
        },
        {
            "id": "heat",
            "name": "Tiá»‡t trĂ¹ng UHT (Sterilization Temp)",
            "phase": "PH_HEAT",
            "sp_key": "heating_sp",
            "unit": "Â°C",
            "tolerance": 1.0,
            "cell1_pv_tag": "Heating_PV",
            "cell2_pv_tag": "Heating_PV"
        },
        {
            "id": "cool",
            "name": "LĂ m láº¡nh vĂ´ trĂ¹ng (Cooling Temp)",
            "phase": "PH_CHILL",
            "sp_key": "cooling_sp",
            "unit": "Â°C",
            "tolerance": 0.5,
            "cell1_pv_tag": "Cooling_PV",
            "cell2_pv_tag": "Cooling_PV"
        },
        {
            "id": "agitator",
            "name": "Tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ cĂ¡nh khuáº¥y (Agitator Speed)",
            "phase": "PH_AGITATE",
            "sp_key": "agitator_sp",
            "unit": "RPM",
            "tolerance": 25.0,
            "cell1_pv_tag": "Agitator_PV",
            "cell2_pv_tag": "Agitator_PV"
        },
    ]

    try:
        matrix_rows = []
        matched_count = 0
        mapped_count = 0

        for p in params_def:
            sp_val = _safe_float(state.recipe_setpoints.get(p["sp_key"])) or 0.0
            c1_node = cell1_tags.get(p["cell1_pv_tag"])
            c2_node = cell2_tags.get(p["cell2_pv_tag"])

            has_c1_map = bool(c1_node)
            has_c2_map = bool(c2_node)

            raw_c1_val = read_tag_from_cache(c1_node) if c1_node else None
            raw_c2_val = read_tag_from_cache(c2_node) if c2_node else None

            pv_hardware: Optional[float] = _safe_float(raw_c1_val)
            pv_simit: Optional[float] = _safe_float(raw_c2_val)

            delta: Optional[float] = None
            status: str = "CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº "

            if has_c1_map and has_c2_map:
                mapped_count += 1
                # If values exist, calculate delta
                if pv_hardware is not None and pv_simit is not None:
                    delta = round(abs(pv_hardware - pv_simit), 2)
                    if state.batch_state == "RUNNING" and abs(pv_hardware - sp_val) > p["tolerance"]:
                        # Active phase is ramping / tracking
                        status = "TRACKING"
                    elif delta <= p["tolerance"]:
                        status = "MATCHED"
                        matched_count += 1
                    else:
                        status = "DEVIATING"
                else:
                    status = "TRACKING" if state.batch_state == "RUNNING" else "CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº "
            elif has_c1_map or has_c2_map:
                # Partially mapped
                status = "CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº "
            else:
                status = "CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº "

            matrix_rows.append({
                "id": p["id"],
                "name": p["name"],
                "phase": p["phase"],
                "unit": p["unit"],
                "sp": sp_val,
                "pv_hardware": pv_hardware,
                "pv_simit": pv_simit,
                "delta": delta,
                "tolerance": p["tolerance"],
                "status": status,
                "c1_mapped": has_c1_map,
                "c2_mapped": has_c2_map
            })

        # Twin Model Fidelity Index: percentage of mapped parameters within tolerance
        fidelity_index: Optional[float] = None
        if mapped_count > 0:
            fidelity_index = round((matched_count / mapped_count) * 100.0, 1)

        # Cycle Times calculation
        now_ts = time.time()
        def _dur_seconds(start: Optional[float], end: Optional[float]) -> Optional[float]:
            if not start:
                return None
            e = end if end else (now_ts if state.batch_state == "RUNNING" else start)
            return max(0.0, e - start)

        t_hw_sec = _dur_seconds(state.t_start_cell1, state.t_end_cell1)
        t_simit_sec = _dur_seconds(state.t_start_cell2, state.t_end_cell2)

        delta_t_sec: Optional[float] = None
        if t_hw_sec is not None and t_simit_sec is not None:
            delta_t_sec = round(abs(t_hw_sec - t_simit_sec), 1)

        def _format_time_sec(sec: Optional[float]) -> str:
            if sec is None:
                return "--"
            s = int(sec)
            mins, secs = divmod(s, 60)
            return f"{mins:02d}m{secs:02d}s"

        return {
            "execution_mode": state.execution_mode,
            "batch_state": state.batch_state,
            "order_code": state.order_code,
            "recipe_name": state.recipe_name,
            "matrix": matrix_rows,
            "benchmark_summary": {
                "mapped_parameters": mapped_count,
                "matched_parameters": matched_count,
                "total_parameters": len(params_def),
                "fidelity_index": fidelity_index,
                "t_hardware_sec": t_hw_sec,
                "t_hardware_formatted": _format_time_sec(t_hw_sec),
                "t_simit_sec": t_simit_sec,
                "t_simit_formatted": _format_time_sec(t_simit_sec),
                "delta_t_sec": delta_t_sec,
                "delta_t_formatted": _format_time_sec(delta_t_sec),
                "cell1_complete": state.cell1_complete,
                "cell2_complete": state.cell2_complete
            }
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[get_deviation_matrix] Deviation calculation error: {exc}")
        return {
            "execution_mode": state.execution_mode,
            "batch_state": state.batch_state,
            "order_code": state.order_code,
            "recipe_name": state.recipe_name,
            "matrix": [],
            "benchmark_summary": None
        }

FILE: D:\MES\backend\db_connector.py

"""
Database Connector Module for MES Production System
Connects to Microsoft SQL Server instance localhost\\WINCC (or .\\WINCC)
Database: MES_Milk_Production
Windows Authentication (Trusted_Connection=yes)
ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server
"""

import pyodbc
import time
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any, Tuple

logger = logging.getLogger("DB_Connector")

# Candidate SQL Server instances
CANDIDATE_SERVERS = [
    r"localhost\WINCC",
    r".\WINCC",
    "localhost",
    "."
]

# Supported ODBC Drivers
DRIVERS = [
    "{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}",
    "{ODBC Driver 18 for SQL Server}",
    "{SQL Server Native Client 11.0}",
    "{SQL Server}"
]

DATABASE_NAME = "MES_Milk_Production"

# Cached working parameters
active_server: Optional[str] = None
active_driver: Optional[str] = None

# Circuit Breaker state
db_offline = False
db_last_attempt_time = 0.0
DB_RETRY_COOLDOWN = 5.0  # Retry cooldown in seconds


def get_connection(timeout: int = 3) -> Optional[pyodbc.Connection]:
    """
    Connects to Microsoft SQL Server using candidate instances and drivers.
    Caches working instance for sub-10ms subsequent queries.
    Gracefully handles reconnects if the SQL service restarts.
    """
    global active_server, active_driver, db_offline, db_last_attempt_time

    now = time.time()
    if db_offline and (now - db_last_attempt_time < DB_RETRY_COOLDOWN):
        return None

    db_last_attempt_time = now

    # 1. Try cached configuration first
    if active_server and active_driver:
        try:
            cs = (
                f"DRIVER={active_driver};"
                f"SERVER={active_server};"
                f"DATABASE={DATABASE_NAME};"
                f"Trusted_Connection=yes;"
                f"TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
                f"Connection Timeout={timeout};"
            )
            conn = pyodbc.connect(cs, timeout=timeout)
            db_offline = False
            return conn
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"Cached connection to {active_server} failed, probing alternatives: {e}")
            active_server = None
            active_driver = None

    # 2. Probe candidate servers and drivers
    for s in CANDIDATE_SERVERS:
        for d in DRIVERS:
            try:
                cs = (
                    f"DRIVER={d};"
                    f"SERVER={s};"
                    f"DATABASE={DATABASE_NAME};"
                    f"Trusted_Connection=yes;"
                    f"TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
                    f"Connection Timeout={timeout};"
                )
                conn = pyodbc.connect(cs, timeout=timeout)
                active_server = s
                active_driver = d
                db_offline = False
                logger.info(f"Connected to SQL Server [{s}] with driver [{d}] on database [{DATABASE_NAME}]")
                return conn
            except Exception:
                continue

    db_offline = True
    return None


# Primary connection string format specifically required:
# "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};SERVER=localhost\WINCC;DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;Trusted_Connection=yes;TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
WINCC_CONN_STR = (
    "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
    "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
    "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
    "Connection Timeout=2;"
)


def verify_mes_database_status() -> bool:
    """
    Returns True ONLY if:
    1. SQL Server is reachable.
    2. Database 'MES_Milk_Production' can be queried with 'SELECT 1'.
    """
    try:
        conn = get_connection(timeout=2)
        if not conn:
            return False
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT 1")
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        conn.close()
        return bool(row and row[0] == 1)
    except Exception:
        return False


def check_db_status() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Health Check endpoint utility:
    Executes SELECT 1 test query.
    Returns: { "connected": true, "database": "MES_Milk_Production", "timestamp": "...", "latency_ms": ... }
    """
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        conn = get_connection(timeout=2)
        if not conn:
            return {
                "connected": False,
                "status": "disconnected",
                "database": DATABASE_NAME,
                "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
                "latency_ms": 0,
                "error": f"Database [{DATABASE_NAME}] unreachable on candidate servers"
            }
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT 1")
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        conn.close()
        latency = round((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000, 2)
        if row and row[0] == 1:
            return {
                "connected": True,
                "status": "connected",
                "database": DATABASE_NAME,
                "server": active_server or r"localhost\WINCC",
                "driver": active_driver or "{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}",
                "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
                "latency_ms": latency
            }
        else:
            return {
                "connected": False,
                "status": "disconnected",
                "database": DATABASE_NAME,
                "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
                "latency_ms": latency,
                "error": "Query verification failed"
            }
    except Exception as e:
        latency = round((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000, 2)
        return {
            "connected": False,
            "status": "disconnected",
            "database": DATABASE_NAME,
            "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
            "latency_ms": latency,
            "error": str(e)
        }


def execute_query(query: str, params: Optional[tuple] = None, timeout: int = 3) -> Any:
    """
    Executes a SQL query.
    Returns list of dicts for SELECT queries.
    Commits and returns True for INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE queries.
    """
    conn = get_connection(timeout=timeout)
    if not conn:
        raise ConnectionError("Microsoft SQL Server database is offline")
    try:
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        if params:
            cursor.execute(query, params)
        else:
            cursor.execute(query)

        stripped = query.strip().upper()
        if stripped.startswith("SELECT") or "OUTPUT" in stripped:
            if not cursor.description:
                conn.close()
                return []
            columns = [column[0] for column in cursor.description]
            results = []
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                results.append(dict(zip(columns, row)))
            conn.close()
            return results
        else:
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            return True
    except Exception as e:
        if conn:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        raise e


def execute_transaction(operations: List[Tuple[str, Optional[tuple]]], timeout: int = 5) -> bool:
    """
    Executes multiple SQL operations inside an atomic transaction.
    Rolls back immediately if any statement fails.
    """
    conn = get_connection(timeout=timeout)
    if not conn:
        raise ConnectionError("Microsoft SQL Server database is offline")
    try:
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        for q, p in operations:
            if p:
                cursor.execute(q, p)
            else:
                cursor.execute(q)
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        if conn:
            try:
                conn.rollback()
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        raise e


def log_user_action(user_name: str, user_role: str, action_name: str, detail: str) -> bool:
    """Writes an interaction into dbo.UserActionLog."""
    try:
        query = """
        INSERT INTO UserActionLog (UserName, UserRole, ActionName, Detail, ActionTime)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE())
        """
        return execute_query(query, (user_name, user_role, action_name, detail), timeout=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to log user action: {e}")
        return False


def log_plc_datalog(order_code: str, tag_name: str, value: float, unit: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
    """Writes a telemetry snapshot into dbo.PLC_Datalog."""
    try:
        tag_unit = unit or ""
        query = """
        INSERT INTO PLC_Datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE())
        """
        return execute_query(query, (order_code, tag_name, float(value), tag_unit), timeout=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Failed to log PLC datalog: {e}")
        return False


def update_tank_stock(
    material_code: str,
    quantity: Optional[float] = None,
    current_stock: Optional[float] = None,
    tank_id: Optional[str] = None,
    mode: str = "add",
    material_name: Optional[str] = None,
    remark: str = "",
    user: str = "Warehouse_Officer"
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Directly updates physical tank inventory in dbo.Material_Stock.
    Strictly executes parameterized UPDATE and calls conn.commit() immediately.
    """
    conn = get_connection(timeout=3)
    if not conn:
        raise ConnectionError("Microsoft SQL Server database is offline")
    
    conn.autocommit = False
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    try:
        mat = (material_code or "").strip().upper()
        tank = (tank_id or "").strip().upper()

        if mode == "set" or (current_stock is not None and quantity is None):
            target_stock = float(current_stock if current_stock is not None else quantity)
            cursor.execute("""
                UPDATE dbo.Material_Stock
                SET CurrentStock = ?,
                    Last_Update = GETDATE()
                WHERE UPPER(Material_Code) = ? OR UPPER(AssignedTank) = ?;
            """, (target_stock, mat, tank))
        else:
            add_qty = float(quantity if quantity is not None else 0.0)
            cursor.execute("""
                UPDATE dbo.Material_Stock
                SET CurrentStock = CurrentStock + ?,
                    Total_Quantity = Total_Quantity + ?,
                    Last_Update = GETDATE()
                WHERE UPPER(Material_Code) = ? OR UPPER(AssignedTank) = ?;
            """, (add_qty, add_qty, mat, tank))
            
            if add_qty > 0:
                m_name = material_name or mat
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO dbo.Material_Input (Material_Code, Material_Name, Quantity, Input_Time, Remark)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, GETDATE(), ?);
                """, (mat, m_name, add_qty, remark))

        # Explicitly call conn.commit() immediately after execution to guarantee persistence to disk
        conn.commit()

        # Fetch updated record
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT Material_Code, Material_Name, AssignedTank, CurrentStock, TankCapacity, Unit, MinSafetyThreshold, Last_Update
            FROM dbo.Material_Stock
            WHERE UPPER(Material_Code) = ? OR UPPER(AssignedTank) = ?;
        """, (mat, tank))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        res = {}
        if row:
            res = {
                "material_code": row[0],
                "material_name": row[1],
                "assigned_tank": row[2],
                "current_stock": float(row[3] or 0.0),
                "tank_capacity": float(row[4] or 5.0),
                "unit": row[5] or "L",
                "min_threshold": float(row[6] or 0.5),
                "last_update": str(row[7] or "")
            }
        cursor.close()
        conn.close()
        return res
    except Exception as e:
        if conn:
            try:
                conn.rollback()
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        raise e

Single Source of Truth for Master Data, Operations, Inventory, Equipment,
ISA-18.2 Alarms, EBR Reports, and FDA 21 CFR Part 11 Audit Trail & E-Signatures.

if backend_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, backend_dir)

# Primary connection string for physical SQL Server instance
SQL_CONN_STR = (
    "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
    "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
    "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
    "Connection Timeout=3;"
)

@contextmanager
def get_fresh_db_connection(timeout: int = 3):
    """
    Acquires a fresh, isolated pyodbc connection to SQL Server.
    Guarantees that transactions are committed on success or rolled back on error,
    and physical connection socket is explicitly closed upon exit to prevent stale connections.
    """
    conn = None
    try:
        conn = pyodbc.connect(SQL_CONN_STR, timeout=timeout)
    except Exception as e:
        for alt in [r".\WINCC", "localhost", r"localhost\SQLEXPRESS", "."]:
            try:
                alt_cs = f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}};SERVER={alt};DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;Trusted_Connection=yes;TrustServerCertificate=yes;Connection Timeout={timeout};"
                conn = pyodbc.connect(alt_cs, timeout=timeout)
                break
            except Exception:
                continue
        if not conn:
            conn = db_connector.get_connection(timeout=timeout)
        if not conn:
            raise ConnectionError(f"Cannot establish fresh connection to SQL Server: {e}")

    try:
        with conn:
            yield conn
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass

    quality_status: Optional[str] = "CONFORMING"
    disposition_action: Optional[str] = "RELEASED"
    sign_off_by: Optional[str] = "Lead QA Specialist"
    quality_results: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None

class AuditLogPayload(BaseModel):

    role: Optional[str] = "Operator"

    detail: Optional[str] = ""

    kepware_channel_device: Optional[str] = None

# ============================================================================
# 1. HEALTH CHECK & DATABASE HANDSHAKE
# ============================================================================

@router.get("/api/db/status")
@router.get("/api/sql/status")
async def get_db_status():
    """
    Heartbeat and health check endpoint for Header / Navbar SQL badge.
    Returns Store-and-Forward buffer status and SQL connection state.
    """
    def _compute_status():
        try:
            base_status = db_connector.check_db_status()
            sql_connected = bool(base_status.get("connected", False))
            pending_count = db_engine.get_pending_buffer_count() if db_engine else 0
            if db_engine:
                db_engine.is_sql_online = sql_connected

            mode = "ONLINE" if (sql_connected and pending_count == 0) else ("SYNCING" if sql_connected else "BUFFERING")

            base_status.update({
                "sql_connected": sql_connected,
                "mode": mode,
                "buffered_count": pending_count,
                "connected": sql_connected,
                "status": "connected" if sql_connected else "disconnected",
            })
            return base_status
        except Exception as exc:
            return {
                "connected": False,
                "sql_connected": False,
                "status": "disconnected",
                "mode": "BUFFERING",
                "buffered_count": 0,
                "error": str(exc),
                "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
            }

    return await asyncio.to_thread(_compute_status)


@router.get("/api/db/test")
@router.post("/api/db/test")
@router.get("/api/sql/test")
@router.post("/api/sql/test")
async def test_db_handshake(request: Request = None, payload: Optional[TestDbPayload] = None):
    r"""
    Tests SQL Server connection handshake to the requested host and database.
    Wrap the connection attempt inside a comprehensive try...except block.
    NEVER let an unhandled exception bubble up as a 500 Internal Server Error.
    Exact connection string format:
      DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};SERVER=localhost\WINCC;DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;Trusted_Connection=yes;
    """
    try:
        body: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        if payload is not None:
            body = payload.dict() if hasattr(payload, "dict") else dict(payload)
        elif request is not None:
            try:
                body = await request.json()
            except Exception:
                body = {}

        target_server = (
            body.get("server_name")
            or body.get("serverName")
            or r"localhost\WINCC"
        )
        target_db = (
            body.get("database_name")
            or body.get("databaseName")
            or "MES_Milk_Production"
        )
        auth_mode = str(body.get("auth_mode") or body.get("authMode") or "windows").lower()
        username = body.get("username") or ""
        password = body.get("password") or ""

        if auth_mode == "sql" and username:
            conn_str = (
                f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}};"
                f"SERVER={target_server};"
                f"DATABASE={target_db};"
                f"UID={username};"
                f"PWD={password};"
                f"TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
                f"Connection Timeout=3;"
            )
        else:
            conn_str = (
                f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}};"
                f"SERVER={target_server};"
                f"DATABASE={target_db};"
                f"Trusted_Connection=yes;"
            )

        def _probe():
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            conn = pyodbc.connect(conn_str, timeout=3)
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("SELECT 1")
            cur.fetchone()
            lat = round((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000, 1)
            conn.close()
            return lat

        try:
            latency_ms = await asyncio.to_thread(_probe)
            return {
                "success": True,
                "connected": True,
                "ok": True,
                "status": "connected",
                "server": target_server,
                "database": target_db,
                "version": "SQL SERVER 2022",
                "latency_ms": latency_ms,
                "message": "Connected successfully"
            }
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"SQL probe error to {target_server}: {e}")
            return {
                "success": False,
                "connected": False,
                "ok": False,
                "status": "disconnected",
                "server": target_server,
                "database": target_db,
                "message": str(e),
                "error": str(e)
            }
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Unexpected error in /api/db/test: {e}")
        return {
            "success": False,
            "connected": False,
            "ok": False,
            "status": "disconnected",
            "message": str(e),
            "error": str(e)
        }

@router.get("/api/customers")
@router.get("/api/master/customers")
async def get_customers():
    """
    Reads all customers from dbo.Customers.
    Returns both SQL PascalCase and frontend camelCase field aliases.
    """
    def _execute():
        with get_fresh_db_connection(timeout=3) as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("""
                    SELECT CustomerID, CustomerCode, CustomerName, ContactEmail, PhoneNumber,
                           OrderPriority, FacilityAddress, IsActive
                    FROM Customers
                    ORDER BY CustomerID ASC
                """)
                rows = cur.fetchall()
                data = []
                for r in rows:
                    cid = r[0]
                    code = r[1] or f"CUS-{cid}"
                    name = r[2] or "Customer"
                    email = r[3] or ""
                    phone = r[4] or ""
                    priority = r[5] or 2
                    address = r[6] or ""
                    is_active = bool(r[7]) if r[7] is not None else True
                    status_str = "Active" if is_active else "Inactive"

                    data.append({
                        # PascalCase
                        "CustomerID": cid,
                        "CustomerCode": code,
                        "CustomerName": name,
                        "ContactEmail": email,
                        "PhoneNumber": phone,
                        "OrderPriority": priority,
                        "FacilityAddress": address,
                        "IsActive": is_active,
                        # snake_case standardized aliases
                        "customer_id": cid,
                        "customer_code": code,
                        "customer_name": name,
                        "contact_email": email,
                        "phone_number": phone,
                        "order_priority": priority,
                        "facility_address": address,
                        "is_active": is_active,
                        # camelCase / aliases
                        "id": cid,
                        "code": code,
                        "name": name,
                        "email": email,
                        "phone": phone,
                        "priority": priority,
                        "address": address,
                        "status": status_str,
                        "activeOrdersCount": 1 if is_active else 0
                    })
                return data

    try:
        data = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute)
        return {"status": "success", "data": data}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error fetching customers: {e}")
        return {"status": "error", "message": str(e), "data": []}


@router.post("/api/customers")
async def create_or_upsert_customer(payload: CustomerCreatePayload):
    """
    Inserts customer into dbo.Customers, or updates if CustomerCode already exists.
    Checks for duplicate code conflicts and returns 400 on error.
    """
    code = (payload.code or payload.CustomerCode or "").strip().upper()
    name = (payload.name or payload.CustomerName or "").strip()
    if not code:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Customer Code is required")
    if not name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Customer Name is required")

    email = (payload.email or payload.ContactEmail or "").strip()
    phone = (payload.phone or payload.PhoneNumber or "").strip()
    priority = int(payload.priority or payload.OrderPriority or 2)
    address = (payload.address or payload.FacilityAddress or "").strip()
    is_active = 1 if (str(payload.status or "").upper() != "INACTIVE" and getattr(payload, 'is_active', True) is not False) else 0

    def _execute():
        with get_fresh_db_connection(timeout=3) as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT CustomerID FROM Customers WHERE CustomerCode = ?", (code,))
                existing = cur.fetchone()

                if existing:
                    cust_id = existing[0]
                    cur.execute("""
                        UPDATE Customers
                        SET CustomerName = ?, ContactEmail = ?, PhoneNumber = ?, OrderPriority = ?, FacilityAddress = ?, IsActive = ?
                        WHERE CustomerID = ?
                    """, (name, email, phone, priority, address, is_active, cust_id))
                    action = "updated"
                else:
                    cur.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO Customers (CustomerCode, CustomerName, ContactEmail, PhoneNumber, OrderPriority, FacilityAddress, IsActive)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """, (code, name, email, phone, priority, address, is_active))
                    cur.execute("SELECT @@IDENTITY")
                    id_row = cur.fetchone()
                    cust_id = int(id_row[0]) if id_row and id_row[0] else None
                    action = "created"

                return {
                    "CustomerID": cust_id,
                    "CustomerCode": code,
                    "CustomerName": name,
                    "ContactEmail": email,
                    "PhoneNumber": phone,
                    "OrderPriority": priority,
                    "FacilityAddress": address,
                    "IsActive": bool(is_active),
                    "action": action,
                    "id": cust_id,
                    "code": code,
                    "name": name,
                    "email": email,
                    "phone": phone,
                    "priority": priority,
                    "address": address,
                    "status": "Active" if is_active else "Inactive"
                }

    try:
        res = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute)
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "message": f"Customer [{code}] {res['action']} successfully",
            "data": res
        }
    except pyodbc.IntegrityError as ie:
        err_msg = str(ie)
        if "2627" in err_msg or "2601" in err_msg or "UQ" in err_msg.upper():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Duplicate customer code")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Database integrity error: {err_msg}")
    except ConnectionError as ce:
        logger.error(f"DB Connection error creating customer: {ce}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Database Connection Failed: {str(ce)}")
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error creating/upserting customer: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))


@router.put("/api/customers/{customer_id}")
async def update_customer(customer_id: int, payload: CustomerUpdatePayload):
    """
    Updates an existing customer by CustomerID in dbo.Customers.
    """
    code = (payload.code or payload.CustomerCode or "").strip().upper() or None
    name = (payload.name or payload.CustomerName or "").strip() or None
    email = payload.email if payload.email is not None else payload.ContactEmail
    phone = payload.phone if payload.phone is not None else payload.PhoneNumber
    priority = payload.priority if payload.priority is not None else payload.OrderPriority
    address = payload.address if payload.address is not None else payload.FacilityAddress

    is_active = None
    if payload.status is not None:
        is_active = 1 if payload.status.strip().upper() != "INACTIVE" else 0
    elif payload.is_active is not None:
        is_active = 1 if payload.is_active else 0
    elif payload.IsActive is not None:
        is_active = 1 if payload.IsActive else 0

    def _execute():
        with get_fresh_db_connection(timeout=3) as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT CustomerID, CustomerCode, CustomerName, ContactEmail, PhoneNumber, OrderPriority, FacilityAddress, IsActive FROM Customers WHERE CustomerID = ?", (customer_id,))
                current = cur.fetchone()
                if not current:
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Customer ID {customer_id} not found")

                curr_cid, curr_code, curr_name, curr_email, curr_phone, curr_priority, curr_address, curr_active = current
                target_code = code if code is not None else curr_code
                target_name = name if name is not None else curr_name
                target_email = email if email is not None else (curr_email or "")
                target_phone = phone if phone is not None else (curr_phone or "")
                target_priority = priority if priority is not None else (curr_priority or 2)
                target_address = address if address is not None else (curr_address or "")
                target_active = is_active if is_active is not None else (1 if curr_active else 0)

                if target_code != curr_code:
                    cur.execute("SELECT CustomerID FROM Customers WHERE CustomerCode = ? AND CustomerID <> ?", (target_code, customer_id))
                    conflict = cur.fetchone()
                    if conflict:
                        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Duplicate customer code")

                cur.execute("""
                    UPDATE Customers
                    SET CustomerCode = ?,
                        CustomerName = ?,
                        ContactEmail = ?,
                        PhoneNumber = ?,
                        OrderPriority = ?,
                        FacilityAddress = ?,
                        IsActive = ?
                    WHERE CustomerID = ?
                """, (target_code, target_name, target_email, target_phone, target_priority, target_address, target_active, customer_id))

                return {
                    "CustomerID": customer_id,
                    "CustomerCode": target_code,
                    "CustomerName": target_name,
                    "ContactEmail": target_email,
                    "PhoneNumber": target_phone,
                    "OrderPriority": target_priority,
                    "FacilityAddress": target_address,
                    "IsActive": bool(target_active),
                    "id": customer_id,
                    "code": target_code,
                    "name": target_name,
                    "email": target_email,
                    "phone": target_phone,
                    "priority": target_priority,
                    "address": target_address,
                    "status": "Active" if target_active else "Inactive"
                }

    try:
        res = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute)
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "message": f"Customer [{res['CustomerCode']}] updated successfully",
            "data": res
        }
    except pyodbc.IntegrityError as ie:
        err_msg = str(ie)
        if "2627" in err_msg or "2601" in err_msg or "UQ" in err_msg.upper():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Duplicate customer code")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Database integrity error: {err_msg}")
    except ConnectionError as ce:
        logger.error(f"DB Connection error updating customer: {ce}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Database Connection Failed: {str(ce)}")
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error updating customer: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))


@router.delete("/api/customers/{customer_id}")
async def delete_customer(customer_id: int):
    """Removes customer by CustomerID from dbo.Customers."""
    def _execute():
        with get_fresh_db_connection(timeout=3) as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT CustomerID, CustomerCode FROM Customers WHERE CustomerID = ?", (customer_id,))
                cust = cur.fetchone()
                if not cust:
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Customer ID {customer_id} not found")
                code = cust[1]
                cur.execute("DELETE FROM Customers WHERE CustomerID = ?", (customer_id,))
                return code

    try:
        code = await asyncio.to_thread(_execute)
        return {"status": "success", "message": f"Customer [{code}] deleted successfully"}
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error deleting customer: {e}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))


# ============================================================================
# 3. MASTER DATA: RECIPES CRUD
# ============================================================================

def _parse_recipe_item(item: Dict[str, Any], default_phase: str = "PH_DOSING") -> Tuple[str, str, str, float, float]:
    phase_id = str(item.get("phaseId") or item.get("phase_id") or default_phase)
    mat_code = str(item.get("material_code") or item.get("material_id") or item.get("materialId") or item.get("tagName") or item.get("id") or "RAW_MILK")
    mat_name = str(item.get("material_name") or item.get("materialName") or item.get("parameterName") or item.get("name") or mat_code)
    
    raw_pct = item.get("percentage") if item.get("percentage") is not None else item.get("targetValue", 0.0)
    try:
        pct = float(raw_pct)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        pct = 0.0

    raw_tol = item.get("tolerance") if item.get("tolerance") is not None else item.get("tolerancePercent", 1.0)
    try:
        if isinstance(raw_tol, str):
            clean_tol = raw_tol.replace("Â±", "").replace("%", "").replace("Â°C", "").strip()
            tol = float(clean_tol) if clean_tol else 1.0
        else:
            tol = float(raw_tol)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        tol = 1.0
    return (phase_id, mat_code, mat_name, pct, tol)
