    try:
        from store_forward_engine import db_engine
    except ImportError:
        db_engine = None


def _json_serial(obj: Any) -> Any:
    """JSON serializer for objects not serializable by default json code"""
    if isinstance(obj, (datetime, date)):
        return obj.isoformat()
    return obj


def _to_snake_case(key: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r'([A-Z]+)([A-Z][a-z])', r'\1_\2', key)
    s = re.sub(r'([a-z0-9])([A-Z])', r'\1_\2', s)
    s = re.sub(r'_+', '_', s)
    return s.lower()


def _format_row_dates(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Helper to convert datetime values in a dict to ISO strings and populate dual-key snake_case."""
    formatted = {}
    for k, v in row.items():
        val = v.isoformat() if isinstance(v, (datetime, date)) else v
        formatted[k] = val
        sk = _to_snake_case(k)
        if sk not in formatted:
            formatted[sk] = val
    return formatted


# ============================================================================
# 1. EQUIPMENT MASTER SERVICE
# ============================================================================

def get_all_equipment() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Retrieves all equipment from dbo.Equipment_Master.
    Computes runtime ratios, health scores, and interlock status.
    """
    sql = """
    SELECT 
        EquipmentID,
        EquipmentCode,
        EquipmentName,
        EquipmentType,
        Location,
        CurrentStatus,
        ISNULL(Runtime_Hours, 0.0) AS Runtime_Hours,
        ISNULL(TotalStarts, 0) AS TotalStarts,
        ISNULL(Maintenance_Limit_Hours, 500.0) AS Maintenance_Limit_Hours,
        ISNULL(Maintenance_Count, 0) AS Maintenance_Count,
        Last_Maintenance_Date,
        Next_Maintenance_Due,
        Created_At,
        CASE WHEN COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Master', 'RatedCurrent') IS NOT NULL THEN ISNULL(RatedCurrent, 10.0) ELSE 10.0 END AS RatedCurrent,
        CASE WHEN COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Master', 'MaxVibrationThreshold') IS NOT NULL THEN ISNULL(MaxVibrationThreshold, 4.5) ELSE 4.5 END AS MaxVibrationThreshold,
        CASE WHEN COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Master', 'MaxTempThreshold') IS NOT NULL THEN ISNULL(MaxTempThreshold, 75.0) ELSE 75.0 END AS MaxTempThreshold,
        CASE WHEN COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Master', 'Notes') IS NOT NULL THEN Notes ELSE '' END AS Notes,
        CASE WHEN COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Master', 'Last_Update') IS NOT NULL THEN Last_Update ELSE Created_At END AS Last_Update
    FROM Equipment_Master
    ORDER BY EquipmentID ASC;
    """
    rows = execute_query(sql)
    results = []
    for r in rows:
        row = _format_row_dates(r)
        runtime = float(row.get("Runtime_Hours", 0.0))
        limit = float(row.get("Maintenance_Limit_Hours", 500.0))
        usage_pct = round((runtime / limit) * 100.0, 1) if limit > 0 else 0.0
        health_ratio = max(0.0, min(100.0, round(100.0 - usage_pct, 1)))
        status = str(row.get("CurrentStatus", "STOP")).upper()
        
        is_interlocked = (
            status in ("MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED", "FAULT", "MAINTENANCE") or
            (limit > 0 and runtime >= limit)
        )

        row["Usage_Percent"] = usage_pct
        row["Health_Ratio"] = health_ratio
        row["Is_Interlocked"] = is_interlocked
        # snake_case standardized aliases
        row["equipment_id"] = row.get("EquipmentID")
        row["equipment_code"] = row.get("EquipmentCode")
        row["equipment_name"] = row.get("EquipmentName")
        row["equipment_type"] = row.get("EquipmentType")
        row["location"] = row.get("Location")
        row["current_status"] = status
        row["runtime_hours"] = runtime
        row["total_starts"] = int(row.get("TotalStarts") or 0)
        row["maintenance_limit_hours"] = limit
        row["maintenance_count"] = int(row.get("Maintenance_Count") or 0)
        row["last_maintenance_date"] = row.get("Last_Maintenance_Date")
        row["next_maintenance_due"] = row.get("Next_Maintenance_Due")
        row["usage_percent"] = usage_pct
        row["health_ratio"] = health_ratio
        row["is_interlocked"] = is_interlocked
        row["rated_current"] = float(row.get("RatedCurrent") or 10.0)
        row["max_vibration"] = float(row.get("MaxVibrationThreshold") or 4.5)
        row["max_temp"] = float(row.get("MaxTempThreshold") or 75.0)
        row["notes"] = row.get("Notes") or ""
        row["last_update"] = row.get("Last_Update") or row.get("Created_At")
        results.append(row)
    return results


def get_equipment_by_id_or_code(identifier: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Lookup equipment by ID or EquipmentCode."""
    if isinstance(identifier, int) or (isinstance(identifier, str) and identifier.isdigit()):
        sql = "SELECT * FROM Equipment_Master WHERE EquipmentID = ?;"
        params = (int(identifier),)
    else:
        sql = "SELECT * FROM Equipment_Master WHERE EquipmentCode = ?;"
        params = (str(identifier).strip(),)
    
    rows = execute_query(sql, params)
    if rows and len(rows) > 0:
        return _format_row_dates(rows[0])
    return None


def update_equipment_status(
    identifier: Any, 
    new_status: str, 
    duration_seconds: int = 0,
    trigger_source: str = "MANUAL_DISPATCH"
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Updates equipment status and records state change event in dbo.Equipment_Status_History.
    Increments TotalStarts if transition is into 'RUNNING'.
    """
    eq = get_equipment_by_id_or_code(identifier)
    if not eq:
        raise ValueError(f"Equipment [{identifier}] not found in database.")

    eq_id = int(eq["EquipmentID"])
    eq_code = eq["EquipmentCode"]
    old_status = str(eq.get("CurrentStatus", "STOP")).strip().upper()
    target_status = str(new_status).strip().upper()

    if old_status == target_status:
        return {"status": "unchanged", "EquipmentID": eq_id, "CurrentStatus": old_status}

    # 1. Insert into Equipment_Status_History
    insert_hist_sql = """
    INSERT INTO Equipment_Status_History (EquipmentID, Old_Status, New_Status, Duration_Seconds, Changed_At)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE());
    """
    
    # 2. Update Equipment_Master
    if target_status == "RUNNING":
        update_eq_sql = """
        UPDATE Equipment_Master
        SET CurrentStatus = ?,
            TotalStarts = ISNULL(TotalStarts, 0) + 1
        WHERE EquipmentID = ?;
        """
    else:
        update_eq_sql = """
        UPDATE Equipment_Master
        SET CurrentStatus = ?
        WHERE EquipmentID = ?;
        """

    ops = [
        (insert_hist_sql, (eq_id, old_status, target_status, int(duration_seconds))),
        (update_eq_sql, (target_status, eq_id))
    ]
    execute_transaction(ops)

    # 3. Log into UserActionLog
    db_connector.log_user_action(
        user_name=trigger_source,
        user_role="OPERATOR",
        action_name="EQUIPMENT_STATUS_CHANGE",
        detail=f"Equipment [{eq_code}] switched status from [{old_status}] to [{target_status}]"
    )

    logger.info(f"Equipment [{eq_code}] transitioned from {old_status} -> {target_status}")
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "EquipmentID": eq_id,
        "EquipmentCode": eq_code,
        "OldStatus": old_status,
        "NewStatus": target_status
    }


# ============================================================================
# 2. EQUIPMENT MAINTENANCE SERVICE
# ============================================================================

def get_maintenance_history(equipment_id: Optional[int] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Retrieves maintenance log history joined with Equipment_Master."""
    if equipment_id:
        sql = """
        SELECT 
            m.Maintenance_ID,
            m.Maintenance_ID AS log_id,
            m.EquipmentID,
            COALESCE(e.EquipmentCode, m.Equipment_Code) AS EquipmentCode,
            COALESCE(e.EquipmentName, m.Equipment_Code) AS EquipmentName,
            COALESCE(m.Ticket_Type, m.Maintenance_Type) AS Ticket_Type,
            m.Maintenance_Type,
            COALESCE(m.Action_Taken, m.Description) AS Action_Taken,
            m.Description,
            m.Technician,
            m.Spare_Parts_Used,
            COALESCE(m.Serviced_At, m.Completed_Date) AS Serviced_At,
            m.Completed_Date,
            m.Cost_VND,
            m.Reset_Runtime_Flag,
            m.Status,
            m.Remarks
        FROM Equipment_Maintenance m
        LEFT JOIN Equipment_Master e ON (m.EquipmentID = e.EquipmentID OR m.Equipment_Code = e.EquipmentCode)
        WHERE m.EquipmentID = ?
        ORDER BY COALESCE(m.Serviced_At, m.Completed_Date) DESC;
        """
        rows = execute_query(sql, (equipment_id,))
    else:
        sql = """
        SELECT 
            m.Maintenance_ID,
            m.Maintenance_ID AS log_id,
            m.EquipmentID,
            COALESCE(e.EquipmentCode, m.Equipment_Code) AS EquipmentCode,
            COALESCE(e.EquipmentName, m.Equipment_Code) AS EquipmentName,
            COALESCE(m.Ticket_Type, m.Maintenance_Type) AS Ticket_Type,
            m.Maintenance_Type,
            COALESCE(m.Action_Taken, m.Description) AS Action_Taken,
            m.Description,
            m.Technician,
            m.Spare_Parts_Used,
            COALESCE(m.Serviced_At, m.Completed_Date) AS Serviced_At,
            m.Completed_Date,
            m.Cost_VND,
            m.Reset_Runtime_Flag,
            m.Status,
            m.Remarks
        FROM Equipment_Maintenance m
        LEFT JOIN Equipment_Master e ON (m.EquipmentID = e.EquipmentID OR m.Equipment_Code = e.EquipmentCode)
        ORDER BY COALESCE(m.Serviced_At, m.Completed_Date) DESC;
        """
        rows = execute_query(sql)

    return [_format_row_dates(r) for r in rows]


def record_maintenance_event(
    equipment_id: Union[int, str],
    maintenance_type: str,
    description: str,
    technician: str,
    cost_vnd: float = 0.0,
    reset_runtime: bool = True,
    ticket_type: Optional[str] = None,
    spare_parts_used: Optional[str] = None,
    action_taken: Optional[str] = None,
    remarks: Optional[str] = None,
    equipment_code: Optional[str] = None
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Records a completed maintenance event:
    1. Inserts record into dbo.Equipment_Maintenance.
    2. Increments Maintenance_Count by 1 in dbo.Equipment_Master.
    3. Updates Last_Maintenance_Date = GETDATE() and Next_Maintenance_Due = DATEADD(month, 3, GETDATE()).
    4. If reset_runtime is True, resets Runtime_Hours to 0.0.
    5. If status was MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED, FAULT, or MAINTENANCE, restores to 'STOPPED'.
    """
    lookup_val = equipment_code or equipment_id
    eq = get_equipment_by_id_or_code(lookup_val)
    if not eq and equipment_id:
        eq = get_equipment_by_id_or_code(equipment_id)
    if not eq:
        raise ValueError(f"Equipment with identifier [{lookup_val}] does not exist.")

    eq_code = eq["EquipmentCode"]
    current_status = str(eq.get("CurrentStatus", "STOPPED")).strip().upper()

    t_type = str(ticket_type or maintenance_type or "Preventive")
    m_type = str(maintenance_type or t_type)
    desc = str(description or remarks or "Báº£o dÆ°á»¡ng Ä‘á»‹nh ká»³ hoĂ n táº¥t.")
    act_taken = str(action_taken or desc)
    parts = str(spare_parts_used or "")
    rem = str(remarks or desc)

    # 1. Insert into Equipment_Maintenance
    insert_maint_sql = """
    INSERT INTO Equipment_Maintenance (
        EquipmentID, Equipment_Code, Maintenance_Type, Ticket_Type, Description,
        Action_Taken, Technician, Spare_Parts_Used, Cost_VND, Completed_Date,
        Serviced_At, Reset_Runtime_Flag, Status, Remarks
    )
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE(), GETDATE(), ?, 'Completed', ?);
    """

    # 2. Update Equipment_Master
    update_eq_sql = """
    UPDATE Equipment_Master
    SET Maintenance_Count = ISNULL(Maintenance_Count, 0) + 1,
        Last_Maintenance_Date = GETDATE(),
        Next_Maintenance_Due = DATEADD(month, 3, GETDATE()),
        Runtime_Hours = CASE WHEN ? = 1 THEN 0.0 ELSE Runtime_Hours END,
        CurrentStatus = CASE WHEN CurrentStatus IN ('MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED', 'FAULT', 'MAINTENANCE', 'STOP') THEN 'STOPPED' ELSE CurrentStatus END
    WHERE EquipmentID = ?;
    """

    ops = [
        (insert_maint_sql, (
            equipment_id, eq_code, m_type, t_type, desc,
            act_taken, str(technician), parts, float(cost_vnd),
            1 if reset_runtime else 0, rem
        )),
        (update_eq_sql, (1 if reset_runtime else 0, equipment_id))
    ]

    # If status changes, log transition
    if current_status in ('MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED', 'FAULT', 'MAINTENANCE', 'STOP'):
        insert_hist_sql = """
        INSERT INTO Equipment_Status_History (EquipmentID, Old_Status, New_Status, Duration_Seconds, Changed_At)
        VALUES (?, ?, 'STOPPED', 0, GETDATE());
        """
        ops.append((insert_hist_sql, (equipment_id, current_status)))

    execute_transaction(ops)

    # 3. Log to audit trail
    db_connector.log_user_action(
        user_name=technician,
        user_role="MAINTENANCE_TECH",
        action_name="MAINTENANCE_LOGGED",
        detail=f"Recorded [{t_type}] maintenance for [{eq_code}]. Spare Parts: [{parts}]. Cost: {cost_vnd:,.0f} VND. Reset Runtime: {reset_runtime}"
    )

    logger.info(f"Recorded maintenance event for Equipment [{eq_code}], count incremented.")
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "EquipmentID": equipment_id,
        "EquipmentCode": eq_code,
        "MaintenanceType": m_type,
        "TicketType": t_type,
        "ResetRuntime": reset_runtime
    }


def get_status_history(equipment_id: Optional[int] = None, limit: int = 100) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Retrieves state transition audit trail from dbo.Equipment_Status_History."""
    if equipment_id:
        sql = f"""
        SELECT TOP {int(limit)}
            h.Hist_ID,
            h.EquipmentID,
            e.EquipmentCode,
            e.EquipmentName,
            h.Old_Status,
            h.New_Status,
            ISNULL(h.Duration_Seconds, 0) AS Duration_Seconds,
            h.Changed_At
        FROM Equipment_Status_History h
        LEFT JOIN Equipment_Master e ON h.EquipmentID = e.EquipmentID
        WHERE h.EquipmentID = ?
        ORDER BY h.Changed_At DESC;
        """
        rows = execute_query(sql, (equipment_id,))
    else:
        sql = f"""
        SELECT TOP {int(limit)}
            h.Hist_ID,
            h.EquipmentID,
            e.EquipmentCode,
            e.EquipmentName,
            h.Old_Status,
            h.New_Status,
            ISNULL(h.Duration_Seconds, 0) AS Duration_Seconds,
            h.Changed_At
        FROM Equipment_Status_History h
        LEFT JOIN Equipment_Master e ON h.EquipmentID = e.EquipmentID
        ORDER BY h.Changed_At DESC;
        """
        rows = execute_query(sql)

    return [_format_row_dates(r) for r in rows]


# ============================================================================
# 3. RUNTIME ACCUMULATION & BACKGROUND TRACKER ENGINE
# ============================================================================

class EquipmentRuntimeEngine:
    """
    Singleton runtime tracker:
    - Tracks elapsed run time for equipments while in RUNNING state.
    - Flushes accumulated runtime increments to dbo.Equipment_Master every 30 seconds.
    - Evaluates Maintenance_Limit_Hours threshold and flags MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.lock = asyncio.Lock()
        # equipment_id -> {"status": str, "last_tick": float, "pending_runtime_hours": float}
        self.tracker: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self.last_flush_time = time.time()
        self._initialized = False

    async def initialize_from_db(self):
        """Loads current states from database on startup."""
        try:
            items = await asyncio.to_thread(get_all_equipment)
            now = time.time()
            for eq in items:
                eq_id = int(eq["EquipmentID"])
                self.tracker[eq_id] = {
                    "code": eq["EquipmentCode"],
                    "status": str(eq.get("CurrentStatus", "STOP")).upper(),
                    "last_tick": now,
                    "pending_runtime_hours": 0.0,
                    "maintenance_limit": float(eq.get("Maintenance_Limit_Hours", 500.0)),
                    "current_db_runtime": float(eq.get("Runtime_Hours", 0.0))
                }
            self._initialized = True
            logger.info(f"EquipmentRuntimeEngine initialized tracking for {len(self.tracker)} equipment assets.")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Failed to initialize EquipmentRuntimeEngine from DB: {e}")

    async def sync_equipment_status(self, eq_id: int, new_status: str):
        """Synchronizes in-memory status when equipment transitions."""
        async with self.lock:
            if eq_id in self.tracker:
                self.tracker[eq_id]["status"] = str(new_status).upper()
                self.tracker[eq_id]["last_tick"] = time.time()

    async def tick_1s(self):
        """Called every second by the supervisor background worker."""
        if not self._initialized:
            await self.initialize_from_db()
            return

        now = time.time()
        async with self.lock:
            for eq_id, state in self.tracker.items():
                if state["status"] == "RUNNING":
                    elapsed = now - state["last_tick"]
                    if elapsed > 0:
                        # Convert elapsed seconds to hours
                        delta_hours = elapsed / 3600.0
                        state["pending_runtime_hours"] += delta_hours
                state["last_tick"] = now

        # Flush every 30 seconds
        if now - self.last_flush_time >= 30.0:
            self.last_flush_time = now
            await self.flush_to_db()

    async def flush_to_db(self):
        """Flushes pending runtime hours to dbo.Equipment_Master and checks limits."""
        to_flush: List[Tuple[int, float]] = []
        async with self.lock:
            for eq_id, state in self.tracker.items():
                if state["pending_runtime_hours"] > 0:
                    to_flush.append((eq_id, state["pending_runtime_hours"]))
                    state["current_db_runtime"] += state["pending_runtime_hours"]
                    state["pending_runtime_hours"] = 0.0

        if not to_flush:
            return

        def _do_flush():
            for eq_id, hours_inc in to_flush:
                try:
                    update_sql = """
                    UPDATE Equipment_Master
                    SET Runtime_Hours = ISNULL(Runtime_Hours, 0.0) + ?
                    WHERE EquipmentID = ?;
                    """
                    execute_query(update_sql, (float(hours_inc), int(eq_id)))

                    # Check limit
                    check_sql = """
                    SELECT EquipmentCode, Runtime_Hours, Maintenance_Limit_Hours, CurrentStatus
                    FROM Equipment_Master
                    WHERE EquipmentID = ?;
                    """
                    rows = execute_query(check_sql, (int(eq_id),))
                    if rows:
                        r = rows[0]
                        rt = float(r.get("Runtime_Hours", 0.0))
                        limit = float(r.get("Maintenance_Limit_Hours", 500.0))
                        c_status = str(r.get("CurrentStatus", "STOP")).upper()
                        if rt >= limit and c_status != "MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED":
                            logger.warning(f"Equipment [{r.get('EquipmentCode')}] reached limit ({rt:.1f}h >= {limit:.1f}h). Flagging MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED.")
                            update_equipment_status(
                                eq_id, 
                                "MAINTENANCE_REQUIRED", 
                                trigger_source="RUNTIME_LIMIT_WATCHDOG"
                            )
                except Exception as err:
                    logger.error(f"Error flushing runtime for equipment {eq_id}: {err}")

        await asyncio.to_thread(_do_flush)


runtime_engine = EquipmentRuntimeEngine()


# ============================================================================
# 4. TELEMETRY HISTORIAN (dbo.PLC_Datalog)
# ============================================================================

TAG_UNIT_MAPPING: Dict[str, str] = {
    "Temp_Sterilizer_PV": "Â°C",
    "Tank_Level_PV": "L",
    "Agitator_Speed_PV": "RPM",
    "Temp_Cooling_PV": "Â°C",
    "Temperature_ACT": "Â°C",
    "Weight_ACT": "kg",
    "Speed_PV": "RPM",
    "Line1.Flow_Rate": "L/min",
    "Line1.Pressure": "bar",
}


def log_telemetry_point(order_code: str, tag_name: str, value: float, unit: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
    """Inserts a single telemetry reading into dbo.PLC_Datalog via Store-and-Forward."""
    try:
        tag_unit = unit or TAG_UNIT_MAPPING.get(tag_name, "")
        if db_engine:
            res = db_engine.log_datalog(order_code, tag_name, float(value), tag_unit)
            return bool(res)
        sql = """
        INSERT INTO PLC_Datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE());
        """
        return execute_query(sql, (order_code, tag_name, float(value), tag_unit), timeout=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Failed to log telemetry point ({tag_name}): {e}")
        return False


def log_telemetry_batch(records: List[Tuple[str, str, float, Optional[str]]]) -> bool:
    """Inserts a batch of telemetry readings via Store-and-Forward."""
    if not records:
        return True
    try:
        if db_engine:
            for oc, tag, val, u in records:
                tag_unit = u or TAG_UNIT_MAPPING.get(tag, "")
                db_engine.log_datalog(oc, tag, float(val), tag_unit)
            return True
        ops = []
        for oc, tag, val, u in records:
            tag_unit = u or TAG_UNIT_MAPPING.get(tag, "")
            sql = """
            INSERT INTO PLC_Datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE());
            """
            ops.append((sql, (oc, tag, float(val), tag_unit)))
        return execute_transaction(ops, timeout=3)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Failed to log telemetry batch: {e}")
        return False


def get_telemetry_for_order(order_code: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 1000) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Retrieves time-series data from dbo.PLC_Datalog for an order.
    If order_code is 'LATEST' or not specified, finds the most recent logged order.
    """
    try:
        if not order_code or order_code.upper() in ("LATEST", "CURRENT", "ACTIVE"):
            find_order_sql = "SELECT TOP 1 OrderCode FROM PLC_Datalog ORDER BY ReadTime DESC;"
            rows = execute_query(find_order_sql)
            if rows and rows[0].get("OrderCode"):
                order_code = rows[0]["OrderCode"]
            else:
                return []

        sql = f"""
        SELECT TOP {int(limit)}
            LogID,
            OrderCode,
            TagName,
            Value,
            ISNULL(Unit, '') AS Unit,
            ReadTime
        FROM PLC_Datalog
        WHERE OrderCode = ?
        ORDER BY ReadTime ASC;
        """
        data = execute_query(sql, (order_code,))
        return [_format_row_dates(r) for r in data]
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error fetching telemetry for order [{order_code}]: {e}")
        return []


# ============================================================================
# 5. SETPOINT DEVIATION MONITORING (dbo.PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring)
# ============================================================================

def log_setpoint_deviation(
    tag_name: str,
    setpoint_val: float,
    actual_val: float,
    tolerance_pct: float = 5.0
) -> bool:
    """
    Compares setpoint vs actual process value and logs deviation into dbo.PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring.
    """
    try:
        sp = float(setpoint_val)
        act = float(actual_val)
        dev = abs(act - sp) / sp * 100.0 if sp != 0.0 else 0.0
        status = "CONFORMING" if dev <= tolerance_pct else "DEVIATION_ALERT"

        sql = """
        INSERT INTO PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring (TagName, SetpointValue, ActualValue, DeviationPercent, ReadTime, Status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE(), ?);
        """
        return execute_query(sql, (tag_name, sp, act, round(dev, 2), status), timeout=2)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"Failed to log setpoint monitoring for {tag_name}: {e}")
        return False


def get_setpoint_monitoring_logs(limit: int = 100) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Retrieves recent setpoint comparisons from dbo.PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring."""
    try:
        sql = f"""
        SELECT TOP {int(limit)}
            LogID,
            TagName,
            SetpointValue,
            ActualValue,
            DeviationPercent,
            ReadTime,
            Status
        FROM PLC_Setpoint_Monitoring
        ORDER BY ReadTime DESC;
        """
        data = execute_query(sql)
        return [_format_row_dates(r) for r in data]
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error fetching setpoint monitoring logs: {e}")
        return []

import os
import json
import pyodbc
import time
import logging

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger("SQL_Init")

CANDIDATE_SERVERS = [
    r"localhost\WINCC",
    "localhost",
    r"localhost\SQLEXPRESS",
    r".\WINCC",
    r".\SQLEXPRESS",
    "."
]

def find_working_server():
    for s in CANDIDATE_SERVERS:
        conn_str = f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}};SERVER={s};DATABASE=master;Trusted_Connection=yes;TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
        try:
            conn = pyodbc.connect(conn_str, timeout=3)
            logger.info(f"Connected to SQL Server instance: {s}")
            conn.close()
            return s
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None

def _provision_12_equipment_assets(cursor):
    """
    Idempotently provisions the exact 12 plant physical equipment assets in dbo.Equipment_Master (ISA-88/ISA-95/ISA-101):
    1: PUMP_INLET_MILK (Raw Milk Intake Pump 1)
    2: PUMP_INLET_SUGAR (Sugar Intake Pump 2)
    3: PUMP_INLET_ADDITIVE (Additive Micro Dosing Pump 3)
    4: AGITATOR_MIX (Homogenizing Mixing Motor - PH12101)
    5: PUMP_OUTLET (Discharge Product Transfer Pump)
    6: HEATER_UHT (UHT Pasteurization Resistor - PH22101)
    7: CHILLER_COOL (Rapid Plate Heat Exchanger - PH22201)
    8: VALVE_INLET_MILK (Raw Milk Intake Control Valve)
    9: VALVE_INLET_SUGAR (Sugar Intake Control Valve)
    10: VALVE_INLET_ADDITIVE (Additive Intake Control Valve)
    11: VALVE_OUTLET_MIX (Tank Bottom Drain Control Valve)
    12: VALVE_CIP_RECYCLE (CIP Sanitation Bypass Valve)
    """
    # Ensure table exists
    cursor.execute("""
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Equipment_Master' AND xtype='U')
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Equipment_Master (
        EquipmentID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
        EquipmentCode NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
        EquipmentName NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        EquipmentType NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        Location NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        CurrentStatus NVARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'STOPPED',
        Runtime_Hours REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
        TotalStarts INT DEFAULT 0,
        Maintenance_Limit_Hours REAL DEFAULT 500.0,
        MaintenanceLimit_Hours REAL DEFAULT 500.0,
        Maintenance_Count INT DEFAULT 0,
        Last_Maintenance_Date DATETIME NULL,
        Next_Maintenance_Due DATETIME NULL,
        RatedSpeed REAL NULL,
        DesignFlow REAL NULL,
        DesignTemp REAL NULL,
        RatedCurrent REAL DEFAULT 10.0,
        MaxVibrationThreshold REAL DEFAULT 4.5,
        MaxTempThreshold REAL DEFAULT 75.0,
        Notes NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        Created_At DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE(),
        Last_Update DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE()
    );
    """)

    # Ensure all engineering columns exist
    cols = [
        ("RatedSpeed", "REAL NULL"),
        ("DesignFlow", "REAL NULL"),
        ("DesignTemp", "REAL NULL"),
        ("Maintenance_Limit_Hours", "REAL DEFAULT 500.0"),
        ("MaintenanceLimit_Hours", "REAL DEFAULT 500.0"),
        ("RatedCurrent", "REAL DEFAULT 10.0"),
        ("MaxVibrationThreshold", "REAL DEFAULT 4.5"),
        ("MaxTempThreshold", "REAL DEFAULT 75.0"),
        ("Notes", "NVARCHAR(500) NULL")
    ]
    for col, col_type in cols:
        cursor.execute(f"""
            IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Master', '{col}') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE dbo.Equipment_Master ADD {col} {col_type};
        """)

    # Ensure Equipment_Maintenance columns exist
    maint_cols = [
        ("Equipment_Code", "NVARCHAR(50) NULL"),
        ("Ticket_Type", "NVARCHAR(50) NULL"),
        ("Action_Taken", "NVARCHAR(255) NULL"),
        ("Spare_Parts_Used", "NVARCHAR(255) NULL"),
        ("Serviced_At", "DATETIME NULL"),
        ("Reset_Runtime_Flag", "BIT DEFAULT 1"),
        ("Remarks", "NVARCHAR(500) NULL"),
    ]
    for col, col_type in maint_cols:
        cursor.execute(f"""
            IF COL_LENGTH('dbo.Equipment_Maintenance', '{col}') IS NULL
            ALTER TABLE dbo.Equipment_Maintenance ADD {col} {col_type};
        """)

    assets = [
        (1, 'PUMP_INLET_MILK', 'BÆ¡m náº¡p sá»¯a tÆ°Æ¡i (BÆ¡m 1)', 'Dosing Pump', 'Line 1 - Raw Milk Intake', None, 100.0, None, 500.0, 'BÆ¡m náº¡p nguyĂªn liá»‡u sá»¯a tÆ°Æ¡i P-101 (PH11101)'),
        (2, 'PUMP_INLET_SUGAR', 'BÆ¡m náº¡p Ä‘Æ°á»ng (BÆ¡m 2)', 'Dosing Pump', 'Line 1 - Sugar Intake', None, 50.0, None, 500.0, 'BÆ¡m Ä‘á»‹nh lÆ°á»£ng siro Ä‘Æ°á»ng P-102 (PH11202)'),
        (3, 'PUMP_INLET_ADDITIVE', 'BÆ¡m náº¡p phá»¥ gia (BÆ¡m 3)', 'Micro Dosing Pump', 'Line 1 - Additive Intake', None, 20.0, None, 500.0, 'BÆ¡m vi lÆ°á»£ng phá»¥ gia P-103 (PH11303)'),
        (4, 'AGITATOR_MIX', 'Äá»™ng cÆ¡ khuáº¥y bá»“n trá»™n', 'Impeller Agitator', 'Line 1 - Mixing Unit', 120.0, None, None, 500.0, 'Äá»™ng cÆ¡ khuáº¥y Ä‘á»“ng hĂ³a bá»“n trá»™n (PH12101)'),
        (5, 'PUMP_OUTLET', 'BÆ¡m xáº£ thĂ nh pháº©m ra bá»“n Ä‘á»‡m', 'Transfer Pump', 'Line 1 - Discharge Line', None, 100.0, None, 500.0, 'BÆ¡m xáº£ thĂ nh pháº©m ra bá»“n Ä‘á»‡m P-203 (PH22202)'),
        (6, 'HEATER_UHT', 'Cá»¥m gia nhiá»‡t tiá»‡t trĂ¹ng UHT', 'Thermal Exchanger', 'Line 1 - UHT Pasteurizer', None, None, 138.0, 500.0, 'Cá»¥m Ä‘iá»‡n trá»Ÿ gia nhiá»‡t tiá»‡t trĂ¹ng UHT H-201 (PH22101)'),
        (7, 'CHILLER_COOL', 'Cá»¥m giáº£i nhiá»‡t lĂ m láº¡nh sá»‘c', 'Plate Heat Exchanger', 'Line 1 - Rapid Chiller', None, None, 4.0, 500.0, 'Bá»™ trao Ä‘á»•i nhiá»‡t lĂ m láº¡nh sá»‘c C-202 (PH22201)'),
        (8, 'VALVE_INLET_MILK', 'Van náº¡p sá»¯a bá»“n trá»™n', 'Control Valve', 'Line 1 - Raw Milk Intake', None, None, None, 500.0, 'Van Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn náº¡p sá»¯a tÆ°Æ¡i V-101 (PH11102)'),
        (9, 'VALVE_INLET_SUGAR', 'Van náº¡p Ä‘Æ°á»ng bá»“n trá»™n', 'Control Valve', 'Line 1 - Sugar Intake', None, None, None, 500.0, 'Van Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn náº¡p siro Ä‘Æ°á»ng V-102 (PH11203)'),
        (10, 'VALVE_INLET_ADDITIVE', 'Van náº¡p phá»¥ gia bá»“n trá»™n', 'Control Valve', 'Line 1 - Additive Intake', None, None, None, 500.0, 'Van vi lÆ°á»£ng phá»¥ gia V-103 (PH11304)'),
        (11, 'VALVE_OUTLET_MIX', 'Van xáº£ Ä‘Ă¡y bá»“n trá»™n', 'Bottom Outlet Valve', 'Line 1 - Mixing Unit', None, None, None, 500.0, 'Van xáº£ Ä‘Ă¡y xáº£ thĂ nh pháº©m V-201 (PH12102)'),
        (12, 'VALVE_CIP_RECYCLE', 'Van tuáº§n hoĂ n CIP/vá»‡ sinh', 'CIP Bypass Valve', 'Line 1 - CIP Station', None, None, None, 500.0, 'Van tuáº§n hoĂ n há»‡ thá»‘ng CIP vá»‡ sinh V-301 (PH13101)')
    ]

    cursor.execute("SELECT EquipmentID, EquipmentCode, Runtime_Hours, TotalStarts, CurrentStatus FROM dbo.Equipment_Master")
    current_rows = cursor.fetchall()
    current_by_id = {row[0]: row for row in current_rows}
    
    needs_reseed = False
    if len(current_rows) != 12:
        needs_reseed = True
    else:
        for eq_id, code, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _ in assets:
            if eq_id not in current_by_id or str(current_by_id[eq_id][1]).upper() != code:
                needs_reseed = True
                break

    if needs_reseed:
        saved_runtimes = {}
        for row in current_rows:
            code_up = str(row[1]).upper()
            saved_runtimes[code_up] = (float(row[2] or 0.0), int(row[3] or 0), str(row[4] or 'STOPPED'))

        if 'PUMP_INLET' in saved_runtimes and 'PUMP_INLET_MILK' not in saved_runtimes:
            saved_runtimes['PUMP_INLET_MILK'] = saved_runtimes['PUMP_INLET']

        cursor.execute("DELETE FROM dbo.Equipment_Master")
        cursor.execute("SET IDENTITY_INSERT dbo.Equipment_Master ON")
        for eq_id, code, name, eq_type, loc, speed, flow, temp, m_limit, notes in assets:
            prev_rt, prev_starts, prev_st = saved_runtimes.get(code, (0.0, 0, 'STOPPED'))
            status = prev_st if prev_st in ('RUNNING', 'STOPPED', 'FAULT', 'MAINTENANCE', 'STOP') else 'STOPPED'
            if status == 'STOP':
                status = 'STOPPED'
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT INTO dbo.Equipment_Master (
                    EquipmentID, EquipmentCode, EquipmentName, EquipmentType, Location,
                    CurrentStatus, Runtime_Hours, TotalStarts, Maintenance_Limit_Hours,
                    MaintenanceLimit_Hours, RatedSpeed, DesignFlow, DesignTemp, Notes,
                    RatedCurrent, MaxVibrationThreshold, MaxTempThreshold,
                    Created_At, Last_Update
                ) VALUES (
                    ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
                    ?, ?, ?, ?,
                    ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
                    10.0, 4.5, 75.0,
                    GETDATE(), GETDATE()
                )
            """, (eq_id, code, name, eq_type, loc, status, prev_rt, prev_starts, m_limit, m_limit, speed, flow, temp, notes))
        cursor.execute("SET IDENTITY_INSERT dbo.Equipment_Master OFF")
        logger.info("[Equipment_Master] Re-aligned to exact 12 core assets (IDs 1..12).")
    else:
        for eq_id, code, name, eq_type, loc, speed, flow, temp, m_limit, notes in assets:
            cursor.execute("""
                UPDATE dbo.Equipment_Master
                SET EquipmentName = ?, EquipmentType = ?, Location = ?,
                    RatedSpeed = ?, DesignFlow = ?, DesignTemp = ?,
                    Maintenance_Limit_Hours = ?, MaintenanceLimit_Hours = ?, Notes = ?,
                    Last_Update = GETDATE()
                WHERE EquipmentID = ? AND EquipmentCode = ?
            """, (name, eq_type, loc, speed, flow, temp, m_limit, m_limit, notes, eq_id, code))
        logger.info("[Equipment_Master] Verified 12 core assets in place.")

_provision_7_equipment_assets = _provision_12_equipment_assets

def init_mes_database():
    server = find_working_server()
    if not server:
        logger.warning("No running SQL Server instance found on host.")
        return False

    master_conn_str = f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}};SERVER={server};DATABASE=master;Trusted_Connection=yes;TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
    try:
        conn = pyodbc.connect(master_conn_str, autocommit=True, timeout=5)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sys.databases WHERE name = 'MES_Milk_Production'")
        if not cursor.fetchone():
            logger.info("Creating database MES_Milk_Production...")
            cursor.execute("CREATE DATABASE MES_Milk_Production")
            time.sleep(1)
        conn.close()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error checking/creating master DB: {e}")
        return False

    db_conn_str = f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server}};SERVER={server};DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;Trusted_Connection=yes;TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
    try:
        conn = pyodbc.connect(db_conn_str, autocommit=True, timeout=5)
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        # 1. Material_Stock (ISA-95 Level 3 Physical Silo / Tank Storage)
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Material_Stock' AND xtype='U')
        CREATE TABLE Material_Stock (
            Material_Code VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
            Material_Name NVARCHAR(150),
            AssignedTank VARCHAR(50),
            CurrentStock FLOAT,
            Total_Quantity FLOAT,
            Unit VARCHAR(20),
            TankCapacity FLOAT,
            MinSafetyThreshold FLOAT,
            Last_Update DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE()
        )
        """)

        # Dynamically ensure Material_Stock has OPC UA & loadcell mapping columns
        mat_stock_cols = [
            ("OpcNodeId", "NVARCHAR(255) NULL"),
            ("OpcHighLevelNodeId", "NVARCHAR(255) NULL"),
            ("OpcLowLevelNodeId", "NVARCHAR(255) NULL"),
            ("SensorType", "NVARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'LOADCELL_MASS'"),
            ("TareWeight", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("CreatedBy", "NVARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'admin'")
        ]
        for col_name, col_spec in mat_stock_cols:
            cursor.execute(f"""
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.Material_Stock') AND name = '{col_name}')
            ALTER TABLE dbo.Material_Stock ADD {col_name} {col_spec};
            """)

        # Physical Laboratory / Pilot Bench Scale (5.0L storage tanks, 0.5L safety min) - ONLY fill NULLs, never overwrite custom calibrations
        cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE dbo.Material_Stock 
        SET TankCapacity = 5.0, MinSafetyThreshold = 0.5, Unit = 'L'
        WHERE AssignedTank = 'TANK-01' AND TankCapacity IS NULL;

        UPDATE dbo.Material_Stock 
        SET TankCapacity = 5.0, MinSafetyThreshold = 0.5, Unit = 'kg'
        WHERE AssignedTank = 'TANK-02' AND TankCapacity IS NULL;

        UPDATE dbo.Material_Stock 
        SET TankCapacity = 5.0, MinSafetyThreshold = 0.5, Unit = 'L'
        WHERE AssignedTank = 'TANK-03' AND TankCapacity IS NULL;
        """)

        # Ensure dbo.Material_Input exists with ISA-95 verification columns
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Material_Input' AND xtype='U')
        CREATE TABLE dbo.Material_Input (
            Input_ID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
            Material_Code NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
            Material_Name NVARCHAR(150),
            Quantity REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            Input_Time DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE(),
            Remark NVARCHAR(MAX),
            Tank_ID NVARCHAR(50) NULL,
            PreIntakeWeight FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0,
            PostIntakeWeight FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0,
            ActualDeliveredWeight FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0,
            WeightDiscrepancy FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0,
            DiscrepancyStatus NVARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'VERIFIED'
        );
        """)

        mat_input_cols = [
            ("Tank_ID", "NVARCHAR(50) NULL"),
            ("TankID", "NVARCHAR(50) NULL"),
            ("ReceiptCode", "NVARCHAR(100) NULL"),
            ("ExpectedQty", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("ActualQty", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("DiscrepancyQty", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("DiscrepancyPercent", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("SupplierLot", "NVARCHAR(100) NULL"),
            ("Status", "NVARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'MATCHED'"),
            ("ReceivedBy", "NVARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Operator'"),
            ("PreIntakeWeight", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("PostIntakeWeight", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("ActualDeliveredWeight", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("WeightDiscrepancy", "FLOAT DEFAULT 0.0"),
            ("DiscrepancyStatus", "NVARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'VERIFIED'")
        ]
        for col_name, col_spec in mat_input_cols:
            cursor.execute(f"""
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.Material_Input') AND name = '{col_name}')
            ALTER TABLE dbo.Material_Input ADD {col_name} {col_spec};
            """)

        # STARTUP SHIELD: Never wipe user-calibrated OPC Node IDs on startup.
        # Clean baseline is only applied if Material_Stock is completely empty during initial seeding.

        # 2. Customers
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Customers' AND xtype='U')
        CREATE TABLE Customers (
            Customer_ID VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
            Customer_Code VARCHAR(50),
            Customer_Name NVARCHAR(150),
            Address NVARCHAR(255),
            Email VARCHAR(100),
            Phone VARCHAR(50),
            Priority INT DEFAULT 2,
            Status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Active',
            ActiveOrders INT DEFAULT 0
        )
        """)

        # 3. Recipes
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Recipes' AND xtype='U')
        CREATE TABLE Recipes (
            Recipe_ID VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
            Recipe_Name NVARCHAR(150),
            Description NVARCHAR(255),
            Target_Volume FLOAT DEFAULT 2.0,
            Temp_Hot FLOAT,
            Temp_Cold FLOAT,
            Status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'RELEASED',
            Version VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'v2.4'
        )
        """)

        # 4. Recipe_Detail
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Recipe_Detail' AND xtype='U')
        CREATE TABLE Recipe_Detail (
            Detail_ID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
            Recipe_ID VARCHAR(50),
            Material_ID VARCHAR(50),
            Material_Name NVARCHAR(150),
            Percentage FLOAT,
            Target_Qty FLOAT,
            Unit VARCHAR(20)
        )
        """)

        # 5. Alarm_History (ISA-18.2 Alarm Lifecycle Management)
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.tables WHERE name = 'Alarm_History')
        BEGIN
            CREATE TABLE dbo.Alarm_History (
                AlarmID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
                AlarmCode NVARCHAR(50),
                Message NVARCHAR(255),
                TriggerTime DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE(),
                IsAcknowledged BIT DEFAULT 0,
                AckTime DATETIME NULL,
                AckUser NVARCHAR(50) NULL
            );
        END
        ELSE
        BEGIN
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.Alarm_History') AND name = 'IsAcknowledged')
                ALTER TABLE dbo.Alarm_History ADD IsAcknowledged BIT DEFAULT 0;
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.Alarm_History') AND name = 'AckTime')
                ALTER TABLE dbo.Alarm_History ADD AckTime DATETIME NULL;
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.Alarm_History') AND name = 'AckUser')
                ALTER TABLE dbo.Alarm_History ADD AckUser NVARCHAR(50) NULL;
        END
        """)

        # 6. UserActionLog (FDA 21 CFR Part 11 & ALCOA+ Audit Trail)
        cursor.execute("""
        IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.tables WHERE name='UserActionLog')
        CREATE TABLE dbo.UserActionLog (
            ID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
            UserName NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
            UserRole NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
            ActionName NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
            Detail NVARCHAR(MAX) NOT NULL,
            ActionTime DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE(),
            FieldName NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
            OldValue NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
            NewValue NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
            Reason NVARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
            SignatureMeaning NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
            RecordHash CHAR(64) NULL,
            IsCorrection BIT DEFAULT 0,
            RefLogID INT NULL
        );
        """)

        # Ensure existing instances receive 21 CFR Part 11 columns
        alter_stmts = [
            ("FieldName", "NVARCHAR(100) NULL"),
            ("OldValue", "NVARCHAR(500) NULL"),
            ("NewValue", "NVARCHAR(500) NULL"),
            ("Reason", "NVARCHAR(500) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'STANDARD_AUDIT'"),
            ("SignatureMeaning", "NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'System Audit Trail'"),
            ("RecordHash", "CHAR(64) NULL"),
            ("IsCorrection", "BIT DEFAULT 0"),
            ("RefLogID", "INT NULL")
        ]
        for col_name, col_spec in alter_stmts:
            cursor.execute(f"""
            IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.columns WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('dbo.UserActionLog') AND name = '{col_name}')
            ALTER TABLE dbo.UserActionLog ADD {col_name} {col_spec};
            """)

        # Deploy INSTEAD OF UPDATE, DELETE Immutability Trigger on dbo.UserActionLog
        cursor.execute("""
        IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.triggers WHERE name = 'trg_UserActionLog_Immutability')
        DROP TRIGGER trg_UserActionLog_Immutability;
        """)
        cursor.execute("""
        CREATE TRIGGER trg_UserActionLog_Immutability
        ON dbo.UserActionLog
        INSTEAD OF UPDATE, DELETE
        AS
        BEGIN
            SET NOCOUNT ON;
            RAISERROR('FDA 21 CFR Part 11 Violation: Records in dbo.UserActionLog are immutable and cannot be updated or deleted.', 16, 1);
            ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
        END;
        """)

        # 7. Users (FDA 21 CFR Part 11 Electronic Signature Authentication)
        cursor.execute("""

# Strict DB Offline Circuit Breaker: If DB fails once, permanently bypass all DB logging
db_logging_disabled: bool = False

def _sync_db_worker(query: str, params: Optional[tuple] = None):
    """Executes query with 1s timeout in detached worker thread; disables further DB attempts if offline."""
    global db_logging_disabled
    if db_logging_disabled:
        return
    try:
        execute_query(query, params, timeout=1)
    except Exception as db_err:
        db_logging_disabled = True
        logger.warning(f"SQL Database is offline ({db_err}). Completely disabling background SQL logging to protect event loop.")

async def safe_db_execute_detached(query: str, params: Optional[tuple] = None):
    """
    Decoupled database execution: runs in a detached thread with strict 1-second timeout.
    Fails silently if SQL Server is offline, completely protecting the OPC UA event loop.
    """
    global db_logging_disabled
    if db_logging_disabled:
        return
    try:
        await asyncio.wait_for(
            asyncio.to_thread(_sync_db_worker, query, params),
            timeout=1.0
        )
    except Exception:
        db_logging_disabled = True

def dispatch_db_log(query: str, params: Optional[tuple] = None):
    """Non-blocking fire-and-forget background database task dispatcher; strictly bypassed when DB is offline."""
    global db_logging_disabled
    if db_logging_disabled:
        return
    try:
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        loop.create_task(safe_db_execute_detached(query, params))
    except RuntimeError:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass

GATEWAY_CONFIG_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "gateway_config.json")

DEFAULT_GATEWAY_CONFIG: Dict[str, Any] = {
    "endpoint": "opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:49320",
    "timeout_ms": 3000,
    "sampling_rate_ms": 1000,
    "sampling_interval_ms": 100,
    "provider_name": "KEPServerEX v6 - Industrial Connectivity Platform",
    "security_policy": "None",
    "auth_mode": "anonymous",
    "username": ""
}

def load_gateway_config() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not os.path.exists(GATEWAY_CONFIG_FILE):
        try:
            with open(GATEWAY_CONFIG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(DEFAULT_GATEWAY_CONFIG, f, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Could not initialize gateway_config.json: {e}")
        return dict(DEFAULT_GATEWAY_CONFIG)
    try:
        with open(GATEWAY_CONFIG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            merged = dict(DEFAULT_GATEWAY_CONFIG)
            merged.update(data)
            return merged
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error loading gateway_config.json: {e}")
        return dict(DEFAULT_GATEWAY_CONFIG)

def save_gateway_config(config_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    try:
        current = load_gateway_config()
        current.update(config_data)
        with open(GATEWAY_CONFIG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(current, f, indent=2)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error saving gateway_config.json: {e}")
        return False

MAPPINGS_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "tag_mappings.json")

DEFAULT_ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

def load_modular_tag_mappings() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not os.path.exists(MAPPINGS_FILE):
        return {}
    try:
        with open(MAPPINGS_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                return data
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error reading {MAPPINGS_FILE}: {e}")
    return {}

def flatten_tag_mappings(raw_data: Dict[str, Any], prefix: str = "") -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    flat: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for key, val in raw_data.items():
        if isinstance(val, dict):
            if "node_id" in val and str(val.get("node_id", "")).strip():
                flat[key] = val
                if prefix:
                    flat[f"{prefix}.{key}"] = val
            else:
                sub_prefix = f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else key
                sub_flat = flatten_tag_mappings(val, prefix=sub_prefix)
                flat.update(sub_flat)
        elif isinstance(val, str) and val.strip() and val.strip() != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
            entry = {"node_id": val.strip()}
            flat[key] = entry
            if prefix:
                flat[f"{prefix}.{key}"] = entry
    return flat

def load_tag_mappings() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    modular = load_modular_tag_mappings()
    if not modular:
        return {}
    flat = flatten_tag_mappings(modular)
    return flat

def atomic_save_json(filepath: str, data: Any):
    """
    Atomically writes JSON to disk via tempfile and os.replace to prevent
    partial writes or file corruption during unexpected crashes or restarts.
    """
    dirname = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(filepath))
    os.makedirs(dirname, exist_ok=True)
    temp_fd, temp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=dirname, prefix="mes_tmp_", suffix=".json")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(temp_fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        os.replace(temp_path, filepath)
    except Exception as e:
        if os.path.exists(temp_path):
            try:
                os.remove(temp_path)
            except Exception:
                pass
        raise e

def save_tag_mappings(mappings: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]):
    """
    Safely saves tag mappings to MAPPINGS_FILE without erasing modular structures.
    Uses atomic file replacement to prevent partial writes.
    """
    modular = load_modular_tag_mappings()
    if not isinstance(modular, dict):
        modular = {}
    for k, v in mappings.items():
        if isinstance(v, dict) and "node_id" in v:
            placed = False
            for mod_k, mod_tags in modular.items():
                if isinstance(mod_tags, dict) and k in mod_tags:
                    modular[mod_k][k].update(v)
                    placed = True
                    break
            if not placed:
                modular.setdefault("operations", {})[k] = v
        elif isinstance(v, dict):
            modular.setdefault(k, {}).update(v)
    atomic_save_json(MAPPINGS_FILE, modular)

def save_modular_tag_mappings(modular_dict: Dict[str, Any]):
    """
    Atomically saves modular tag mappings dictionary to MAPPINGS_FILE.
    """
    atomic_save_json(MAPPINGS_FILE, modular_dict)

def reload_tag_metadata():
    global MODULAR_TAG_MAPPINGS, ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS, NODE_TO_TAG_META, explorer_watchlist
    MODULAR_TAG_MAPPINGS = load_modular_tag_mappings()
    ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS = load_tag_mappings()
    NODE_TO_TAG_META = {
        info["node_id"]: {"tag_name": tag_name, **info}
        for tag_name, info in ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS.items()
        if isinstance(info, dict) and "node_id" in info
    }
    explorer_watchlist.clear()
    for tag_name, info in ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS.items():
        if not isinstance(info, dict) or "node_id" not in info:
            continue
        register_subscribed_node(info["node_id"])
        explorer_watchlist.append({
            "tag_name": tag_name,
            "node_id": info["node_id"],
            "role": info.get("role", "General Process Tag"),
            "unit": info.get("unit", "--"),
            "writable": info.get("writable", True),
            "expected_type": "Float" if info.get("unit") in ("Â°C", "RPM", "pH", "Â°Bx", "mm/s", "A") else ("Boolean" if info.get("unit") == "Bool" else ("String" if tag_name == "Batch_State" else ("Int16" if info.get("unit") == "L" else "Float"))),
            "description": info.get("description", "")
        })



# Known OPC UA and Network Socket Connection Error Identifiers
CONNECTION_ERROR_NAMES = {
    "BadSecureChannelClosed", "BadConnectionClosed", "BadSessionClosed",
    "BadCommunicationError", "BadServerHalted", "BadSessionIdInvalid",
    "BadTimeout", "BadDisconnect", "BadTcpEndpointUrlInvalid",
    "BadNoContinuationPoints"
}

def is_connection_error(exc: Exception) -> bool:
    """Detects whether an exception indicates a dropped OPC UA socket or session."""
    if isinstance(exc, (ConnectionError, ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError, TimeoutError, OSError)):
        return True
    exc_type = type(exc).__name__
    if exc_type in CONNECTION_ERROR_NAMES:
        return True
    msg = str(exc)
    for name in CONNECTION_ERROR_NAMES:
        if name in msg:
            return True
    if any(keyword in msg.lower() for keyword in ("connection", "socket", "closed", "transport", "disconnect", "reset by peer")):
        return True
    return False


def build_initial_tag_cache() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    cache = {}
    try:
        for tag_name, info in ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS.items():
            if not isinstance(info, dict) or "node_id" not in info:
                continue
            node_id = str(info["node_id"]).strip()
            if not node_id or node_id == "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
                continue
            unit = info.get("unit", "--")
            role = info.get("role", "General Process Tag")
            writable = info.get("writable", True)
            description = info.get("description", tag_name)

            if unit in ("Â°C", "RPM", "pH", "Â°Bx", "mm/s", "A"):
                dt = "Float"
                val = 0.0
            elif unit in ("L", "kg", "Kg"):
                dt = "Float"
                val = 0.0
            elif unit in ("Bool", "BOOL"):
                dt = "Boolean"
                val = False
            elif unit == "State":
                dt = "String"
                val = "IDLE"
            else:
                dt = "Float"
                val = 0.0

            cache[node_id] = {
                "tag_name": tag_name,
                "node_id": node_id,
                "role": role,
                "unit": unit,
                "writable": writable,
                "description": description,
                "type": dt,
                "data_type": dt,
                "value": val,
                "quality": "Bad",
                "timestamp": "--:--:--"
            }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[build_initial_tag_cache] Tag cache initialization fallback: {exc}")
    return cache

    "last_updated": "--:--:--",

}

# ============================================================================
# CENTRAL SUBSCRIBED NODES BUS REGISTRY (Single Bus Across All Modules)
# ============================================================================
subscribed_nodes: Set[str] = set()

def register_subscribed_node(node_id: str) -> None:
    """Registers a node ID into the central continuous telemetry poll list."""
    if not node_id:
        return
    nid = str(node_id).strip()
    if nid and nid != "[CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº ]":
        subscribed_nodes.add(nid)
        clean_stripped = nid.replace("ns=2;s=", "").strip()
        if clean_stripped:
            subscribed_nodes.add(clean_stripped)

def unregister_subscribed_node(node_id: str) -> None:
    """Removes a node ID from the central continuous telemetry poll list."""
    if not node_id:
        return
    nid = str(node_id).strip()
    subscribed_nodes.discard(nid)
    clean_stripped = nid.replace("ns=2;s=", "").strip()
    subscribed_nodes.discard(clean_stripped)

# Initialize subscribed_nodes with essential MES tags
for _tname, _tinfo in ESSENTIAL_MES_TAGS.items():
    if isinstance(_tinfo, dict) and "node_id" in _tinfo:
        register_subscribed_node(_tinfo["node_id"])

# DYNAMIC INVENTORY TANK TELEMETRY & POLLER REGISTRY
