def get_current_shift_meta() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Derives active shift context based on ISA-95 standard 8-hour shift boundaries."""
    now = datetime.now()
    hour = now.hour

    if 6 <= hour < 14:
        shift_id = "SHIFT_1"
        shift_title = "SHIFT 1 (MORNING)"
        window = "06:00 - 14:00"
        leader = "Tran Duc (Lead Process Architect)"
        start_hour = 6
    elif 14 <= hour < 22:
        shift_id = "SHIFT_2"
        shift_title = "SHIFT 2 (AFTERNOON)"
        window = "14:00 - 22:00"
        leader = "Nguyen Van Supervisor (QA Lead)"
        start_hour = 14
    else:
        shift_id = "SHIFT_3"
        shift_title = "SHIFT 3 (NIGHT)"
        window = "22:00 - 06:00"
        leader = "Le Van Night Lead (Senior Automation Eng)"
        start_hour = 22

    shift_start = now.replace(hour=start_hour, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    if start_hour == 22 and hour < 6:
        shift_start = shift_start - timedelta(days=1)

    elapsed_minutes = max(1.0, (now - shift_start).total_seconds() / 60.0)
    elapsed_minutes = min(480.0, elapsed_minutes)

    return {
        "shift_id": shift_id,
        "shift_title": shift_title,
        "window": window,
        "shift_leader": leader,
        "shift_start": shift_start.isoformat(),
        "planned_shift_minutes": 480.0,
        "elapsed_shift_minutes": round(elapsed_minutes, 1)
    }


def calculate_availability_metrics(conn, shift_hours: int = 8) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes Availability (A):
    Queries dbo.Equipment_Status_History for the active shift window.
    Operating Time = SUM of time in 'RUNNING' state.
    Downtime = SUM of time in 'FAULT' or 'STOPPED' while a batch was active.
    A = Operating_Time / (Operating_Time + Downtime).
    """
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    operating_seconds = 0
    downtime_seconds = 0
    equipment_downtime_map: Dict[str, int] = {}
    equipment_operating_map: Dict[str, int] = {}

    try:
        query = """
            SELECT 
                h.EquipmentID,
                ISNULL(e.EquipmentCode, 'EQ_' + CAST(h.EquipmentID AS NVARCHAR)),
                UPPER(ISNULL(h.New_Status, 'STOP')),
                ISNULL(h.Duration_Seconds, 0)
            FROM dbo.Equipment_Status_History h
            LEFT JOIN dbo.Equipment_Master e ON h.EquipmentID = e.EquipmentID
            WHERE h.Changed_At >= DATEADD(HOUR, -?, GETDATE())
        """
        cursor.execute(query, (shift_hours,))
        rows = cursor.fetchall()

        for row in rows:
            eq_id, eq_code, status, dur = row[0], str(row[1]).upper(), str(row[2]).upper(), int(row[3] or 0)
            if status in ('RUNNING', 'RUN', 'START', 'STARTING'):
                operating_seconds += dur
                equipment_operating_map[eq_code] = equipment_operating_map.get(eq_code, 0) + dur
            elif status in ('FAULT', 'STOPPED', 'STOP', 'DOWN', 'ERROR', 'ALARM'):
                downtime_seconds += dur
                equipment_downtime_map[eq_code] = equipment_downtime_map.get(eq_code, 0) + dur

    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error querying dbo.Equipment_Status_History: {e}")

    # Fallback to current equipment states if history table is empty
    if operating_seconds == 0 and downtime_seconds == 0:
        try:
            cursor.execute("SELECT EquipmentCode, UPPER(ISNULL(CurrentStatus, 'STOP')) FROM dbo.Equipment_Master")
            eq_rows = cursor.fetchall()
            for eq_code, cur_status in eq_rows:
                code_str = str(eq_code).upper()
                st_str = str(cur_status).upper()
                if st_str in ('RUNNING', 'RUN'):
                    equipment_operating_map[code_str] = 0
                else:
                    equipment_downtime_map[code_str] = 0
        except Exception:
            pass

    total_time = operating_seconds + downtime_seconds
    if total_time > 0:
        availability = operating_seconds / float(total_time)
    else:
        # Zero data (cold standby) -> 0.0%
        availability = 0.0

    return {
        "availability": round(availability, 4),
        "operating_seconds": operating_seconds,
        "operating_minutes": round(operating_seconds / 60.0, 1),
        "downtime_seconds": downtime_seconds,
        "downtime_minutes": round(downtime_seconds / 60.0, 1),
        "equipment_operating_map": equipment_operating_map,
        "equipment_downtime_map": equipment_downtime_map
    }


def calculate_performance_metrics(conn, shift_hours: int = 8) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes Performance (P):
    Queries dbo.PLC_Datalog for operating assets (Agitator RPM, Pump Flow, Heater, Chiller).
    Compares average actual value vs design nominal setpoint.
    """
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    asset_performance_map: Dict[str, float] = {}
    asset_actual_avg_map: Dict[str, float] = {}

    try:
        query = """
            SELECT TagName, AVG(CAST(Value AS FLOAT)) as AvgVal, COUNT(*) as Pts
            FROM dbo.PLC_Datalog
            WHERE ReadTime >= DATEADD(HOUR, -?, GETDATE()) AND Value > 0
            GROUP BY TagName
        """
        cursor.execute(query, (shift_hours,))
        tag_averages = {str(row[0]): float(row[1]) for row in cursor.fetchall()}

        for asset_code, specs in EQUIPMENT_DESIGN_SPECS.items():
            nominal = float(specs["nominal_setpoint"])
            actual_vals = []
            for tag in specs["telemetry_tags"]:
                if tag in tag_averages:
                    actual_vals.append(tag_averages[tag])

            if actual_vals and nominal > 0:
                avg_actual = sum(actual_vals) / len(actual_vals)
                asset_actual_avg_map[asset_code] = round(avg_actual, 1)

                if asset_code == "CHILLER_COOL":
                    # Cooling: actual temp <= nominal target is 100% conforming
                    if avg_actual <= nominal:
                        perf = 1.0
                    else:
                        perf = max(0.0, min(1.0, nominal / avg_actual))
                else:
                    # Speed, Flow, Heat: actual / nominal
                    perf = max(0.0, min(1.0, avg_actual / nominal))
                asset_performance_map[asset_code] = round(perf, 4)
            else:
                asset_performance_map[asset_code] = 0.0
                asset_actual_avg_map[asset_code] = 0.0

    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error querying dbo.PLC_Datalog for performance: {e}")
        for asset_code in EQUIPMENT_DESIGN_SPECS:
            asset_performance_map[asset_code] = 0.0
            asset_actual_avg_map[asset_code] = 0.0

    # Overall Line Performance
    active_perfs = [p for p in asset_performance_map.values() if p > 0.0]
    if active_perfs:
        overall_performance = sum(active_perfs) / len(active_perfs)
    else:
        # Zero active production telemetry -> 0.0%
        overall_performance = 0.0

    return {
        "performance": round(overall_performance, 4),
        "asset_performance_map": asset_performance_map,
        "asset_actual_avg_map": asset_actual_avg_map
    }


def calculate_quality_metrics(conn, shift_hours: int = 8) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes Quality (Q):
    Queries dbo.Quality_Result and dbo.ProductionReport:
    Q = Count(Status = 'PASS') / Total Count of inspected batches.
    """
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    total_inspected = 0
    passed_count = 0
    shift_output_liters = 0.0

    try:
        # 1. Check dbo.ProductionReport
        rep_query = """
            SELECT 
                COUNT(*) as TotalBatches,
                SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(ISNULL(QualityStatus, '')) IN ('PASS', 'CONFORMING') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as PassedBatches,
                ISNULL(SUM(CAST(TotalProduced AS FLOAT)), 0.0) as TotalLiters
            FROM dbo.ProductionReport
            WHERE CreatedTime >= DATEADD(HOUR, -?, GETDATE())
        """
        cursor.execute(rep_query, (shift_hours,))
        rep_row = cursor.fetchone()
        if rep_row and rep_row[0] > 0:
            total_inspected = int(rep_row[0] or 0)
            passed_count = int(rep_row[1] or 0)
            shift_output_liters = float(rep_row[2] or 0.0)

        # 2. Check dbo.Quality_Result if production reports were empty
        if total_inspected == 0:
            qr_query = """
                SELECT 
                    COUNT(*) as TotalTests,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN UPPER(ISNULL(Evaluation, '')) IN ('PASS', 'CONFORMING') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as PassedTests
                FROM dbo.Quality_Result
                WHERE CheckedAt >= DATEADD(HOUR, -?, GETDATE())
            """
            cursor.execute(qr_query, (shift_hours,))
            qr_row = cursor.fetchone()
            if qr_row and qr_row[0] > 0:
                total_inspected = int(qr_row[0] or 0)
                passed_count = int(qr_row[1] or 0)

        # 3. Check dbo.Orders completed volume if shift output is still 0
        if shift_output_liters == 0.0:
            ord_query = """
                SELECT ISNULL(SUM(CAST(ISNULL(ActualVolume, TargetVolume) AS FLOAT)), 0.0)
                FROM dbo.Orders
                WHERE UPPER(Status) IN ('COMPLETED', 'FINISHED', 'DONE')
                AND (ActualEndTime >= DATEADD(HOUR, -?, GETDATE()) OR ScheduledStartTime >= DATEADD(HOUR, -?, GETDATE()))
            """
            cursor.execute(ord_query, (shift_hours, shift_hours))
            ord_row = cursor.fetchone()
            if ord_row:
                shift_output_liters = float(ord_row[0] or 0.0)

    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Error querying quality results: {e}")

    if total_inspected > 0:
        quality_rate = passed_count / float(total_inspected)
    else:
        # Zero inspected batches in shift -> 0.0%
        quality_rate = 0.0

    return {
        "quality": round(quality_rate, 4),
        "total_inspected": total_inspected,
        "passed_count": passed_count,
        "failed_count": max(0, total_inspected - passed_count),
        "shift_output_liters": round(shift_output_liters, 1)
    }


def get_oee_summary(shift_hours: int = 8) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Returns Executive OEE Summary for the active shift without mock fallbacks."""
    shift_meta = get_current_shift_meta()
    conn = None

    try:
        conn = db_connector.get_connection(timeout=3)
        if not conn:
            raise RuntimeError("Database unreachable")

        avail_data = calculate_availability_metrics(conn, shift_hours)
        perf_data = calculate_performance_metrics(conn, shift_hours)
        qual_data = calculate_quality_metrics(conn, shift_hours)

        a = avail_data["availability"]
        p = perf_data["performance"]
        q = qual_data["quality"]

        overall_oee = round(a * p * q, 4)
        overall_oee_pct = round(overall_oee * 100.0, 1)
        a_pct = round(a * 100.0, 1)
        p_pct = round(p * 100.0, 1)
        q_pct = round(q * 100.0, 1)

        # Industry Standard Benchmark Classification (ISA-95)
        if overall_oee >= 0.85:
            benchmark = "WORLD_CLASS"
            benchmark_label = "World Class Benchmark (>=85%)"
            badge_color = "emerald"
        elif overall_oee >= 0.70:
            benchmark = "TYPICAL"
            benchmark_label = "Industry Typical (70% - 84%)"
            badge_color = "sky"
        elif overall_oee > 0.0:
            benchmark = "NEEDS_IMPROVEMENT"
            benchmark_label = "Needs Improvement (<70%)"
            badge_color = "amber"
        else:
            benchmark = "STANDBY"
            benchmark_label = "Cold Standby (0.0%)"
            badge_color = "slate"

        # Record snapshot in dbo.OEE_Report if active shift has non-zero records
        if overall_oee > 0.0:
            try:
                cur = conn.cursor()
                cur.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO dbo.OEE_Report (Shift_Name, Availability, Performance, Quality, Overall_OEE, Calculated_At)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, GETDATE())
                    """,
                    (shift_meta["shift_title"], a_pct, p_pct, q_pct, overall_oee_pct)
                )
                conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                pass

        active_batch_count = 0
        try:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.Production_Orders WHERE Status IN ('RUNNING', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'STARTING')")
            b_row = cur.fetchone()
            if b_row and b_row[0] is not None:
                active_batch_count = int(b_row[0])
        except Exception:
            pass

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "line_oee": overall_oee_pct,
            "oee": overall_oee_pct,
            "overall_oee": overall_oee_pct,
            "availability": a_pct,
            "performance": p_pct,
            "quality": q_pct,
            "quality_rate": q_pct,
            "shift_output_volume": qual_data["shift_output_liters"],
            "shift_target_volume": 5000.0,
            "active_batches": active_batch_count,
            "operating_time_minutes": avail_data["operating_minutes"],
            "downtime_minutes": avail_data["downtime_minutes"],
            "shift_output_liters": qual_data["shift_output_liters"],
            "total_inspected_batches": qual_data["total_inspected"],
            "passed_batches": qual_data["passed_count"],
            "failed_batches": qual_data["failed_count"],
            "planned_shift_minutes": shift_meta["planned_shift_minutes"],
            "elapsed_shift_minutes": shift_meta["elapsed_shift_minutes"],
            "benchmark": benchmark,
            "benchmark_label": benchmark_label,
            "badge_color": badge_color,
            "shift_name": shift_meta["shift_title"],
            "shift_window": shift_meta["window"],
            "shift_leader": shift_meta["shift_leader"],
            "calculated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "is_standby": overall_oee == 0.0
        }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error computing OEE summary: {e}")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": str(e),
            "line_oee": 0.0,
            "oee": 0.0,
            "overall_oee": 0.0,
            "availability": 0.0,
            "performance": 0.0,
            "quality": 0.0,
            "quality_rate": 0.0,
            "shift_output_volume": 0.0,
            "shift_target_volume": 5000.0,
            "active_batches": 0,
            "operating_time_minutes": 0.0,
            "downtime_minutes": 0.0,
            "shift_output_liters": 0.0,
            "benchmark": "STANDBY",
            "benchmark_label": "Cold Standby (0.0%)",
            "badge_color": "slate",
            "shift_name": shift_meta["shift_title"],
            "shift_window": shift_meta["window"],
            "shift_leader": shift_meta["shift_leader"],
            "calculated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "is_standby": True
        }
    finally:
        if conn:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass


def get_equipment_oee_breakdown(shift_hours: int = 8) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Returns granular OEE and Six Big Losses classification for key production assets:
    - PUMP_INLET
    - AGITATOR_MIX
    - HEATER_UHT
    - CHILLER_COOL
    """
    conn = None
    results = []

    try:
        conn = db_connector.get_connection(timeout=3)
        if not conn:
            raise RuntimeError("Database unreachable")

        avail_data = calculate_availability_metrics(conn, shift_hours)
        perf_data = calculate_performance_metrics(conn, shift_hours)
        qual_data = calculate_quality_metrics(conn, shift_hours)
        line_q = qual_data["quality"]

        # Read equipment master details
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT EquipmentID, EquipmentCode, EquipmentName, CurrentStatus, Runtime_Hours FROM dbo.Equipment_Master")
        eq_db_map = {}
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            code = str(row[1]).upper()
            eq_db_map[code] = {
                "id": int(row[0]),
                "code": code,
                "name": str(row[2]),
                "status": str(row[3] or "STOP").upper(),
                "runtime_hours": float(row[4] or 0.0)
            }

        target_assets = [
            "PUMP_INLET_MILK",
            "PUMP_INLET_SUGAR",
            "PUMP_INLET_ADDITIVE",
            "AGITATOR_MIX",
            "HEATER_UHT",
            "CHILLER_COOL",
            "PUMP_OUTLET",
        ]

        for asset_code in target_assets:
            specs = EQUIPMENT_DESIGN_SPECS.get(asset_code, {})
            db_info = eq_db_map.get(asset_code, {
                "id": 0,
                "code": asset_code,
                "name": specs.get("name", asset_code),
                "status": "STOP",
                "runtime_hours": 0.0
            })

            cur_status = db_info["status"]
            op_secs = avail_data["equipment_operating_map"].get(asset_code, 0)
            dt_secs = avail_data["equipment_downtime_map"].get(asset_code, 0)

            total_secs = op_secs + dt_secs
            if total_secs > 0:
                asset_a = op_secs / float(total_secs)
            else:
                asset_a = 0.0

            asset_p = perf_data["asset_performance_map"].get(asset_code, 0.0)
            actual_val = perf_data["asset_actual_avg_map"].get(asset_code, 0.0)

            # Asset Quality rate follows line quality if processing
            asset_q = line_q if (asset_a > 0 or cur_status == 'RUNNING') else 0.0

            asset_oee = round(asset_a * asset_p * asset_q, 4)
            asset_oee_pct = round(asset_oee * 100.0, 1)

            # Calculate Health % based on runtime vs maintenance limit
            limit_hours = float(specs.get("maintenance_limit_hours", 2000.0))
            rt_hours = db_info["runtime_hours"]
            if limit_hours > 0:
                health_pct = max(0.0, min(100.0, 100.0 - (rt_hours / limit_hours * 100.0)))
            else:
                health_pct = 100.0

            # Six Big Losses Classification (TPM / ISA-95)
            if cur_status == "FAULT":
                loss_category = "UNPLANNED_BREAKDOWN"
                loss_label = "Unplanned Breakdown (Sá»± cá»‘ báº¥t thÆ°á»ng)"
            elif dt_secs > op_secs and dt_secs > 0:
                loss_category = "SETUP_ADJUSTMENT"
                loss_label = "Setup & Idle Downtime (Dá»«ng Ä‘iá»u chá»‰nh)"
            elif 0.0 < asset_p < 0.85:
                loss_category = "REDUCED_SPEED"
                loss_label = "Reduced Speed Loss (Tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ suy giáº£m)"
            elif 0.0 < asset_q < 0.95:
                loss_category = "PROCESS_DEFECT"
                loss_label = "Quality Defect / Reject (Sáº£n pháº©m lá»—i)"
            elif cur_status in ("STOP", "IDLE") and asset_oee == 0.0:
                loss_category = "IDLE_STANDBY"
                loss_label = "Cold Standby / Inactive (Chá» lá»‡nh)"
            else:
                loss_category = "OPTIMAL_RUNNING"
                loss_label = "Nominal Running (Váº­n hĂ nh chuáº©n)"

            results.append({
                "equipment_id": asset_code,
                "equipment_code": asset_code,
                "equipment_name": specs.get("name", db_info["name"]),
                "equipment_type": specs.get("type", "Process Asset"),
                "status": cur_status,
                "current_status": cur_status,
                "oee": asset_oee_pct,
                "availability": round(asset_a * 100.0, 1),
                "performance": round(asset_p * 100.0, 1),
                "quality": round(asset_q * 100.0, 1),
                "actual_value": actual_val,
                "nominal_setpoint": specs.get("nominal_setpoint", 0.0),
                "unit": specs.get("unit", "EU"),
                "operating_minutes": round(op_secs / 60.0, 1),
                "downtime_minutes": round(dt_secs / 60.0, 1),
                "runtime_hours": round(rt_hours, 1),
                "health_percent": round(health_pct, 1),
                "loss_reason": loss_label,
                "loss_category": loss_category,
                "loss_label": loss_label
            })

        return results

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Error computing equipment OEE breakdown: {e}")
        # Return graceful standby list
        for code, specs in EQUIPMENT_DESIGN_SPECS.items():
            results.append({
                "equipment_id": 0,
                "equipment_code": code,
                "equipment_name": specs["name"],
                "equipment_type": specs["type"],
                "current_status": "STOP",
                "oee": 0.0,
                "availability": 0.0,
                "performance": 0.0,
                "quality": 0.0,
                "actual_value": 0.0,
                "nominal_setpoint": specs["nominal_setpoint"],
                "unit": specs["unit"],
                "operating_minutes": 0.0,
                "downtime_minutes": 0.0,
                "runtime_hours": 0.0,
                "health_percent": 100.0,
                "loss_category": "IDLE_STANDBY",
                "loss_label": "Cold Standby / Inactive (Chá» lá»‡nh)"
            })
        return results
    finally:
        if conn:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass


def get_oee_losses_breakdown(shift_hours: int = 8) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes Pareto breakdown of top OEE losses:
    1. Unplanned Downtime (Availability Loss)
    2. Speed Loss (Performance Loss)
    3. Quality Defect (Quality Loss)
    Includes an 8-hour shift trendline comparing Target OEE (85%) vs Actual OEE.
    """
    summary = get_oee_summary(shift_hours)
    a_pct = summary["availability"]
    p_pct = summary["performance"]
    q_pct = summary["quality"]
    oee_pct = summary["oee"]

    a = a_pct / 100.0
    p = p_pct / 100.0
    q = q_pct / 100.0

    if oee_pct > 0.0:
        downtime_loss_pct = round((1.0 - a) * 100.0, 1)
        speed_loss_pct = round(a * (1.0 - p) * 100.0, 1)
        quality_loss_pct = round(a * p * (1.0 - q) * 100.0, 1)
    else:
        # In standby, losses are not active penalties
        downtime_loss_pct = 0.0
        speed_loss_pct = 0.0
        quality_loss_pct = 0.0

    losses_pareto = [
        {
            "category": "Unplanned Downtime",
            "category_vn": "Dá»«ng mĂ¡y ngoĂ i káº¿ hoáº¡ch",
            "loss_percent": downtime_loss_pct,
            "duration_minutes": summary["downtime_minutes"],
            "impact": "High" if downtime_loss_pct > 10.0 else "Normal",
            "description": "Equipment breakdown, emergency stop, or valve failure during active batch."
        },
        {
            "category": "Speed Loss",
            "category_vn": "Tá»•n tháº¥t giáº£m tá»‘c Ä‘á»™",
            "loss_percent": speed_loss_pct,
            "duration_minutes": round(summary["operating_time_minutes"] * (1.0 - p), 1) if p > 0 else 0.0,
            "impact": "Medium" if speed_loss_pct > 5.0 else "Normal",
            "description": "Asset operation below nominal design capacity (e.g. throttled pump/agitator)."
        },
        {
            "category": "Quality Defect",
            "category_vn": "Pháº¿ pháº©m & Sáº£n pháº©m lá»—i",
            "loss_percent": quality_loss_pct,
            "duration_minutes": 0.0,
            "impact": "Critical" if quality_loss_pct > 2.0 else "Normal",
            "description": "Batches quarantined or failing HACCP/ISO specifications."
        }
    ]

    # Generate 8-point hourly trendline across active 8-hour shift
    now = datetime.now()
    hourly_trend = []
    for i in range(7, -1, -1):
        slot_time = now - timedelta(hours=i)
        time_label = slot_time.strftime("%H:00")
        # In cold standby without historical batches, actual is 0.0
        actual_val = oee_pct if i == 0 else 0.0
        hourly_trend.append({
            "hour": time_label,
            "target_oee": 85.0,  # World class benchmark target
            "actual_oee": actual_val
        })

    return {
        "status": "success",
        "unplanned_downtime_min": summary["downtime_minutes"],
        "speed_loss_percent": speed_loss_pct,
        "quality_defect_percent": quality_loss_pct,
        "top_loss_category": "Unplanned Downtime" if downtime_loss_pct > 0 else "None (System Idle)",
        "overall_oee": oee_pct,
        "losses": losses_pareto,
        "hourly_trend": hourly_trend,
        "six_big_losses": [
            {"name": "Equipment Failure / Breakdown", "type": "Availability", "value_pct": downtime_loss_pct},
            {"name": "Setup & Adjustments", "type": "Availability", "value_pct": 0.0},
            {"name": "Idling & Minor Stops", "type": "Performance", "value_pct": round(speed_loss_pct * 0.4, 1)},
            {"name": "Reduced Speed", "type": "Performance", "value_pct": round(speed_loss_pct * 0.6, 1)},
            {"name": "Process Defects / Rejects", "type": "Quality", "value_pct": quality_loss_pct},
            {"name": "Reduced Yield / Startup Rejects", "type": "Quality", "value_pct": 0.0}
        ]
    }

FILE: D:\MES\backend\opc_routes.py

"""
/* Hallmark Â· component: OperationsOpcRoutes Â· register: industrial-workbench
 * standards: ISA-88 Batch Control / ISA-95 MOM / ISA-101 HMI
 * strict: Central OPC UA Gateway Session Reuse (Port 49320)
 */

FastAPI RESTful Routes for Operations Dual-Run SIMIT Twin Supervision
and Real-Time Deviation Benchmarking.
"""

import sys
import os
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel

# Ensure backend directory in sys.path
backend_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if backend_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, backend_dir)

try:
    from . import batch_engine
except (ImportError, ValueError):
    import batch_engine

logger = logging.getLogger("OperationsOpcRoutes")

router = APIRouter(tags=["Operations & Dual-Run SIMIT Twin Engine"])

# ============================================================================
# PAYLOAD MODELS
# ============================================================================

class ExecutionModePayload(BaseModel):
    execution_mode: str  # mode1_physical | mode2_simit | mode3_dual

class DispatchRecipePayload(BaseModel):
    order_code: str
    execution_mode: Optional[str] = "mode3_dual"
    recipe_name: Optional[str] = "Fresh Pasteurized Milk"
    target_volume: Optional[float] = 1000.0
    setpoints: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None

class StartBatchPayload(BaseModel):
    order_code: Optional[str] = None
    execution_mode: Optional[str] = None

class CompleteCellPayload(BaseModel):
    cell: str  # 'cell1' or 'cell2'

# ============================================================================
# ROUTE ENDPOINTS
# ============================================================================

@router.get("/api/operations/dual-run/status")
async def get_dual_run_status():
    """
    Returns live Dual-Run benchmark status:
    - Active execution mode (Physical / SIMIT / Dual-Run)
    - Independent completion flags and elapsed cycle times
    - Recipe handshake state
    """
    try:
        completion_data = batch_engine.evaluate_completion()
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "state": batch_engine.state.batch_state,
            "execution_mode": batch_engine.state.execution_mode,
            "order_code": batch_engine.state.order_code,
            "recipe_name": batch_engine.state.recipe_name,
            "cell1_ack": batch_engine.state.cell1_ack,
            "cell2_ack": batch_engine.state.cell2_ack,
            "cell1_complete": batch_engine.state.cell1_complete,
            "cell2_complete": batch_engine.state.cell2_complete,
            "t_start_cell1": batch_engine.state.t_start_cell1,
            "t_start_cell2": batch_engine.state.t_start_cell2,
            "t_end_cell1": batch_engine.state.t_end_cell1,
            "t_end_cell2": batch_engine.state.t_end_cell2,
            **completion_data
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[get_dual_run_status] Fallback: {exc}")
        return {
            "status": "warning",
            "state": batch_engine.state.batch_state,
            "execution_mode": batch_engine.state.execution_mode,
            "order_code": batch_engine.state.order_code,
            "recipe_name": batch_engine.state.recipe_name,
            "cell1_ack": False,
            "cell2_ack": False,
            "cell1_complete": False,
            "cell2_complete": False,
            "duration_cell1": "--",
            "duration_cell2": "--"
        }

@router.post("/api/operations/dual-run/mode")
async def set_dual_run_mode(payload: ExecutionModePayload):
    """
    Switches active execution mode:
    - Mode 1: mode1_physical
    - Mode 2: mode2_simit
    - Mode 3: mode3_dual
    """
    try:
        valid_modes = ("mode1_physical", "mode2_simit", "mode3_dual")
        mode = str(payload.execution_mode).strip()
        if mode not in valid_modes:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=400,
                detail=f"Invalid execution mode '{mode}'. Must be one of: {valid_modes}"
            )
        batch_engine.state.execution_mode = mode
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "execution_mode": mode
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[set_dual_run_mode] Error: {exc}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))

@router.post("/api/operations/dual-run/dispatch-recipe")
async def dispatch_recipe_endpoint(payload: DispatchRecipePayload):
    """
    ISA-88 Recipe Downlink:
    - Mode 1: Writes SP setpoints to operations.cell1 tags only
    - Mode 2: Writes SP setpoints to operations.cell2_simit tags only
    - Mode 3: Concurrently writes to BOTH Cell 1 and Cell 2 tags
    - Verifies Ack_Recipe_Loaded flags
    """
    try:
        res = await batch_engine.download_recipe(
            order_code=payload.order_code,
            execution_mode=payload.execution_mode or "mode3_dual",
            recipe_name=payload.recipe_name or "Fresh Pasteurized Milk",
            target_volume=payload.target_volume or 1000.0,
            setpoints=payload.setpoints
        )
        return res
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[dispatch_recipe_endpoint] Error: {exc}")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": str(exc),
            "order_code": payload.order_code,
            "ready_to_start": False
        }

@router.post("/api/operations/dual-run/start-batch")
async def start_batch_endpoint(payload: StartBatchPayload):
    """
    Triggers batch execution:
    - Emits 500ms CMD_Start pulse to active cell(s)
    - Records start timestamps: t_start_cell1 and t_start_cell2
    - Activates runtime timers
    """
    try:
        res = await batch_engine.start_batch(
            order_code=payload.order_code,
            execution_mode=payload.execution_mode
        )
        return res
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[start_batch_endpoint] Error: {exc}")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": str(exc),
            "batch_state": batch_engine.state.batch_state
        }

@router.get("/api/operations/dual-run/deviation-matrix")
async def get_deviation_matrix_endpoint():
    """
    Real-Time Digital Twin Deviation Matrix:
    - Parameter SP vs Physical PV vs SIMIT Twin PV
    - Absolute delta Î” = |R - S|
    - Status evaluation: MATCHED, DEVIATING, TRACKING, CHÆ¯A ĂNH Xáº 
    - Twin Model Fidelity Index & Cycle Time Comparison
    """
    try:
        return batch_engine.get_deviation_matrix()
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[get_deviation_matrix_endpoint] Error: {exc}")
        return {
            "execution_mode": batch_engine.state.execution_mode,
            "batch_state": batch_engine.state.batch_state,
            "matrix": [],
            "benchmark_summary": None
        }

@router.post("/api/operations/dual-run/complete-cell")
async def complete_cell_endpoint(payload: CompleteCellPayload):
    """
    Manually or signal-driven mark Cell 1 or Cell 2 complete.
    In Dual-Run mode, overall order finalizes strictly when Physical Cell 1 completes.
    """
    try:
        cell = str(payload.cell).strip().lower()
        if cell in ("cell1", "cell1_physical", "physical"):
            res = batch_engine.evaluate_completion(simulated_cell1=True)
        elif cell in ("cell2", "cell2_simit", "simit", "twin"):
            res = batch_engine.evaluate_completion(simulated_cell2=True)
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cell must be 'cell1' or 'cell2'")
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "cell": cell,
            **res
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[complete_cell_endpoint] Error: {exc}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc))

@router.post("/api/operations/dual-run/reset")
async def reset_dual_run_endpoint():
    """Resets the dual-run state machine back to IDLE."""
    try:
        batch_engine.state.reset()
        return {
            "status": "success",
            "message": "Dual-run benchmark state reset to IDLE."
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"[reset_dual_run_endpoint] Error: {exc}")
        return {
            "status": "error",
            "message": str(exc)
        }

"""
pdm_feature_extractor.py
Module trĂ­ch xuáº¥t Ä‘áº·c trÆ°ng chuá»—i thá»i gian (Feature Engineering) cho báº£o trĂ¬ dá»± Ä‘oĂ¡n.
TĂ­nh toĂ¡n cĂ¡c chá»‰ sá»‘: RMS, Peak-to-Peak, Crest Factor, Kurtosis, Skewness, Variance.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats
from typing import Dict, Any, List

class TimeSeriesFeatureExtractor:
    @staticmethod
    def extract_scalar_features(signal_values: List[float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        TrĂ­ch xuáº¥t bá»™ Ä‘áº·c trÆ°ng thá»‘ng kĂª miá»n thá»i gian tá»« máº£ng sá»‘ liá»‡u cáº£m biáº¿n.
        Xá»­ lĂ½ an toĂ n cĂ¡c trÆ°á»ng há»£p máº£ng rá»—ng, máº£ng háº±ng sá»‘ hoáº·c dá»¯ liá»‡u nhiá»…u.
        """
        arr = np.array(signal_values, dtype=np.float64)
        n_samples = len(arr)

        if n_samples == 0:
            return {
                "mean": 0.0, "std": 0.0, "rms": 0.0, "peak_to_peak": 0.0,
                "crest_factor": 0.0, "kurtosis": 0.0, "skewness": 0.0, "sample_count": 0
            }

        mean_val = float(np.mean(arr))
        std_val = float(np.std(arr))
        
        # 1. TĂ­nh giĂ¡ trá»‹ hiá»‡u dá»¥ng RMS (Root Mean Square) - Äáº·c trÆ°ng cho cĂ´ng suáº¥t tiĂªu hao
        rms_val = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(arr ** 2)))
        
        # 2. BiĂªn Ä‘á»™ Ä‘á»‰nh-Ä‘á»‰nh Peak-to-Peak (P2P) - PhĂ¡t hiá»‡n xung va Ä‘áº­p cÆ¡ khĂ­
        p2p_val = float(np.ptp(arr))
        peak_val = float(np.max(np.abs(arr)))

        # 3. Há»‡ sá»‘ Ä‘á»‰nh Crest Factor = Peak / RMS (Tá»· lá»‡ xung sá»‘c trĂªn nÄƒng lÆ°á»£ng ná»n)
        crest_factor = float(peak_val / (rms_val + 1e-8))

        # 4. Äá»™ nhá»n Kurtosis & Äá»™ lá»‡ch Skewness - Nháº­n diá»‡n mĂ©o dáº¡ng phĂ¢n phá»‘i do mĂ²n cÆ¡ khĂ­
        if std_val < 1e-6:
            kurtosis_val = 0.0
            skewness_val = 0.0
        else:
            kurtosis_val = float(stats.kurtosis(arr, fisher=True)) # Fisher = 0 cho phĂ¢n phá»‘i chuáº©n
            skewness_val = float(stats.skew(arr))

        return {
            "mean": round(mean_val, 4),
            "std": round(std_val, 4),
            "rms": round(rms_val, 4),
            "peak_to_peak": round(p2p_val, 4),
            "crest_factor": round(crest_factor, 4),
            "kurtosis": round(kurtosis_val, 4),
            "skewness": round(skewness_val, 4),
            "sample_count": n_samples
        }

    @staticmethod
    def detect_micro_fluctuations(signal_values: List[float], threshold_sigma: float = 2.5) -> float:
        """
        PhĂ¡t hiá»‡n tá»· lá»‡ vi dao Ä‘á»™ng báº¥t thÆ°á»ng (Micro-fluctuations) vÆ°á»£t ngÆ°á»¡ng 2.5 sigma.
        Dáº¥u hiá»‡u Ä‘iá»ƒn hĂ¬nh cá»§a rÆ¡ trá»¥c hoáº·c mĂ²n bi giai Ä‘oáº¡n khá»Ÿi phĂ¡t.
        """
        arr = np.array(signal_values, dtype=np.float64)
        if len(arr) < 5:
            return 0.0

        mean = np.mean(arr)
        std = np.std(arr)
        if std < 1e-5:
            return 0.0

        z_scores = np.abs((arr - mean) / std)
        outliers_ratio = float(np.mean(z_scores > threshold_sigma))
        return round(outliers_ratio, 4)

"""
SCADA OPC UA Gateway Bridge Service (ISA-95 Level 2 <-> Level 3 MES)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
Compliance:
- ISA-95 Level 2 Supervisory SCADA Gateway (Siemens WinCC Unified PC Runtime)
- ISA-95 Level 3 Manufacturing Execution System (MES)
- Exclusively communicates with Level 2 SCADA at port 4890. Never directly touches Level 1 PLCs.

Target Endpoint:
- opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4890

Target SCADA Tags:
1. Temperature: ns=3;s="nhiet do" (Float / Analog)
2. Pump Status: ns=3;s="pump"     (Boolean / Discrete)
Auto-resolves to WinCC Unified PC Runtime address space.
"""

import asyncio
import logging
import math
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

# Initialize logger
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s"
)
logger = logging.getLogger("scada_gateway")

# Global Telemetry & State Storage
SCADA_ENDPOINT = "opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4890"

# Target Tag definitions with WinCC Unified auto-resolution fallbacks
TAG_CANDIDATES = {
    "temp": [
        'ns=3;s="nhiet do"',
        'ns=3;s=nhiet do',
        'ns=1;s=1.257.1.0.0.0', # WinCC Unified RT physical tag
    ],
    "pump": [
        'ns=3;s="pump"',
        'ns=3;s=pump',
        'ns=1;s=1.256.1.0.0.0', # WinCC Unified RT physical tag
    ]
}

telemetry_store: Dict[str, Any] = {
    "scada_connected": False,
    "nhiet_do": 0.0,
    "pump_status": False,
    "last_updated": None,
    "endpoint": SCADA_ENDPOINT,
    "poll_count": 0,
    "last_error": None,
    "simulation_mode": False,
    "active_nodes": {
        "temp_node": None,
        "pump_node": None,
    }
}

# Active OPC UA client reference for write operations
active_opc_client = None
opc_write_lock = asyncio.Lock()
resolved_nodes = {
    "temp": None,
    "pump": None,
}

app = FastAPI(
    title="MES SCADA OPC UA Gateway Bridge",
    description="ISA-95 Level 3 MOM to Level 2 WinCC Unified SCADA Gateway Bridge",
    version="1.0.0",
)

# Enable CORS for MES Frontend (Vite running on port 3000, 5173, etc.)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


async def discover_nodes_by_browsename(client):
    """
    Auto-searches the WinCC Unified HmiRuntime object hierarchy if direct candidates fail.
    Finds nodes whose browse name matches 'nhiet do' or 'pump'.
    """
    discovered = {}
    objects = client.nodes.objects

    async def scan(node, depth=0):
        if depth > 4 or ("temp" in discovered and "pump" in discovered):
            return
        try:
            children = await node.get_children()
            for child in children:
                try:
                    bn = await child.read_browse_name()
                    name_lower = bn.to_string().lower()
                    if "nhiet do" in name_lower and "temp" not in discovered:
                        discovered["temp"] = child
                        logger.info(f"Discovered Temperature node via browse: {bn.to_string()} -> {child.nodeid.to_string()}")
                    elif "pump" in name_lower and "pump" not in discovered:
                        discovered["pump"] = child
                        logger.info(f"Discovered Pump node via browse: {bn.to_string()} -> {child.nodeid.to_string()}")
                    await scan(child, depth + 1)
                except Exception:
                    continue
        except Exception:
            pass

    await scan(objects, 0)
    return discovered


async def resolve_tag(client, tag_key: str):
    """
    Resolves an OPC UA tag using direct candidate list or hierarchical discovery.
    """
    candidates = TAG_CANDIDATES.get(tag_key, [])
    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            node = client.get_node(candidate)
            # Test reading value
            val = await node.read_value()
            logger.info(f"Resolved [{tag_key}] using NodeId: {candidate} (current value = {val})")
            return node
        except Exception:
            continue

    # Try automatic tree search
    logger.info(f"Direct candidates for [{tag_key}] did not match. Performing hierarchical search...")
    discovered = await discover_nodes_by_browsename(client)
    if tag_key in discovered:
        return discovered[tag_key]

    # Fallback to first candidate representation
    return client.get_node(candidates[0])


async def scada_polling_worker():
    """
    Continuous background loop connecting to SCADA OPC UA Server at opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4890
    Polls 'nhiet do' and 'pump' every 500ms using Anonymous/Guest mode.
    Handles graceful auto-reconnects and fallback simulation when offline.
    """
    global active_opc_client, resolved_nodes

    try:
        from asyncua import Client, ua
    except ImportError:
        logger.error("asyncua library not installed! Run: py -m pip install asyncua")
        return

    sim_tick = 0

    while True:
        try:
            logger.info(f"Attempting connection to SCADA Gateway: {SCADA_ENDPOINT} (Anonymous/Guest)...")
            
            async with Client(url=SCADA_ENDPOINT, timeout=3) as client:
                active_opc_client = client
                logger.info(f"CONNECTED to WinCC Unified SCADA Gateway at {SCADA_ENDPOINT}")
                telemetry_store["scada_connected"] = True
                telemetry_store["last_error"] = None

                # Locate nodes for Temperature and Pump
                temp_node = await resolve_tag(client, "temp")
                pump_node = await resolve_tag(client, "pump")
                resolved_nodes["temp"] = temp_node
                resolved_nodes["pump"] = pump_node

                telemetry_store["active_nodes"]["temp_node"] = temp_node.nodeid.to_string()
                telemetry_store["active_nodes"]["pump_node"] = pump_node.nodeid.to_string()

                logger.info(f"Active Polling Targets: Temp={telemetry_store['active_nodes']['temp_node']}, Pump={telemetry_store['active_nodes']['pump_node']}")

                while True:
                    start_time = time.time()
                    try:
                        async with opc_write_lock:
                            # Read Temperature (Analog / Float)
                            temp_val = await temp_node.read_value()
                            
                            # Read Pump (Discrete / Boolean)
                            pump_val = await pump_node.read_value()

                        telemetry_store["nhiet_do"] = round(float(temp_val), 2)
                        telemetry_store["pump_status"] = bool(pump_val)
                        telemetry_store["scada_connected"] = True
                        telemetry_store["poll_count"] += 1
                        telemetry_store["last_updated"] = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3]
                        telemetry_store["last_error"] = None

                    except Exception as poll_err:
                        logger.warning(f"OPC UA Tag Read Exception: {poll_err}")
                        raise poll_err

                    elapsed = time.time() - start_time
                    sleep_time = max(0.05, 0.5 - elapsed)
                    await asyncio.sleep(sleep_time)

        except Exception as conn_err:
            telemetry_store["scada_connected"] = False
            telemetry_store["last_error"] = str(conn_err)
            active_opc_client = None

            # Strict Physical Commissioning: Never synthesize telemetry when offline
            telemetry_store["nhiet_do"] = 0.0
            telemetry_store["last_updated"] = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3]

            logger.info(f"SCADA Gateway connection state: {conn_err}. Retrying in 2 seconds...")
            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)


@app.on_event("startup")
async def on_startup():
    logger.info("Initializing SCADA OPC UA Background Poller (500ms cycle)...")
    asyncio.create_task(scada_polling_worker())

@app.get("/api/scada/live")
async def get_scada_live():
    """
    Returns real-time telemetry polled every 500ms from the SCADA Gateway.
    Strictly follows requirement:
    { "scada_connected": bool, "nhiet_do": float, "pump_status": bool }
    """
    return {
        "scada_connected": telemetry_store["scada_connected"],
        "nhiet_do": telemetry_store["nhiet_do"],
        "pump_status": telemetry_store["pump_status"],
        "timestamp": telemetry_store["last_updated"] or datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
        "endpoint": telemetry_store["endpoint"],
        "poll_count": telemetry_store["poll_count"],
        "simulation_mode": telemetry_store["simulation_mode"],
        "active_nodes": telemetry_store["active_nodes"],
        "last_error": telemetry_store["last_error"],
    }


@app.post("/api/scada/pump/{state}")
async def set_pump_state(state: str):
    """
    Writes boolean state directly to the WinCC Unified SCADA OPC UA tag for pump.
    Accepts: true/false, 1/0, start/stop, on/off.
    """
    global active_opc_client, resolved_nodes

    normalized = state.strip().lower()
    if normalized in ["true", "1", "start", "on", "run", "running"]:
        target_val = True
    elif normalized in ["false", "0", "stop", "off", "idle", "stopped"]:
        target_val = False
    else:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Invalid pump state '{state}'. Expected 'true'/'false' or 'start'/'stop'."
        )

    logger.info(f"MES Level 3 Command -> Set Pump state to {target_val}")

    # Write directly through live OPC UA session
    if active_opc_client is not None and telemetry_store["scada_connected"]:
        try:
            from asyncua import ua
            async with opc_write_lock:
                node = resolved_nodes.get("pump") or await resolve_tag(active_opc_client, "pump")
                # Strict OPC UA Boolean write
                dv = ua.DataValue(ua.Variant(target_val, ua.VariantType.Boolean))
                await node.write_value(dv)
                telemetry_store["pump_status"] = target_val
                logger.info(f"OPC UA Write Successful: {node.nodeid.to_string()} = {target_val}")

            return {
                "success": True,
                "pump_status": target_val,
                "scada_connected": True,
                "node_id": node.nodeid.to_string(),
                "message": f"Successfully updated SCADA OPC UA pump tag to {target_val}",
            }
        except Exception as write_err:
            logger.error(f"Failed writing to SCADA OPC UA: {write_err}")
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=502,
                detail=f"Failed writing to SCADA OPC UA tag: {str(write_err)}"
            )

    # Simulation fallback mode
    if telemetry_store["simulation_mode"]:
        telemetry_store["pump_status"] = target_val
        return {
            "success": True,
            "pump_status": target_val,
            "scada_connected": False,
            "simulation_mode": True,
            "message": f"[Simulation Mode] Pump status locally toggled to {target_val}",
        }

    # Offline notification
    telemetry_store["pump_status"] = target_val
    return {
        "success": False,
        "pump_status": target_val,
        "scada_connected": False,
        "message": "SCADA Gateway at opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4890 is currently OFFLINE. (Command recorded in MES buffer).",
    }


@app.post("/api/scada/simulation-toggle")
async def toggle_simulation(request: Request):
    """
    Toggle or set simulation mode for presentation rehearsal when WinCC Unified is not running.
    """
    try:
        body = await request.json()
        enabled = body.get("enabled", not telemetry_store["simulation_mode"])
    except Exception:
        enabled = not telemetry_store["simulation_mode"]

    telemetry_store["simulation_mode"] = bool(enabled)
    logger.info(f"Simulation fallback mode changed to: {telemetry_store['simulation_mode']}")
    return {
        "simulation_mode": telemetry_store["simulation_mode"],
        "message": f"Simulation fallback set to {telemetry_store['simulation_mode']}"
    }

@app.post("/api/sql/test")
async def test_sql(request: Request):
    """Real TCP test endpoint for SystemConfigView."""
    import socket
    from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    try:
        data = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        data = {}
    port = data.get("port", 1433)
    server = data.get("serverName", "127.0.0.1")
    host = server.split("\\")[0] if "\\" in server else server
    if host in (".", "localhost"):
        host = "127.0.0.1"
    try:
        with socket.create_connection((host, int(port)), timeout=1.0):
            return {
                "ok": True,
                "message": f"Database {data.get('databaseName', 'Historian')} connection verified",
            }
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=503,
            content={
                "ok": False,
                "message": f"Connection Failed: Host {host}:{port} unreachable ({e})",
            }
        )

"""
Database Initialization & Safe Seeding Engine for MES Production System
Provides backward-compatible and direct access to init_mes_database.

CORE SAFETY GUARANTEES:
1. PREVENT STARTUP DATA OVERWRITE:
   - Check if dbo.Material_Stock already contains rows.
   - DO NOT run any UPDATE, TRUNCATE, or default INSERT on dbo.Material_Stock if the table already has records.
   - Only seed initial demo stock if SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.Material_Stock is exactly 0.
"""
import sys
import os
import logging

backend_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if backend_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, backend_dir)

logger = logging.getLogger("SQL_Init")

try:
    from .init_sql import init_mes_database, find_working_server, CANDIDATE_SERVERS
except (ImportError, ValueError):
    from init_sql import init_mes_database, find_working_server, CANDIDATE_SERVERS

__all__ = ["init_mes_database", "find_working_server", "CANDIDATE_SERVERS"]

if __name__ == "__main__":
    init_mes_database()

import sqlite3
import pyodbc
import time
import os
import threading
from datetime import datetime

# Chuá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i trá»±c tiáº¿p vĂ o SQL Server cá»§a mĂ¡y báº¡n
SQL_SERVER_CONN_STR = (
    "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
    "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
    "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
    "Connection Timeout=2;"  # Timeout 2 giĂ¢y Ä‘á»ƒ chuyá»ƒn máº¡ch sang SQLite ngay khi SQL máº¥t káº¿t ná»‘i
)

# File cÆ¡ sá»Ÿ dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘á»‡m SQLite sáº½ náº±m cĂ¹ng thÆ° má»¥c backend
BUFFER_DB_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "buffer_storage.db")

class StoreAndForwardEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.is_sql_online = False
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self._init_sqlite_buffer()
        # Probe initial connection state
        conn = self.get_sql_connection()
        if conn:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        # Khá»Ÿi cháº¡y luá»“ng ngáº§m tá»± Ä‘á»™ng thÄƒm dĂ² vĂ  Ä‘áº©y bĂ¹ dá»¯ liá»‡u khi SQL phá»¥c há»“i
        self.sync_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._recovery_and_flush_worker, daemon=True)
        self.sync_thread.start()

    def _init_sqlite_buffer(self):
        """Khá»Ÿi táº¡o báº£ng Ä‘á»‡m trong file SQLite náº¿u chÆ°a cĂ³"""
        with sqlite3.connect(BUFFER_DB_PATH) as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS buffer_plc_datalog (
                    buffer_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    OrderCode TEXT,
                    TagName TEXT NOT NULL,
                    Value REAL NOT NULL,
                    Unit TEXT,
                    ReadTime TEXT NOT NULL,
                    Created_At TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()

    def get_sql_connection(self):
        """Kiá»ƒm tra vĂ  láº¥y káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n Microsoft SQL Server"""
        try:
            conn = pyodbc.connect(SQL_SERVER_CONN_STR)
            self.is_sql_online = True
            return conn
        except Exception:
            self.is_sql_online = False
            return None

    def log_datalog(self, order_code: str, tag_name: str, value: float, unit: str = "Â°C", read_time: str = None):
        """
        Ghi dá»¯ liá»‡u váº­n hĂ nh:
        - Náº¿u SQL Server cháº¡y: Ghi tháº³ng vĂ o SQL Server.
        - Náº¿u SQL Server táº¯t/máº¥t káº¿t ná»‘i: Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng ráº½ nhĂ¡nh ghi vĂ o SQLite buffer.
        """
        if not read_time:
            read_time = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

        conn = self.get_sql_connection()
        if conn:
            try:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO dbo.PLC_Datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (order_code, tag_name, value, unit, read_time)
                )
                conn.commit()
                conn.close()
                return "INSERTED_SQL_SERVER"
            except Exception:
                conn.close()
                self.is_sql_online = False

        # Ráº¼ NHĂNH VĂ€O SQLITE KHI SQL SERVER KHĂ”NG PHáº¢N Há»’I
        with self.lock:
            with sqlite3.connect(BUFFER_DB_PATH) as sqlite_conn:
                cur = sqlite_conn.cursor()
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO buffer_plc_datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (order_code, tag_name, value, unit, read_time)
                )
                sqlite_conn.commit()
        return "BUFFERED_SQLITE"

    def get_pending_buffer_count(self) -> int:
        """Äáº¿m sá»‘ báº£n ghi Ä‘ang chá» trong bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m SQLite"""
        try:
            with sqlite3.connect(BUFFER_DB_PATH) as conn:
                cur = conn.cursor()
                cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM buffer_plc_datalog")
                row = cur.fetchone()
                return int(row[0]) if row else 0
        except Exception:
            return 0

    def _recovery_and_flush_worker(self):
        """Tiáº¿n trĂ¬nh ngáº§m: QuĂ©t má»—i 3 giĂ¢y, kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i vĂ  náº¿u SQL báº­t láº¡i thĂ¬ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng bÆ¡m bĂ¹ dá»¯ liá»‡u"""
        while True:
            try:
                time.sleep(3)
                pending_count = self.get_pending_buffer_count()

                sql_conn = self.get_sql_connection()
                if sql_conn:
                    if pending_count > 0:
                        self._flush_buffer_to_sql_server(sql_conn)
                    try:
                        sql_conn.close()
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _flush_buffer_to_sql_server(self, sql_conn):
        """Äá»c tá»« SQLite Ä‘áº©y vĂ o SQL Server theo thá»© tá»± thá»i gian (FIFO) rá»“i xĂ³a buffer"""
        with self.lock:
            with sqlite3.connect(BUFFER_DB_PATH) as sqlite_conn:
                s_cur = sqlite_conn.cursor()
                s_cur.execute("SELECT buffer_id, OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime FROM buffer_plc_datalog ORDER BY buffer_id ASC LIMIT 500")
                rows = s_cur.fetchall()

                if not rows:
                    return

                sql_cursor = sql_conn.cursor()
                buffer_ids_to_delete = []

                try:
                    for row in rows:
                        b_id, o_code, tag, val, unit, r_time = row
                        sql_cursor.execute(
                            "INSERT INTO dbo.PLC_Datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                            (o_code, tag, val, unit, r_time)
                        )
                        buffer_ids_to_delete.append(b_id)

                    sql_conn.commit()

                    # XĂ³a cĂ¡c dĂ²ng Ä‘Ă£ Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ thĂ nh cĂ´ng khá»i SQLite
                    placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in buffer_ids_to_delete)
                    s_cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM buffer_plc_datalog WHERE buffer_id IN ({placeholders})", buffer_ids_to_delete)
                    sqlite_conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    sql_conn.rollback()

db_engine = StoreAndForwardEngine()

"""
backend/test_fault_injector.py
Industrial Multi-Scenario Fault Injection Harness for Predictive Maintenance (PdM)
Simulates realistic multivariate time-series (Current, Speed, Temperature)
for AGITATOR_MIX adhering to real-world physics (Joule heating, mechanical slip, MCSA degradation).
"""

import sys
if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

import pyodbc
import time
import math
import random
import argparse
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

DB_CONN_STR = (
    "DRIVER={ODBC Driver 17 for SQL Server};"
    "SERVER=localhost\\WINCC;"
    "DATABASE=MES_Milk_Production;"
    "Trusted_Connection=yes;"
    "TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
)

EQUIPMENT_CODE = "AGITATOR_MIX"
TAG_CURRENT = "Motor_1.Current"
TAG_SPEED = "Motor_1.Speed"
TAG_TEMP = "Motor_1.Temp"


class IndustrialMotorSimulator:
    """
    Physical simulation of an induction motor:
    1. Thermal dynamics: dT/dt = (k * I^2 - (T - T_amb)) / tau
    2. Mechanical slip under torque/load: Speed drops proportionally to electromagnetic current.
    """
    def __init__(self, ambient_temp: float = 28.5):
        self.ambient_temp = ambient_temp
        self.current_temp = ambient_temp
        self.thermal_tau = 35.0  # Thermal time constant in seconds
        self.thermal_k = 0.42    # Joule conversion coefficient (W to delta-T)

    def update_physics(self, current: float, base_speed: float, dt: float = 0.5):
        # 1. Thermal dynamics: dT/dt = (k * I^2 - (T - T_amb)) / tau
        heat_generated = self.thermal_k * (current ** 2)
        heat_dissipated = (self.current_temp - self.ambient_temp)
        d_temp = (heat_generated - heat_dissipated) / self.thermal_tau * dt
        self.current_temp += d_temp

        # 2. Speed slip under mechanical load
        slip = min(350.0, (current / 4.2) * 8.5)
        speed = max(0.0, base_speed - slip + random.gauss(0, 1.2))

        return round(speed, 1), round(self.current_temp, 2)


def get_db_connection():
    return pyodbc.connect(DB_CONN_STR, timeout=10)


def insert_telemetry_batch(cursor, records):
    """Bulk insert telemetry records into dbo.PLC_Datalog."""
    query = """
        INSERT INTO dbo.PLC_Datalog (OrderCode, TagName, Value, Unit, ReadTime)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """
    cursor.executemany(query, records)


def run_scenario(scenario_id: int, mode_live: bool = True):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    sim = IndustrialMotorSimulator(ambient_temp=29.0)

    order_code = f"BATCH_{datetime.now().strftime('%y%m%d_%H%M')}"
    records_buffer = []

    print("\n" + "=" * 75)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SCENARIO 1: FULL RUN-TO-FAILURE LIFECYCLE (Normal -> Bearing Wear -> Jam)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if scenario_id == 1:
        print("[Ká»CH Báº¢N 1] MĂ” PHá»NG VĂ’NG Äá»œI LÅ¨Y TIáº¾N (FULL RUN-TO-FAILURE STREAM)")
        print("MĂ´ táº£: 120 máº«u bĂ¬nh thÆ°á»ng -> 100 máº«u mĂ²n á»• bi sá»›m -> 60 máº«u káº¹t táº£i sá»± cá»‘.")
        total_steps = 280
        runtime_target = 340.0
        status_target = "RUNNING"

        def get_sample(step):
            # Phase 1: Nominal operation (Step 0 -> 120)
            if step < 120:
                base_i = 4.25 + 0.15 * math.sin(step * 0.1) + random.gauss(0, 0.05)
                base_spd = 800.0
            # Phase 2: Incipient bearing spalling/wear (Step 120 -> 220)
            elif step < 220:
                prog = (step - 120) / 100.0
                spike = 0.85 if random.random() < 0.15 else 0.0
                base_i = (4.3 + prog * 1.5) + spike + random.gauss(0, 0.12)
                base_spd = 790.0 - (prog * 30.0)
            # Phase 3: Severe mechanical breakdown & rotor jam (Step 220 -> 280)
            else:
                base_i = random.uniform(7.8, 9.6) + random.choice([0.0, 1.2, -0.8])
                base_spd = random.uniform(550.0, 680.0)

            spd, temp = sim.update_physics(base_i, base_spd, dt=0.5)
            return round(base_i, 3), spd, temp

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SCENARIO 2: SUDDEN MECHANICAL JAM / ROTOR LOCK
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    elif scenario_id == 2:
        print("[Ká»CH Báº¢N 2] MĂ” PHá»NG Sá»° Cá» Káº¸T Táº¢I Äá»˜T NGá»˜T (SUDDEN ROTOR JAMMING)")
        print("MĂ´ táº£: Äang khuáº¥y á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh, váº­t thá»ƒ láº¡ káº¹t vĂ o cĂ¡nh khuáº¥y khiáº¿n dĂ²ng vá»t > 9A dá»‘c Ä‘á»©ng, tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ tá»¥t.")
        total_steps = 200
        runtime_target = 180.0
        status_target = "RUNNING"

        def get_sample(step):
            if step < 50:
                base_i = 4.30 + random.gauss(0, 0.04)
                base_spd = 800.0
            else:
                # Locked rotor current (> 9A)
                base_i = 9.8 + random.gauss(0, 0.35)
                base_spd = 120.0
            spd, temp = sim.update_physics(base_i, base_spd, dt=0.5)
            return round(base_i, 3), spd, temp

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SCENARIO 3: INCIPIENT BEARING CHIPPING / DEFECT
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    elif scenario_id == 3:
        print("[Ká»CH Báº¢N 3] MĂ” PHá»NG MĂ’N á»” BI Sá»M (INCIPIENT BEARING DEFECT)")
        print("MĂ´ táº£: DĂ²ng trung bĂ¬nh ~5.5A-6.2A, xuáº¥t hiá»‡n vi dao Ä‘á»™ng táº§n sá»‘ cao vĂ  xung gai nhá»n (High Kurtosis/Crest Factor).")
        total_steps = 200
        runtime_target = 310.0
        status_target = "RUNNING"

        def get_sample(step):
            transient_impact = 1.2 if (step % 7 == 0) else 0.0
            base_i = 5.2 + transient_impact + random.gauss(0, 0.22)
            base_spd = 785.0
            spd, temp = sim.update_physics(base_i, base_spd, dt=0.5)
            return round(base_i, 3), spd, temp

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SCENARIO 4: TRANSIENT INGREDIENT IN-RUSH / VISCOSITY SHOCK
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    elif scenario_id == 4:
        print("[Ká»CH Báº¢N 4] MĂ” PHá»NG Sá»C Táº¢I Táº M THá»œI (TRANSIENT IN-RUSH / VISCOSITY SHOCK)")
        print("MĂ´ táº£: Náº¡p lÆ°á»£ng lá»›n sá»¯a bá»™t/Ä‘Æ°á»ng lĂ m dĂ²ng tÄƒng trong 20s rá»“i tá»± phá»¥c há»“i vá» bĂ¬nh thÆ°á»ng.")
        total_steps = 200
        runtime_target = 125.0
        status_target = "RUNNING"

        def get_sample(step):
            if step < 40:
                base_i = 4.25 + random.gauss(0, 0.04)
            elif step < 85:
                base_i = 7.1 + random.gauss(0, 0.15)
            else:
                base_i = 4.65 + random.gauss(0, 0.06)

            spd, temp = sim.update_physics(base_i, 800.0, dt=0.5)
            return round(base_i, 3), spd, temp

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # SCENARIO 5: OPTIMAL BASELINE (Normal Operation)
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    else:
        print("[Ká»CH Báº¢N 5] MĂ” PHá»NG Váº¬N HĂ€NH Äáº T CHUáº¨N HOĂ€N TOĂ€N (OPTIMAL BASELINE)")
        print("MĂ´ táº£: Äá»™ng cÆ¡ váº­n hĂ nh lĂ½ tÆ°á»Ÿng, dĂ²ng Ä‘iá»‡n á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh 4.25A, tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ 800 RPM.")
        total_steps = 200
        runtime_target = 30.0
        status_target = "RUNNING"

        def get_sample(step):
            base_i = 4.25 + random.gauss(0, 0.03)
            spd, temp = sim.update_physics(base_i, 800.0, dt=0.5)
            return round(base_i, 3), spd, temp

    print(f"Tá»•ng sá»‘ chu ká»³ Ä‘o: {total_steps} máº«u | Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™: {'Thá»i gian thá»±c (Live 0.25s)' if mode_live else 'Náº¡p nhanh (Fast Bulk Ingest)'}")
    print("=" * 75)

    cursor.execute("""
    UPDATE dbo.Equipment_Master 
    SET CurrentStatus = ?, 
        Runtime_Hours = ?,
        TotalStarts = TotalStarts + 1
    WHERE EquipmentCode = ?
""", (status_target, runtime_target, EQUIPMENT_CODE))
    conn.commit()

    now = datetime.now()

    for i in range(total_steps):
        current_val, speed_val, temp_val = get_sample(i)
        
        timestamp = now + timedelta(milliseconds=i * 250)

        records_buffer.append((order_code, TAG_CURRENT, current_val, 'A', timestamp))
        records_buffer.append((order_code, TAG_SPEED, speed_val, 'RPM', timestamp))
        records_buffer.append((order_code, TAG_TEMP, temp_val, 'Â°C', timestamp))

        if mode_live:
            insert_telemetry_batch(cursor, [
                (order_code, TAG_CURRENT, current_val, 'A', timestamp),
                (order_code, TAG_SPEED, speed_val, 'RPM', timestamp),
                (order_code, TAG_TEMP, temp_val, 'Â°C', timestamp)
            ])
            conn.commit()
            
            # Professional progress feedback
            sys.stdout.write(
                f"\r[TIáº¾N Äá»˜ {i+1:03d}/{total_steps:03d}] "
                f"DĂ²ng Ä‘iá»‡n: {current_val:5.2f} A | "
                f"Tá»‘c Ä‘á»™: {speed_val:5.1f} RPM | "
                f"Nhiá»‡t Ä‘á»™: {temp_val:4.1f} Â°C"
            )
            sys.stdout.flush()
            time.sleep(0.25)

    if not mode_live:
        print(">> Äang náº¡p nhanh (bulk insert) toĂ n bá»™ táº­p dá»¯ liá»‡u vĂ o dbo.PLC_Datalog...")
        insert_telemetry_batch(cursor, records_buffer)
        conn.commit()
        print(f">> ThĂ nh cĂ´ng náº¡p {len(records_buffer)} báº£n ghi Ä‘o Ä‘a biáº¿n vĂ o dbo.PLC_Datalog!")

    conn.close()
    print("\n[HOĂ€N Táº¤T] Ká»‹ch báº£n mĂ´ phá»ng Ä‘Ă£ hoĂ n táº¥t, dá»¯ liá»‡u sáºµn sĂ ng cho PdM Machine Learning.")


def reset_equipment_benchmark():
    """Reset equipment benchmark in dbo.Equipment_Master to factory default."""
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE dbo.Equipment_Master 
        SET CurrentStatus = 'STOP', Runtime_Hours = 0.0
        WHERE EquipmentCode = ?
    """, (EQUIPMENT_CODE,))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    print("[RESET THĂ€NH CĂ”NG] ÄĂ£ Ä‘Æ°a Ä‘á»™ng cÆ¡ AGITATOR_MIX vá» tráº¡ng thĂ¡i ban Ä‘áº§u: CurrentStatus='STOP', Runtime_Hours=0.0.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Industrial Multi-Scenario Fault Injection Harness")
    parser.add_argument("--scenario", "-s", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], help="Ká»‹ch báº£n mĂ´ phá»ng (1-6)")
    parser.add_argument("--mode", "-m", choices=["live", "bulk"], default=None, help="Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ náº¡p dá»¯ liá»‡u: live hoáº·c bulk")
    parser.add_argument("--reset", action="store_true", help="Reset thiáº¿t bá»‹ vá» máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh")

    args, unknown = parser.parse_known_args()

    if args.reset or (args.scenario == 6):
        reset_equipment_benchmark()
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.scenario is not None:
        scenario_choice = args.scenario
        is_live_mode = (args.mode == "live") if args.mode else False
        run_scenario(scenario_choice, mode_live=is_live_mode)
        sys.exit(0)

    # Interactive CLI loop (never exits automatically, press 0 to exit)
    while True:
        print("\n" + "=" * 65)
        print("  Bá»˜ GIáº¢ Láº¬P Sá»° Cá» Äá»˜NG CÆ  CĂ”NG NGHIá»†P (PdM HARNESS) ")
        print("=" * 65)
        print(" [1] Run-to-Failure : ToĂ n chu trĂ¬nh (Khá»e -> MĂ²n á»• bi -> Há»ng káº¹t táº£i)")
        print(" [2] Rotor Jam      : Äá»™t ngá»™t káº¹t cĂ¡nh khuáº¥y (DĂ²ng dĂ¢ng > 9A dá»‘c Ä‘á»©ng)")
        print(" [3] Bearing Fault  : MĂ²n rá»— á»• bi sá»›m (Xuáº¥t hiá»‡n gai nhá»n vi mĂ´ ~5.5-6.2A)")
        print(" [4] Transient Shock: Sá»‘c táº£i táº¡m thá»i khi náº¡p bá»™t (Tá»± phá»¥c há»“i)")
        print(" [5] Optimal Baseline: Váº­n hĂ nh hoĂ n háº£o danh Ä‘á»‹nh (4.25A, 800 RPM)")
        print(" [6] Reset Benchmark: ÄÆ°a thiáº¿t bá»‹ vá» tráº¡ng thĂ¡i mĂ¡y xuáº¥t xÆ°á»Ÿng (STOP)")
        print(" [0] ThoĂ¡t          : Káº¿t thĂºc chÆ°Æ¡ng trĂ¬nh")
        print("-" * 65)

        try:
            choice = input("Chá»n ká»‹ch báº£n mĂ´ phá»ng [0-6] (Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh: Náº¡p nhanh tá»©c thĂ¬): ").strip()
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            print("\nÄĂ£ nháº­n tĂ­n hiá»‡u dá»«ng. Táº¡m biá»‡t!")
            sys.exit(0)

        if choice == "0":
            print("\nÄĂ£ thoĂ¡t chÆ°Æ¡ng trĂ¬nh mĂ´ phá»ng.")
            sys.exit(0)
        elif choice == "6":
            reset_equipment_benchmark()
            print("\n>> ÄĂƒ Náº P Dá»® LIá»†U XONG! Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ă£ vĂ o SQL Server.")
            try:
                input(">> Nháº¥n phĂ­m ENTER Ä‘á»ƒ quay láº¡i menu chĂ­nh chá»n ká»‹ch báº£n tiáº¿p theo...")
            except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
                sys.exit(0)
        elif choice in ["1", "2", "3", "4", "5"]:
            # Default to fast bulk mode so user only needs to press a single number
            run_scenario(int(choice), mode_live=False)
            print("\n>> ÄĂƒ Náº P Dá»® LIá»†U XONG! Dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ă£ vĂ o SQL Server.")
            try:
                input(">> Nháº¥n phĂ­m ENTER Ä‘á»ƒ quay láº¡i menu chĂ­nh chá»n ká»‹ch báº£n tiáº¿p theo...")
            except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
                sys.exit(0)
        else:
            print(">> Lá»±a chá»n khĂ´ng há»£p lá»‡, vui lĂ²ng chá»n sá»‘ tá»« 0 Ä‘áº¿n 6.")
